# Benchmark Protocol — v1.1 DRAFT

> ## READ THIS BEFORE ANY NUMBER IN THIS DOCUMENT IS QUOTED
>
> **Every criterion, control, failure criterion, reporting rule and numeric constant that is new
> in this document is POST HOC.** The complete, canonical list is **§1.1**. The banner does not
> restate it, because a partial restatement is how a post hoc label gets lost. Nothing new in this
> document was preregistered, and nothing new in it may ever be reported as preregistered.
>
> The post hoc material was written on 2026-09-19, **after** `qc/qc_001.md` reported that the
> CpG-density control C2 reproduces B1's differential-retention ratio to within 0.6% on HCC1395
> and 0.3% on COLO829.
>
> **The preregistered outcome of protocol v1.0 stands and is not overturned by this document.**
> It is reproduced verbatim in §20.0 and every artifact this protocol produces must carry it
> beside any new verdict, in full, unedited, under the fencing rule of §22.5.
>
> **The maximum positive result this document can produce is that the mechanism claim SURVIVED
> the post hoc tests of §1.1.** It cannot establish the mechanism (§20.6 clause 0). A post hoc
> criterion can withdraw a claim or leave it standing; it cannot promote it.
>
> **That maximum is not reachable on this data, and the protocol says so before the run.** §20.6
> records, as a finding of the DESIGN rather than of EXEC-002, that the positive label L-I is
> closed by §21 F11's preservation of EXEC-001's retention ratios (§20.6 clause 0b). EXEC-002's
> remaining purpose is to determine WHICH non-success label applies, and in particular whether the
> mechanism half is withdrawn ON EVIDENCE or withdrawn AS UNDECIDABLE — which are different
> conclusions and are reported as different strings (§20.7).
>
> v1.0 (`protocols/v1.0_locked.md`, sha256
> `9c615da4af068a5330bb512942810c9f2229aa2348554cddcfc130021e200b8c`) and the whole of `EXEC-001`
> are **preserved unmutated**. This is a new file. Nothing in v1.0 or in EXEC-001 is edited,
> deleted or re-run in place.

---

## 0. Revision log

### Design iteration 8 — resolution of the five-lens adversarial panel on the iteration-7 draft

The iteration-7 draft did not pass. Five reviewers returned REVISE, with 24 major findings —
lens 1 `MAJ-L1-01..05`, lens 2 `MAJ-L2-01..04`, lens 3 `SV-01..06`, lens 4 `X-01..05`, lens 5
`PH-01..05` — and 36 minor ones. Every major finding is resolved below, named, with the sections
that changed. No finding is argued with. Two findings prescribed DIFFERENT repairs for the same
defect (MAJ-L1-03 and X-03, on how a threshold transfers between samples); this draft chooses one
transfer, states it normatively, forbids the other two, and says why the rejected one was
rejected — which is what both findings asked for.

```text
MAJ-L1-01  The pattern-blind comparator set omitted the property that GENERATES score_b1, so leg
           (iv) — the only criterion that can exclude the read-quality confound — was satisfiable
           by that confound. The adversary is concrete and correct: mean per-read
           modification-call UNCERTAINTY is invariant to the DIRECTION of every call, so it
           carries no methylation pattern whatever, and it is correlated with d/t by construction.
           Neither C4a (alignment mismatch, a different noise channel) nor C4b (a property of the
           read's genomic FOOTPRINT, not of its calls) can see it.
           THREE ARMS ADDED, all computable in the same BAM pass from data `ref_calls` already
           reads and discards: C4c per-read modification-call uncertainty, with the invariance
           proof written out because that proof is what makes the arm pattern-blind; C4d read
           length; C4e mapping quality. The five are declared as a NAMED SET
           P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}; S6 leg (iv) and S5 leg (d) are the max over ALL of P;
           §21 F12 fires on ANY member. §12.1's "C4a and C4b isolate it" and §10.1's "the two of
           those that this design can measure" are DELETED and replaced by the enumerated set
           actually built plus an explicit statement that the class is OPEN. §16.7 (c) gains the
           consequence §10.5 already had: a comparator whose Spearman correlation with score_b1 is
           below RHO_MIN = 0.10 has not tested the confound, and its leg returns UNDECIDABLE for
           that sample, never HOLDS. §22.1 stores query_alignment_length[], mapq[] and the
           call-confidence summary so untested members of the class can be diagnosed later. The
           LIBRARY-OF-ORIGIN confound the finding names is stated as a standing limitation:
           no v1.1 outcome distinguishes "methylation identifies tumour-derived reads" from "some
           read property identifies the spiked-in library" (§18.1, §24 item 21).
           changed: §10.1, §10.2, §11, §12.1, §16.7, §18.1, §20.3, §20.4, §21 F12, §22.1, §22.2,
                    §24 items 16 and 21, §25 U6

MAJ-L1-02  C3's density strata were approximate DECILES of an integer score that scales with read
           length, so "fixed density" spanned a wide range of t, and within such a stratum a ratio
           against a fixed cut systematically prefers LOW-t reads. A density-only filter could
           therefore satisfy leg (ii).
           C3 now stratifies on the EXACT INTEGER value of score_C2 (§10.1 step 2). Permuting
           score_b1 among reads of identical t is the clean null the arm always claimed to be.
           Adjacent t values are merged ONLY where a stratum would fall below STRATUM_MIN = 30
           scorable reads, and every merge is recorded. A VALIDITY trigger is added beside §10.5's
           power trigger: a realised stratum whose score_C2 MAX/MIN ratio exceeds
           STRATUM_WIDTH_MAX = 1.25, or whose t range straddles 1/cut, makes leg (ii) UNDECIDABLE
           at that target — never HELD and never a refutation. §18.1 states that B1 > C3 excludes
           CpG density only at the REALISED stratum resolution, which is reported numerically.
           changed: §10.1, §10.5, §15.5.11, §18.1, §20.4, §22.1, §22.2, §1.1

MAJ-L1-03  "At the same retention" had no single operational meaning, because the protocol never
    X-03   said how a threshold chosen in one sample transfers to the other samples entering the
           same z. Four inequivalent transfers were available and the choice is not neutral
           between the ">" arms and C2.
           §15.5.4 now defines the transfer NORMATIVELY and IDENTICALLY for every arm: the
           GLOBAL KEPT-READ COUNT TRANSFER. A ladder point is characterised by the number of
           scorable reads the arm keeps in the sample where it was chosen, expressed as the keep
           fraction phi = K/n_scorable; the same phi is realised in every other sample of that z
           computation by that arm's canonical ladder point attaining round(phi * n_scorable) kept
           scorable reads there. Percentile transfer and cut-VALUE transfer are both FORBIDDEN and
           named as forbidden.
           X-03 preferred cut-value transfer. The ambiguity that produced the disagreement is
           removed rather than argued: cut-value transfer is EXACT for the ">" arms and does not
           EXIST for C2, whose threshold is a count; a mixed rule would compare B1 at one kind of
           threshold against C2 at another, which is QC-02's defect ("matched the rule, did not
           match the aggressiveness") reintroduced at the blank end. A count-based transfer is
           exactly attainable for every arm, is in the same coordinates as the rest of §15.5, and
           is the only one of the four that is arm-neutral. That is why it is chosen.
           AXIS-B's cross-sample canonical point, the attainability of a MEAN over three blanks,
           and the tolerance for a mean are all defined (§15.5.2, §15.5.4, §15.5.7 step 3).
           Every row of matched_retention_comparison.tsv records both arms' realised per-sample
           thresholds and realised global keep fractions, and V-e asserts that the two arms'
           blank thresholds were derived by the same rule.
           changed: §10.3, §15.5.2, §15.5.4, §15.5.7, §22.2, §26

MAJ-L1-04  The BLANK_TRIPLE safeguard was confined to AXIS-L x V2, although the draft's own
           measurement (sample_sd/sqrt(G) = 0.97 +- 0.02) shows the same reduction holds under V1
           at matched G. The asymmetry ran in the permissive direction.
           `decided_by` is now computed SYMMETRICALLY under BOTH constructions from the realised
           quantities, by a rule fixed in §1.1 before EXEC-002: BLANK_TRIPLE iff the two arms'
           matched kept-ALT counts agree within the count tolerance AND their sample_sd values
           agree within SAMPLE_SD_TOL = 5% (the natural constant from the 0.97 +- 0.02
           measurement). §20.4's clause now reads "under EITHER construction". §15.5.9 states in
           advance, as an operating characteristic, that AXIS-L may therefore be unable to carry a
           positive leg-(i) result at all, rather than letting the V1 cells carry it by default.
           changed: §15.5.4, §15.5.9, §20.4, §22.2, §1.1

MAJ-L1-05  The bootstrap that decides legs (i) and (iv) was under-specified at the one point that
    X-04   sets its width: whether n_tau is re-derived from each resample's own scorable_ALT or
           held at the observed value, and whether the score arrays, cut and ladder are recomputed
           on the resample.
           §15.5.8 (a) now states normatively: the scorable-read set, the stored score arrays, the
           cut and therefore the LADDER are FIXED across resamples — only gate-passing candidate
           rows are drawn — and n_tau IS RE-DERIVED from each resample's own scorable_ALT, the
           conservative reading, so that matching noise lies inside the interval. Both alternatives
           are named as forbidden. Realised n_tau and the realised inter-arm count difference are
           recorded per resample. An unattainable match inside a resample makes that resample
           UNDEFINED, counted, and subject to the same 20% ceiling as a permutation draw. null_sd
           is defined (SV-02) and is stated NOT to be a null-model sd.
           changed: §15.5.8, §16.2, §22.2, §26

MAJ-L2-01  Raising C1 from B = 200 to B = 2000 silently re-parameterised the PREREGISTERED S1,
           S4 clause (ii) and F8, and deterministically contradicted F11.
           §20.1, §20.2, §21 F8, §21 F11 and §23.2 now state normatively that S1's C1 term, S4
           clause (ii)'s noise band, F8's band and every F11-checked C1 column are computed from
           DRAWS j = 0..199 ONLY — EXEC-001's preserved 200-draw aggregation, reproduced bit for
           bit. The 2000-draw vector is used SOLELY for post hoc S5 leg (c), S6 leg (iii) and
           §15.5.8's permutation nulls. The two aggregations are emitted under distinct column
           names (`_200` and `_2000` suffixes) so no artifact can be read ambiguously. The
           draw-count change is entered in §1.1 as a post hoc modification affecting a
           preregistered criterion's INPUT, and §20.9's line is corrected. The related F11 defect
           — aggregate.py returns C1's alt_kept as a median that need not be an integer — is fixed
           by exempting C1's median-derived columns from the integer requirement and checking them
           at 4 dp instead.
           changed: §10.1, §15.5.8, §20.1, §20.2, §20.9, §21 F8, §21 F11, §22.2, §23.2, §26, §1.1

MAJ-L2-02  The sd-inflation factor was never pinned to f = 1 for the HOLDS decision, and the
           inflation is structurally biased in B1's favour under V2 at matched count, so it could
           LICENSE a pass rather than only disqualify one.
           §15.5.10 and §20.4 now state: every S6 leg outcome that can contribute to a HOLD is
           read at f = 1 AND ONLY AT f = 1. A leg that does not HOLD at f = 1 can never HOLD at
           any other factor. The f in {2, 4, 11.7} rows are DISQUALIFYING-ONLY. F14 gains the
           INFLATION-ONLY clause. §15.5.10's framing is corrected with the per-unit-f algebra the
           finding supplies, and states plainly that under V2 at matched counts the test is WEAK
           in the very cell §15.5.4 identifies as decisive.
           changed: §15.5.10, §20.4, §21 F14, §22.2

MAJ-L2-03  §15.5.9's operating characteristics were computed for leg (i), AXIS-L, one construction
           — and then quoted as the power of the criterion §20.4 actually installs, which is a
           16-fold per-individual conjunction.
           `protocols/support/s6_operating_characteristics.py` IS REWRITTEN AND RE-RUN, and
           §15.5.9's tables are now its output rather than iteration 7's. It applies §15.5.11's
           region filter as it will be applied at evaluation, runs the EMPIRICAL-percentile
           bootstrap rule that §15.5.8 (a) installs, re-derives n_tau inside each resample, adds
           the correlated-blank model and the f = 11.7 rows, and adds RESULT 4 (the family-wise
           refutation rule's size and power) and RESULT 5. The figure "of order 0.06" is
           SUPERSEDED by the measurement and, where it survives as a quotation, carries the scope
           label "leg (i), AXIS-L, pooled, both individuals" and the word superseded.
           WHAT THE MEASUREMENT FOUND, and it is worse than iteration 7 reported: the pooled rule's
           SIZE is 0.093-0.182 (independent blanks) and 0.269-0.343 (correlated blanks), not 0.05;
           the family-wise refutation rule's size is 0.158-0.417, not 0.05; and at f = 11.7 under
           the correlated model the design is inert under V2 and badly calibrated under V1. AXIS-B
           and legs (ii)-(iv) are still not simulable by this script, so RESULT 5 reports an
           UPPER BOUND and §15.5.9 and §24 item 18 state that the power of S6 AS INSTALLED is
           UNKNOWN AND STRICTLY LOWER, rather than letting the leg-(i) number stand in for it.
           Every HOLD and every refutation label must now be reported with its measured size.
           changed: §15.5.9 (whole), protocols/support/s6_operating_characteristics.py, §20.4,
                    §20.5, §20.9, §22.2, §24 item 18, §26

MAJ-L2-04  The only positive branch L-I was unreachable by arithmetic, while §20.7 converted every
           other outcome — including UNDECIDABLE — into an affirmative withdrawal.
           §20.6 gains CLAUSE 0b, stating as a finding of the DESIGN (not of EXEC-002) that L-I is
           closed by F11's preservation of EXEC-001's retention_ratio values, that the mechanism
           half of H1 is therefore withdrawn on the strength of EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md, and that
           EXEC-002's purpose is limited to determining WHICH non-success label applies. §20.7's
           withdrawal rule is SPLIT: WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE for labels backed by a family-wise
           eligible refutation or a determined S5 failure, and WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE for L-H,
           the latter propagating as "not established by this benchmark, and not shown false"
           rather than as a negative finding. Handoff question 6 is re-answered with that scoping.
           This is NOT a loosening of S5 or §16.2 — that is the forbidden direction and it is not
           taken. It is a correction to what the design may claim.
           changed: §20.3, §20.6, §20.7, §20.10, §24 item 18, and the handoff block

SV-01      The family-wise refutation bar used a BOOTSTRAP resampling distribution as if it were a
           NULL distribution. It is centred on the observed effect, so the rule was unfirable at
           the target attaining the minimum and had no power anywhere. Every refutation label in
           the protocol was structurally unreachable.
           §15.5.8 (d) now builds the family-wise reference from the CENTRED, STUDENTISED
           quantity (D* - D_obs)/null_sd*, which is a distribution under the hypothesis of no
           difference. REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE fires when the observed min standardised difference
           falls below the 5th percentile of the centred min. §15.5.9 gains RESULT 4 reporting the
           rule's SIZE under exact equivalence and its POWER against the 1.5x and 2x alternatives
           on the same 20,000-simulation model.
           changed: §15.5.8, §15.5.9, §20.4, §22.2

SV-02      `null_sd` — the denominator of the statistic that decides S6 — was never defined.
           §15.5.8 (a) now defines it normatively as reading (b): the studentising sd computed
           INSIDE each resample (null_sd*), with the observed-data bootstrap sd of the paired
           difference (null_sd_obs) used for the reported point statistic and for display. It is
           added to §1.1's frozen register and to §20.8 clause 3. §15.5.9's simulated column is
           renamed `sim_sd_D` so it cannot be mistaken for the executor's estimator, and §22.2
           says which estimator fills which column. The protocol states in terms that null_sd is a
           BOOTSTRAP sd of a paired difference, not a null-model sd.
           changed: §15.5.8, §15.5.9, §20.8, §22.2, §1.1

SV-03      The bootstrap had no undefined-resample rule, no admissibility ceiling and no reporting
           column, although the counts guarantee a large undefined fraction: COLO829's three-blank
           C2 denominator rests on ONE kept blank read, absent from ~36% of resamples.
           §15.5.8 (c)'s machinery is extended to the bootstrap: what makes a resample undefined is
           enumerated; `n_boot_undefined`, `n_boot_floored`, `boot_admissible` and
           `boot_mcse_delta_lo/hi` are §22.2 columns; the same 20% ceiling applies, above which
           the interval is INADMISSIBLE and the leg or S5 ratio comparison returns UNDECIDABLE
           rather than being silently conditioned; and the DISCRIMINATING evaluable target set
           entering §20.4's pooled mean is FIXED on the observed data and held fixed across
           resamples. §20.3 states in advance that COLO829's three-blank C2 interval is expected
           to be INADMISSIBLE on these counts.
           changed: §15.5.8, §16.2, §20.3, §22.2, §26

SV-04      The decision statistic is a difference of two min-over-replicates functionals; the
           percentile bootstrap is unreliable for a min under near-ties; near-ties are present
           (COLO829 C2 V1 per-replicate lo 0.1684 / 0.1676 / 2.0485); and §15.5.9 simulated a
           SYMMETRIC 1.645*sd proxy rather than the empirical percentile rule that decides.
           §15.5.9 now requires the simulation to implement the ACTUAL rule — the 2000-resample
           EMPIRICAL percentile — and the tables in the protocol are the simulated-actual ones.
           `argmin_unstable_frac_B1` and `argmin_unstable_frac_comp` become required columns and an
           ADMISSIBILITY condition: a target where either arm's replicate argmin moves in more
           than ARGMIN_UNSTABLE_MAX = 0.20 of resamples returns UNDECIDABLE, because the bootstrap
           is not estimating a fixed functional there. The unproved sentence "This is strictly
           harder than the point comparison it replaces, in BOTH directions" is DELETED and
           replaced by what can be shown. The three per-replicate paired differences §15.5.7 step
           4 already stores are promoted from diagnostic to required companion output on every row
           carrying a leg verdict.
           changed: §15.5.8, §15.5.9, §22.2, §26, §1.1

SV-05      The pseudo-replication is carried into the statistic but not into the resampling
           distribution, and the size claim that justifies the run is computed under the
           independence model §15.5.10 itself refutes.
           §15.5.9 now REQUIRES both tables: the f = 1, three-independent-blanks model AND a
           CORRELATED-BLANK model with the effective blank count set to 1, plus the realised size
           at f = 11.7. The design's stated size is the correlated-blank figure. Every S5 and S6
           interval is scoped explicitly as CONDITIONAL ON THIS BLANK LIBRARY, and the sentence
           "the size is controlled at 0.05 in both directions" is REMOVED from §15.5.9 point 6 and
           §24 item 18 and replaced by the realised figures with their model named.
           changed: §15.5.9, §15.5.10, §16.2, §24 items 17 and 18

SV-06      §16.5's quotation carve-out made a provenance claim that is false: the three-blank
           ratios 1.5687 / 1.9955 / 4.1644 / 3.7338 appear in neither qc_001.md nor
           ablation_results.tsv — they are new v1.1 designer computations, two of them resting on
           two and ONE kept blank read.
           The carve-out now states truthfully which numbers are EXEC-001 quotations and which are
           new v1.1 computations, and §16.5's own rule is APPLIED to the new ones: the three-blank
           C2 quantities are displayed as RAW COUNTS in §16.2, §20.3 and §20.10, never as 1.9955
           or 3.7338. §16.2's justification for requiring both blank conventions is re-derived
           without reference to an "order of magnitude" disagreement measured on one read.
           changed: §16.2, §16.5, §20.3, §20.10

X-01       The inputs EXEC-002 re-runs on DO NOT EXIST. All 28 BAMs were converted to CRAM on
           2026-09-12 (EV-0052, verified 2026-09-19). The draft contained no occurrence of CRAM,
           reference, EV-0052 or inputs_manifest, and §2-9 asserted normatively that "The BAMs,
           paths, levels, replicates and roles are identical", which is false.
           A new §6.1 INPUTS names the 28 CRAM paths and their .crai, the decode reference
           /big8_disk/ref/GRCh38_no_alt_analysis_set.fasta with md5
           a6da8681616c05eb542f1d91606a7b2f, and the pysam open as mode 'rc' with
           reference_filename, recorded as a deviation with `Methodology changed: NO` per
           EV-0052's recommended_next_action. The false sentence is DELETED. EV-0052 and
           execution/inputs_manifest.tsv are added to §1 created_from. An input-availability
           precheck is the FIRST line of §26. §23.1 is re-costed against CRAM decode: the executor
           MEASURES one pass before committing the budget, and the inherited per-pass figure is
           labelled an unverified lower bound taken from a different input format. §21 F11 gains
           an INPUT-FORMAT branch so a reproduction failure traceable to the conversion is
           investigated and reported rather than stranding the benchmark.
           changed: §1, §2-9, new §6.1, §14, §21 F11, §22.1, §23.1, §23.2, §26, §24 item 22

X-02       The per-worker leakage guarantee was unsatisfiable under the actual inputs, and C4a's
           fallback path required a file §14 forbids opening.
           §14's per-worker expectation is REWRITTEN to enumerate what the run actually opens — 28
           CRAMs, 28 .crai, the two candidate frames, and the decode reference with its md5 — and
           states why the reference is non-leaking in QC-07's own wording. The prohibition stays
           SHARP on the four named classes (matched normal, orthogonal truth VCF, panel of
           normals, population database) rather than on "any new path". A TAG PRECHECK is added
           before the 28 passes: sample reads from every input, record NM and MD presence and
           counts, fire NM_SPARSE off the MEASURED number, and — if BOTH tags are absent — do not
           compute C4a and return UNDECIDABLE for every C4a leg. Reaching for the reference to
           compute NM is forbidden.
           changed: §10.2, §11, §14, §21 F12, §22.1, §26

X-05       §23's import rule and §15.5.10's mandatory sd inflation could not both be obeyed:
           mrdz.score.z_interval derives sd internally and exposes no inflation parameter.
           §15.5.10 now writes the arithmetic out explicitly over mrdz's own outputs —
           lo_f = z - f*(z - lo), hi_f = z + f*(hi - z), with z, lo, hi taken from
           mrdz.score.z_interval at f = 1 — and §23 carves this out as ARITHMETIC ON mrdz OUTPUTS
           rather than a reimplementation of them. Rescaling blank_totals to obtain an inflated
           sd_b is FORBIDDEN, and the reason is stated: it moves blank_mean and therefore moves z.
           changed: §15.5.10, §23

PH-01      §20.0's reprint of v1.0's named outcomes announced itself as "in full" and was
           abridged; every dropped clause was the one stating what the non-success MEANS, and the
           injunction "which may not be relabelled" was dropped entirely.
           §20.0 now carries protocols/v1.0_locked.md lines 713-728 BYTE FOR BYTE, including the
           introducing line and all four trailing clauses. The block travels inside explicit
           BEGIN/END fences and may not be paraphrased, truncated or re-wrapped in any artifact.
           §26 gains a byte-comparison check of BOTH reprinted v1.0 blocks against their sources
           before EXEC-002 reports anything.
           changed: §20.0, §22.5, §26

PH-02      §1.1 was declared "canonical and complete" and omitted the entire verdict apparatus —
           §20.7's labels including L-I, §20.8's clauses, §20.9, §12.1, §14's guards, §18.1,
           §22.3, §22.4, §24 items 11-20 and §25 U4-U6 — so a reader partitioning the document by
           §1 got an unregistered residue containing every rule that decides and names a verdict.
           §1.1 gains a seventh block, VERDICT AND CLAIM-CONTROL RULES, enumerating them, and a
           CATCH-ALL sentence: anything not carried over verbatim from v1.0 is POST HOC whether or
           not it is enumerated, because enumeration is for navigation, not for scope. §20.8 gains
           a heading clause stating that every freeze in it is relative to EXEC-002 only and that
           none of it may be described as preregistered. §1's `preregistered_scope` becomes an
           explicit section list so the complement of §1.1 is computable.
           changed: §1, §1.1, §20.8

PH-03      A post hoc UNDECIDABLE was headlined under v1.0's preregistered label string, and
           §20.10 pre-committed to exactly that outcome.
           L-B is SPLIT: L-B1 fires only on a DETERMINED §16.2 interval FAILURE and returns
           MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED; L-B2 fires when no S5 leg FAILS but at least one is UNDECIDABLE
           and routes to L-H. UNDECIDABLE is removed from L-B's trigger. Every label string in
           §20.7 now carries its REGISTER suffix — "(v1.0 §21 F7 — PREREGISTERED)" or
           "(POST HOC §1.1, via S5)" — and the suffix is required in V1_1_OUTCOME,
           branch_labels_applicable and every propagated copy. §20.10's EXPECTED HEADLINE is
           corrected to MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE, which is what its own preceding sentences
           establish. RULE 3 gains the clause that no label whose trigger includes an UNDECIDABLE
           comparison may outrank MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE.
           changed: §20.7, §20.10, §22.2, §22.5

PH-04      Mandatory propagation was defined for the withdrawal branch only, leaving the POSITIVE
           branch — where the post hoc qualifier is load-bearing and most likely to be dropped in
           transit — with no rule at all.
           §20.7's propagation block is rewritten to bind EVERY headline. For L-I the exact text
           to be written into each of the four targets is specified: the §20.7 label verbatim with
           its register suffix, the literal POST_HOC_2026-09-19 marker, a pointer to the §20.0 v1.0
           block, and §16.4's sentence naming the single individual x construction cell in which
           the detection outcome changed. The binding sentence is added: no v1.1 result may be
           entered into research_state.yaml known_evidence, research-os.json or any FIND record
           without the POST_HOC_2026-09-19 marker and the §16.4 restriction in the same entry.
           §26's single withdrawal-only item becomes one item per branch.
           changed: §20.7, §26

PH-05      The permanent post hoc label was defined per ROW while the register it carries is per
           COLUMN and per FILE, so every mixed artifact was provably mislabelled on at least one
           column; and statistical_tests.tsv had no defined home.
           `posthoc` becomes a TWO-VALUED per-row register: POST_HOC_2026-09-19 or
           PREREGISTERED_V1_0, never blank. Any artifact carrying both registers on ONE row must
           carry a COLUMN-LEVEL MANIFEST — a header comment line listing the post hoc column names
           verbatim — and this is named explicitly for ablation_results.tsv and
           per_sample_metrics.tsv. results_v1_1/statistical_tests.tsv is named in §22.2's tree,
           EXEC-001's row is copied in unmodified carrying PREREGISTERED_V1_0, and
           results/statistical_tests.tsv is added to §22.4's read-only list. §26 gains the
           matching items.
           changed: §17, §22.2, §22.3, §22.4, §26

MINOR FINDINGS ADOPTED (36 returned; each is resolved at the section named)
  §16.2 leg (a)'s interval rule named explicitly (lo(B1) > 1.0; C1's band containing 1) and
  §20.6's S4 conjunct pinned to v1.0's POINT rule so the preregistered criterion is not
  re-parameterised — §16.2, §20.3, §20.6, §20.10;  §20.7 RULE 4 scoped to the FAILURE direction

  UNRELIABLE promoted from reporting obligation to an admissibility condition — §16.3, §20.4;
  the full 28-cell operating-characteristics table made part of the frozen basis — §15.5.9,
  §20.8;  §10.6's seed-averaging decomposed into reported seed-only and bootstrap-only components

  rules scoped to human-readable artifacts with TSV reference columns instead — §20.0, §16.4,
  §22.2;  "two post hoc tests" corrected to "the post hoc tests of §1.1" — banner, §20.6;
  §10.2's AGENTS.md rule 8 violation repaired by moving the inference to a labelled interpretation

  pre-emptive weighting of a named non-success outcome moved to §24 — §20.5, §24 item 18;
  §1's preregistered_scope made machine-readable — §1.

No v1.0 review finding (B001-B014, N001-N013), no QC finding and no panel finding from any
iteration has been deleted.
```

### Design iteration 7 — resolution of the five-lens adversarial panel on the iteration-6 draft

The iteration-6 draft did not pass. Five reviewers returned REVISE. Every major finding is
resolved below, named, with the section that changed. Where a finding rested on a reading the
draft permitted but did not intend, the ambiguity is removed and that is said plainly; no finding
is argued with.

```text
MAJ-01  C3 excludes CpG density only; the draft asserted it excludes everything.
        The exclusivity sentences in §10.1 and §18.1 are DELETED and replaced by the enumerated
        exclusion set C3 actually earns. The live alternative the panel named — score_b1 = d/t is
        a per-read DISCORDANCE rate, maximised by a noisy read — is measured, not argued away:
        EXEC-001's own HCC1395 TF0 rep1 shows B1 keeping 10/25 scorable ALT reads (0.400) while
        keeping K_B1/n_scorable = 2895/58990 = 4.908% of all scorable reads, an 8.15x enrichment
        of ALT-carrying reads IN A BLANK WITH NO TUMOUR (execution/raw/HCC1395_TF0_rep1.json,
        results/per_sample_metrics.tsv). Two new methylation-PATTERN-BLIND comparator arms are
        added, C4a (per-read alignment mismatch rate) and C4b (per-read ambiguous-call fraction),
        built in the same BAM pass, on the same scorable set, swept over the same count ladder
        (§10.2). S6 gains leg (iv) against both, with an interval. §16.7 makes the (d, t) joint
        distribution of kept ALT reads and the TF0 ALT-enrichment ratio standing outputs. §21 F12
        names MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED — READ-QUALITY CONFOUND.
        changed: §10.1, §10.2, §10.4, §11, §15.5, §16.7, §18.1, §20.4, §21 F12, §22.1, §22.2, §24

MAJ-02  C2 had no uncertainty band although it is the comparator that killed v1.0, and its
        realised numbers are one-read objects decided by a seeded tie-break.
        C2 now carries a 200-seed tie-break band (§10.6). Seed index j = 0 is EXEC-001's seed
        exactly, so the reproduction check of §15.5.7 V-a is untouched; j = 1..199 are new.
        S5 leg (b) and S6 leg (i) are evaluated against C2's band, not its point value. Every
        sample reports n_tied_at_k, the size of the seed-dependent block at C2's top-k boundary.
        The panel's sensitivity calculation is reproduced in §20.10 and is correct: one extra kept
        blank ALT read on HCC1395 TF0 rep1 moves C2's five-blank ratio from 1.6839 to 1.3891 and
        reverses leg (b).
        changed: §10.6, §15.5.5, §15.5.8, §16.2, §20.3, §20.4, §21 F9, §22.1, §22.2, §23.2

MAJ-03  S5 was adjudicated at a precision §16.5 forbade, the blank set was unnamed, and the
    M3  LOW_COUNT fallback named no quantity.
   MAJ-2 The significant-figure floor is REMOVED as an evidential rule and retained as a DISPLAY
        convention only (§16.5). S5's legs are decided by INTERVAL DOMINANCE at full stored
        precision (§16.2, §20.3): B1's lower limit above the comparator's upper limit HOLDS,
        the reverse FAILS, overlap returns UNDECIDABLE. A tie is inside the overlap and therefore
        UNDECIDABLE, which is a non-success — this satisfies both the finding that said a tie may
        not be HELD and the finding that said a tie may not silently pass. The LOW_COUNT clause is
        DELETED; the interval rule is defined at every count. The blank set is stated normatively
        and BOTH conventions are required to hold (§16.2): the five-replicate convention that
        aggregate.py actually used (HCC1395 B1 retention_blank 0.363463 = mean of 10/25, 8/22,
        8/20, 8/21, 6/22) and the three-replicate convention that matches the blanks entering z.
        §16.5's worked example is corrected: HCC1395's ratio rests on 40 kept blank reads and
        COLO829's on 15, not on 26 and 6.
        changed: §16.2, §16.5, §20.3, §20.10, §21 F9, §22.2, §22.3

MAJ-04  The sweep matched on the MEAN retention over replicates while the statistic is the MIN
    M5  over replicates, so the arms were mismatched at the replicate that decides the outcome.
   MAJ-6 Resolved by option (a), the narrowing one: §15.5.4 now permits a sample-specific
        threshold FOR THE SWEEP ONLY, and AXIS-L matches each 0.1% replicate to the target
        INDIVIDUALLY and EXACTLY on the integer kept-ALT count. Each replicate's z is a complete,
        internally consistent single-threshold evaluation of that individual; the v1.0 rule that
        one threshold rule applies to every sample holds WITHIN each z computation. The min over
        replicates is then taken exactly as v1.0 does. §10.3 is amended accordingly and the
        amendment is scoped to §15.5.
        matched_retention_comparison.tsv gains lo_B1_rep1..3, lo_C2_rep1..3, the three
        per-replicate paired differences and argmin_replicate per arm, as v1.0 §17 already
        required and the iteration-6 schema dropped.
        changed: §10.3, §15.5.4, §15.5.5, §15.5.7, §22.2

MAJ-05  "At the same retention" admitted a 0.10 realised gap between the two arms, and Z was
    M6  linearly interpolated across a step function of integer counts.
    X2  Both are removed. The sweep no longer sweeps a percentile grid with interpolation; it
   MAJ-8 enumerates the ATTAINABLE operating points and matches the arms on the INTEGER kept-ALT
   (c,d) COUNT (§15.5.2, §15.5.7). Interpolation of Z is FORBIDDEN outright. Where an arm cannot
        attain the target count the nearest attainable count is used only if it differs by at most
        one read; otherwise the target returns UNDECIDABLE for that leg, never a pass. The panel's
        evidence that the percentile grid cannot resolve the curve is decisive and is quoted in
        §15.5.2: COLO829 TF0 rep1 has cut 0.100000 = 1/10 with K_B1 = 3020 while rep2 has cut
        0.096774 = 3/31 with K_B1 = 3821 at essentially identical n_scorable — a cut difference of
        0.0032 moving 800 reads, because score_b1 = d/t is a discrete rational multiset with heavy
        atoms. A bisection on the percentile cannot land inside an atom. The ladder can.
        changed: §10.3, §15.5.1, §15.5.2, §15.5.7, §21 F10, §22.1, §22.2

MAJ-06  "95th percentile of Z(C3, tau) over 200 draws" had two admissible readings.
        The conservative one is mandated: PER-DRAW matching. Each draw is matched to the target on
        its OWN count ladder, the count floor is applied per draw, the band is the 5th/95th
        percentile of the per-draw Z, and the fraction of excluded draws is reported and bounds
        the band's admissibility. §15.5.8's NULL-1 and NULL-2 carry the same rule.
        changed: §15.5.5, §15.5.8

MAJ-07  `region` gated a verdict and was never defined.
    M4  §15.5.11 defines it mechanically, numerically, before execution, from quantities that do
    X6  not involve the B1-versus-comparator contrast, and §20.8 clause 3 freezes it.
        DEGENERATE_HIGH targets are excluded from S6 in BOTH directions: they may neither carry a
        pass nor trigger a refutation. A failing leg at any DISCRIMINATING target fails that leg
        regardless of anything else.
        changed: §15.5.11, §20.4, §20.7, §20.8, §22.2, §24

M1      S5 leg (a) was v1.0's S4 clause (i) only, and §20.6 dropped S4 from the conjunction, so
(lens 2) there was a data configuration failing v1.0 and passing v1.1 — the bar moved sideways.
        Fixed both ways. S5 leg (a) is now v1.0's S4 QUOTED VERBATIM, both clauses. S4 itself is
        restored to §20.6's conjunction as a named necessary condition, exactly as v1.0 §20 has
        it. The word "demoted" is restricted to what S4 LICENSES, never to whether it binds.
        §20.9's strictness line is corrected. §21 states that F1-F8 remain binding and that where
        a preregistered failure criterion and a post hoc verdict rule disagree, the PREREGISTERED
        one controls.
        changed: §20.2, §20.3, §20.6, §20.9, §21

M2      S5's outcome is fixed in advance by preserved EXEC-001 values plus F11, so the iteration-6
(lens 2) verdict ladder short-circuited and S6 — the criterion the draft called deciding — had no
    M3   reachable named outcome.
(lens 5) §20.3 now records that S5 leg (b)'s POINT comparison is DETERMINED IN ADVANCE and states
        the determined values. §20.7 is rebuilt as a full outcome MAP: S4, S5, each S6 leg and S7
        carry their own labels, every combination maps to a fixed label, all applicable labels are
        computed and reported, and a severity precedence rule selects the headline
        (REFUTED > UNSUPPORTED > NOT REPLICATED > UNDECIDABLE > SURVIVED). REFUTED is reachable
        independently of S5. criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv gains branch_labels_applicable.
        v1.0 §20's named-outcome block is reprinted inside §20.
        changed: §20.3, §20.4, §20.5, §20.7, §22.2

MAJ-1   Leg (i) had no null and no interval although its null sd is about 1 to 3 z-units.
        §15.5.8 now specifies a PAIRED CANDIDATE BOOTSTRAP (B = 2000): one resample recomputes
        both arms at the same matched count, and the decision is on the interval of the paired
        difference. Leg (i) HOLDS only if the 5th percentile of the paired difference exceeds 0;
        it FAILS only if the 95th percentile falls below 0; otherwise UNDECIDABLE. This is
        strictly harder than the point comparison it replaces in the support direction and
        strictly harder in the refutation direction as well. delta_lo, delta_hi and null_sd are
        columns of matched_retention_comparison.tsv.
        changed: §15.5.8, §17, §20.4, §22.2

MAJ-3   No power analysis existed, and the iteration-6 S6 was an intersection of roughly 300
        zero-tolerance inequalities.
        §15.5.9 now carries the operating characteristics, computed before EXEC-002 from
        EXEC-001's own counts, and printed in the protocol. They are bad enough to change the
        design: the all-target intersection holds with probability 0.03-0.07 against a real 1.5x
        advantage and produces a spurious refutation somewhere with probability 0.17 under exact
        equivalence. That is not a criterion. S6's verdict-bearing rule is therefore the POOLED
        standardised paired difference within each cell (size 0.05, power 0.25-0.30 at 1.5x and
        0.53-0.69 at 2x), the refutation direction is family-wise controlled, and the all-target
        intersection is RETAINED and REPORTED as S6-STRICT but no longer decides alone.
        This recalibration is between two POST HOC drafts, not against v1.0: S6 in every form is
        an additional necessary condition, so v1.1 remains strictly harder than v1.0 (§20.9).
        It is declared rather than quietly applied, and the operating characteristics that
        motivated it are in the document.
        changed: §15.5.9, §20.4, §20.7, §21 F12, §24

MAJ-4   On AXIS-L under V2 the statistic reduces algebraically to the arm's three blank counts.
        §15.5.4 now states the reduction instead of leaving it to be found, the count floor is
        raised (§15.5.6) so that sqrt(mean_blank) is never estimated from fewer than five kept
        blank reads across three non-empty replicates, and every row records whether leg (i) at
        that target is decided by the matched count or by the blank triple.
        changed: §15.5.4, §15.5.6, §22.2

MAJ-5   v1.0 §24 item 3's "equally, for every arm" does not transfer to a cross-arm comparison at
        matched retention, because both sd_b and sample_sd are arm-specific there.
        §15.5.10 adds a MANDATORY blank-spread sensitivity analysis at inflation factors
        {1, 2, 4, 11.7}, the last being EV-0036's measured between-library understatement (89.08
        against the five pseudo-blanks' 7.64). Every S6 leg is recomputed at each factor and the
        ORDERING, not merely the magnitude, must survive. §24 item 17 records that the carried-over
        item 3 no longer covers this comparison. §21 F14 names the consequence.
        changed: §15.5.10, §21 F14, §22.2, §24

MAJ-7   The permutation machinery was a one-sided test called something else, with undefined ties,
        silently conditioned bands and B = 200.
        §15.5.8 now names it: a one-sided permutation test at alpha = 0.05 with B = 2000, exact
        only under the stated within-stratum exchangeability null. Ties route to UNDECIDABLE.
        n_draws_undefined is reported per target and a band with more than 20% undefined draws is
        INADMISSIBLE. The Monte Carlo error of the percentile is reported. statistical_tests.tsv
        stops recording `method NONE` for these rows; v1.0's rows keep NONE unchanged.
        changed: §15.5.8, §17, §22.2

MAJ-8   Incompatible evidential standards across the same counts; zero-spread blank triples; an
        undefined branch; and §15.5.5 contradicting §15.5.6(c) on undefined Z.
        One floor now governs both reporting and evaluation (§15.5.6). A zero-spread blank triple
        is UNDECIDABLE at that target, flagged, never a value. Interpolation is gone. The
        inadmissible-nearest-point branch is gone with it. The contradiction is resolved in one
        direction, in the protocol text: an undefined Z caused by counts is
        EXCLUDED_UNDERPOWERED — never a pass and never a refutation, because an undefined
        statistic cannot refute (§15.5.5, §15.5.6).
        changed: §15.5.5, §15.5.6, §15.5.11, §22.2

X1      The percentile grid was not guaranteed to reach the targets and no behaviour was defined.
        The count ladder makes the attainable range explicit per arm per sample. A target outside
        it is TARGET_UNREACHABLE, with the attainable [n_min, n_max] recorded; never a pass, and
        it counts against the three-evaluable-target floor as an exclusion.
        changed: §15.5.2, §15.5.6, §22.2

X3      §23.2 misstated C1's seeding and left C3's unspecified.
        The as-executed derivations are now quoted verbatim from execution/random_seeds.yaml and
        aggregate.py, including the single-generator-per-(individual, draw) rule advanced over
        samples in a fixed order. C3 and the C2 seed band are given the same explicit form. One
        permutation per (draw, sample) is drawn and REUSED at every ladder point.
        execution/random_seeds.yaml is added to §1 created_from.
        changed: §1, §23.2, §26

X4      score_b1 was to be stored at 6 decimal places, which is lossy for a rational d/t whose own
        order statistics define the cut, under a strict `>` rule.
        The schema now stores the INTEGER numerator and denominator and defines score_b1 = d/t
        exactly, reproducing run_sample.py's arithmetic bit for bit. Thresholds are ladder
        INDICES, not floating-point percentiles, so the PCT = 95 slice is exact by construction.
        changed: §22.1, §15.5.2

X5      V-a was vacuous where it mattered and collided with §16.5.
        V-a is now a RECONSTRUCTION IDENTITY at the per-candidate level, and the aggregate-level
        equality is retained as a second, weaker check. F11 names the exact columns and precision
        and explicitly exempts the display-floored columns, so the two rules cannot contradict.
        changed: §15.5.7, §21 F11, §21 F13

X7      V-c was false by construction and unattainable.
        V-c is restated against B0_scorable, a named anchor with retention_scorable_only = 1.0 by
        construction, and convergence is checked at each arm's ATTAINABLE maximum rather than at
        an unreachable R = 1.
        changed: §15.5.7, §15.5.11

M1      The draft invented a positive outcome above its own ceiling.
(lens 5) "SUPPORTED IN FULL" and "MECHANISM ESTABLISHED" are DELETED from every branch. The
        maximum positive v1.1 outcome is "MECHANISM NOT WITHDRAWN — POST HOC, NOT ESTABLISHED",
        and §20.6 clause 0 binds it. A follow-up PREREGISTRATION is a required output of that
        branch.
        changed: §20.6, §20.7, §24

M4      Three inconsistent registers of what is post hoc, and unlabelled post hoc constants.
(lens 5) §1.1 is the single canonical register. The banner, §20 and §24 cite it rather than
        restate it. "preregistered-in-this-document" is deleted from §15.5.8.
        changed: banner, §1, §1.1, §15.5.8, §20, §22.2, §24

M6      registered_outcome_v1_1.txt opened with `REGISTERED_OUTCOME: H1 SUPPORTED`.
(lens 5) §22.5 specifies the file layout normatively: the v1.1 verdict first under distinct keys,
        the v1.0 block second inside explicit BEGIN/END fences, and the withdrawal sentence above
        the fence.
        changed: §22.5, §20.0, §26

FINDING-ID MAP, because the five panels reused identifiers. Lens 1 (mechanism discrimination)
issued MAJ-01..MAJ-07; lens 3 (statistical validity) issued MAJ-1..MAJ-8; lens 2 (strictness) and
lens 5 (pre-registration hygiene) each issued M1..M6; lens 4 (executability) issued X1..X7. Every
one of the 32 is resolved above. The two that are resolved inside another finding's block without
being named there are recorded here:

    M2 (lens 5)  the §20.3 escape clause colliding with §16.5 and §20.10, and the demand that
                 §16.5's bands be justified or labelled post hoc.
                 -> resolved by the MAJ-03 / M3 / MAJ-2 block: §16.5 becomes a DISPLAY convention
                    with no evidential role and is stated to be a convention rather than a

                    full stored precision by the §16.2 interval rule; §16.5 is listed in §1.1 as
                    POST HOC; §20.10 is labelled a full-precision audit note under §16.5's
                    explicit carve-out.
    M5 (lens 5)  the exclusivity assertion in §10.1 contradicted by §18.1, and the AGENTS.md
                 rule 8 violation of asserting an interpretation inside normative protocol text.
                 -> resolved by the MAJ-01 block: the sentence is deleted from §10.1 and replaced

                    positive branch is bound to the corrected exclusion set and no longer names a

Minor findings adopted: per-leg count floor (§15.5.6); blank floor on non-empty replicates rather
```

### Design iteration 6 — resolution of qc/qc_001.md

```text
Resolution for QC-01 (S4 is passed by the confound it was written to exclude):
- changed section: §10 (new control C3), §15.4, §16, §20 (S4 preserved; new S5), §21 (new F9), §22
- change made: v1.0's S4 is PRESERVED VERBATIM and its EXEC-001 outcome (HELD) is preserved.
  S4 continues to bind (§20.6); what changes is what it LICENSES. The mechanism claim moves to
  post hoc criteria S5 and S6. S5 requires B1's differential-retention ratio to EXCEED C2's on
  both individuals, not merely to exceed its own blank, and contains v1.0's S4 as a conjunct.
  On the EXEC-001 numbers S5 leg (b) fails on HCC1395 (B1 1.6741 vs C2 1.6839) — §20.10.
  A third control C3 is added: a CpG-DENSITY-STRATIFIED permutation of score_B1, which destroys
  the read-to-methylation-pattern correspondence while PRESERVING the read-to-density
  correspondence. C1 destroys both; C2 has only density.
- status: RESOLVED BY REDESIGN; the criterion is not yet evaluated (EXEC-002 required)

Resolution for QC-02 (S1's control legs are decided at incomparable count scales):
- changed section: §10.3, §15.5, §16, §17, §20 (new S6), §21 (new F10, F11), §22, §23, §26
- change made: S1, S2 and S3 are PRESERVED VERBATIM and UNCHANGED. A new POST HOC criterion S6
  is ADDED on top of them: the RETENTION SWEEP. Each arm's zlo_min is traced as a function of its
  REALISED ALT retention, and B1 must dominate at MATCHED REALISED RETENTION, on both retention
  axes, on both individuals, under both variance constructions, and outside the permutation
  bands. PCT = 95 remains the operating point of S1-S5; the sweep is an ANALYSIS and §20.8
  forbids re-selecting the operating point from it.
- status: RESOLVED BY REDESIGN; the criterion is not yet evaluated (EXEC-002 required)

Resolution for QC-06 / deviation D3 (B0's literal §16 retention exceeds 1):
- changed section: §16, §22
- change made: §16.1 defines TWO retention columns normatively. `retention` is the literal v1.0
  formula, kept and reported unchanged including B0's values above 1. `retention_scorable_only`
  restricts B0's numerator to scorable ALT reads. Every criterion reads the latter.
- status: FOLDED IN

Resolution for QC-06 / deviation D4 (§21 F8 requires a band §22's schema omitted):
- changed section: §22
- change made: `retention_ratio_p05` and `retention_ratio_p95` are named columns.
- status: FOLDED IN

Carried forward as BINDING CONSTRAINTS ON INTERPRETATION (not new criteria):
- QC-03 -> §16.4, §20.6, §24 item 11.   QC-04 -> §16.5, §15.5.6, §24 item 12.
- QC-05 -> §16.6, §20.7, §24 item 13.

Unchanged and carried over BY REFERENCE to protocols/v1.0_locked.md, not retyped:
  §2, §4, §5, §6, §7, §8, §9, §11, §12 (extended in §12.1), §13, §14 (scoped in §14),
  §18 (extended in §18.1), §19, §25.
Every section this iteration CHANGES is written out in full below.
```

## 1. Benchmark identity

```yaml
benchmark_id: BM-0001-methyl-gate-mrd
version: 1.1
status: DRAFT
supersedes: none — v1.0 is not superseded, it is EXTENDED
v1_0_relationship: >-
  v1.0 remains the locked, preregistered protocol and its registered outcome stands as recorded.
  v1.1 adds post hoc criteria that may QUALIFY or WITHDRAW the mechanism half of the v1.0
  conclusion. It CANNOT strengthen it and cannot establish anything: every added criterion is a
  further necessary condition, and surviving a necessary condition written after the confounding
  measurement was seen is not evidence for the claim, only a failure to kill it (§20.6 clause 0).
```

**CATCH-ALL, which and not the enumeration above defines the scope.** *Any section, clause,
constant, column or label in this document that is not carried over verbatim from
`protocols/v1.0_locked.md` is **POST HOC**, whether or not it is enumerated in §1.1. Enumeration
is for navigation, not for scope.* Where this document and §1's `preregistered_scope_sections`
disagree about an item, the item is treated as POST HOC.

**One post hoc change touches a PREREGISTERED criterion's INPUT and is registered as such
(MAJ-L2-01).** Raising C1's draw count from 200 to 2000 would change the median that S1, S4 clause
(ii) and F8 read. It is therefore SPLIT rather than raised: those three preregistered criteria, and
every F11-checked C1 column, read the **200-draw** vector `j = 0..199` exactly as EXEC-001
aggregated it; the 2000-draw vector is a **new, separately named** post hoc object used only by S5
leg (c), S6 leg (iii) and §15.5.8's permutation nulls (§20.1, §20.2, §21 F8, §23.2).

**Nothing in §1.1 is preregistered. A reader is entitled to discount all of it.** §20.9 argues
that every item is a further necessary condition rather than a loosened one, and §20.10 records
that one of them already fails on the data in hand.

- **§3 (hypotheses) is UNCHANGED and may not be edited** (AGENTS.md rule 1). H1 as written in

- **§6's 28 primary passes are re-run in EXEC-002** with the extended raw schema of §22.1. The

  "The BAMs, paths, levels, replicates and roles are identical" was FALSE and is deleted (X-01).

### 6.1 INPUTS — the files EXEC-002 actually opens (POST HOC §1.1; resolves X-01)

`research/evidence/EV-0052.yaml`, verified 2026-09-19 by direct inspection, records that **all 28
dilution BAMs named in `execution/inputs_manifest.tsv` and in every `execution/raw/*.json` `bam`
field are absent from disk.** They were replaced by CRAM with accompanying `.crai` on 2026-09-12
by a storage conversion run by another user, after EXEC-001 completed on 2026-08-31. An executor
who takes v1.0 §6's paths at face value aborts on the first of the 28 passes.

