## 10. Treatment arms

§10's normative gate ordering, the B1 arm, the C1 arm, the C2 arm and the empty-scorable-set case
are **UNCHANGED** and carried over verbatim from `protocols/v1.0_locked.md` §10. The constants
ML=128/255, MIN_COV=10, CONF_BAND=[0.1, 0.9], MIN_CPG=5 and **PCT=95** are unchanged, and PCT=95
remains the operating point at which S1-S5 are evaluated.

Six additions follow. None alters any v1.0 arm.

### 10.1 C3 — CpG-density-stratified permutation control (POST HOC §1.1; addresses QC-01)

C1 destroys the read-to-score correspondence completely: it is the null for *any* correspondence.
C2 carries CpG density and no methylation pattern: it is the *density comparator*. Neither is a
null for "methylation pattern beyond CpG density", which is one of the things QC-01 found
unprotected. C3 is that null, and only that null.

Steps 1-6 exactly as B1; consensus, confident-position set and cutoff **unchanged**. Then, before
step 7:

```text
1. Compute score_C2(read) = number of confident positions on the read, for every scorable read
   — the same integer already computed for C2 (run_sample.py, `score_c2[name] = t`).

2. Partition the sample's scorable reads into DENSITY STRATA by **the EXACT INTEGER value of
   score_C2** (resolves MAJ-L1-02). The iteration-7 rule used approximate DECILES, and the panel
   is right that a decile of an integer that scales with read length is not a fixed density: at
   HCC1395's EXEC-001 cut of exactly 1/13, a read with t <= 12 is kept on a SINGLE discordant
   call while a read with t = 40 needs d >= 4 and t = 200 needs d >= 16, so within a wide stratum
   `score_b1 = d/t > cut` systematically prefers LOW-t reads from the arithmetic of the ratio
   alone. A density-only filter could then satisfy leg (ii). Exact-t strata remove that, and they
   are available for free because t is integer-valued over roughly 60,000-77,000 scorable reads.

     a. Let v[0] < v[1] < ... < v[m-1] be the DISTINCT score_C2 values present, ascending, and
        let n[i] be the number of scorable reads carrying v[i]. The INITIAL strata are the m
        singleton value-classes: one stratum per distinct t.
     b. MERGE, and only for the reason stated. Walk i = 0 .. m-1 ascending, accumulating into the
        current stratum. Close the current stratum at the first i at which its accumulated count
        reaches STRATUM_MIN = 30 scorable reads. Merging is therefore only ever between ADJACENT
        t values and only ever to clear the count floor. A trailing stratum below STRATUM_MIN is
        merged into its immediate lower neighbour. Reads sharing a score_C2 value are ALWAYS in
        the same stratum. STRATUM_MIN is fixed here, before EXEC-002, and frozen by §20.8
        clause 3: 30 is the count at which a within-stratum permutation can move a read across the
        cut with probability better than 1/30, and it is the same order as §16.5's top display
        band.
     c. RECORDED PER SAMPLE (§22.1), not merely computed: the realised stratum count, the realised
        sizes, the within-stratum MIN and MAX of score_C2, and `n_merges` with the merged value
        lists. These are GATING quantities, not descriptive ones — see the VALIDITY trigger in
        §10.5.

3. Permute the score_B1 values uniformly at random WITHIN each stratum, across the scorable reads
   of that stratum, under draw index j and the seed given verbatim in §23.2.

4. cut_C3 at a ladder point is the SAME score value as B1's at that ladder point. The global
   score_B1 multiset is unchanged by a within-stratum permutation, so the ladder is unchanged by
   construction, exactly as for C1.

5. An ALT read is kept iff it is scorable and its PERMUTED score_B1 > cut_C3.
```

**What C3 preserves:** the consensus, the confident-position set, the scorable/unscorable
partition, the global score multiset, the cut, the overall keep fraction among scorable reads, and
the statistical association between a read's CpG density and its score, at stratum resolution.

**What C3 destroys:** which *individual read within its own density stratum* carries which score.

**What `B1 > C3` licenses, stated exactly.** It excludes **CpG density at stratum resolution**,
**filter aggressiveness** and **counting statistics** as explanations, because all three survive
the permutation. It does **not** exclude any other read-level property correlated with `score_B1`
within a stratum. Named, uncontrolled-by-C3 candidates: per-read basecall and modification-call
error rate, per-read modification-call CONFIDENCE, read length, local coverage, allele-specific
germline methylation, mapping quality, and **library of origin** — the mixtures are tumour spiked
into that individual's own matched normal (v1.0 §6), so tumour and normal reads come from two
different libraries and any read property discriminating library of origin produces differential
retention with no tumour biology involved (§18.1, §24 item 21).

**The design installs comparators for FIVE of these and does not claim the class is covered.** The
declared pattern-blind set is `P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}` (§10.2), addressing alignment error
rate, positional ambiguity, modification-call confidence, read length and mapping quality. **The
class of methylation-pattern-blind read properties is OPEN**; this design tests NAMED MEMBERS of
it. Local coverage, allele-specific germline methylation and library of origin remain
UNCONTROLLED and are recorded as such in §24 items 16 and 21. The iteration-7 sentence "C4a and
C4b address the two of those that this design can measure" is **DELETED**: it was false as a
matter of fact — read length, mapping quality, local coverage and per-read modification-call
confidence are all measurable, four of them at zero extra I/O in the same BAM pass — and it
implied a coverage claim the design cannot make.

> The iteration-6 draft asserted here that `B1 > C3` "can be produced only by the methylation
> pattern on the individual read". **That sentence is deleted.** It was false, and it was the
> warrant for an outcome label this protocol no longer contains. §18.1 carried the same error for
> read length and is corrected there.

C3's stochasticity and aggregation follow C1's rule in v1.0 §10: one *draw* is a complete re-run
of the arm across all of that individual's samples, blanks included, under one draw index and
seed; **B = 2000 draws** (§15.5.8, raised from 200 under MAJ-7); the reported statistic is the
median across draws with the 5th and 95th percentiles beside it.

**C3 is entirely POST HOC, so B = 2000 raises nothing that was preregistered.** C1 is different
and its draw count is SPLIT rather than raised, because three preregistered objects read C1's
median: S1's C1 term, S4 clause (ii)'s band and F8's band all read the PRESERVED `j = 0..199`
aggregation, and only the post hoc criteria read `j = 0..1999` (§15.5.8 b, §20.1, §23.2,
MAJ-L2-01).

**C3 is a control, not a candidate method.** No result may report C3 as a deployable arm.

### 10.2 P — the declared methylation-pattern-blind comparator set (POST HOC §1.1; resolves MAJ-01, MAJ-L1-01)

**Why these exist.** `score_b1 = d / t` (`run_sample.py` line 91) is a per-read *discordance rate
against the population consensus*. It is maximised by a **noisy** read as readily as by an
aberrantly methylated one, and C3 destroys the read-to-error-rate correspondence exactly as
thoroughly as it destroys the read-to-pattern correspondence. So a filter that knows only per-read
error rate passes C3.

**OBSERVATION (EXEC-001, `execution/raw/HCC1395_TF0_rep1.json`, `results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`).**
On HCC1395 TF0 rep1 — a blank — B1 keeps **10 of 25** scorable ALT reads
(`retention_scorable_only` 0.400) while keeping `K_B1 / n_scorable = 2895 / 58990 = 4.908%` of all
scorable reads. The ratio is **8.15**.

**ASSUMPTION, named as an assumption (AGENTS.md rule 8).** `A1`: an ALT read in a pure-normal
blank passing the `1 <= alt <= 2` gate carries a spurious ALT base — a basecall or alignment
error. `A1` is an assumption and not a construction: such a read can also be a mapping artifact,
or a low-frequency germline or subclonal call that the candidate frame did not remove. This
protocol does not test `A1` and no criterion rests on it.

**INTERPRETATION, labelled as interpretation and separated from both (AGENTS.md rule 8).** If `A1`
holds for most such reads, the 8.15 observation means `score_b1` is coupled to a read's propensity
to carry a spurious ALT base, with no tumour methylation involved. That is an interpretation of one
number under one named assumption. It is what motivates the comparator set below; it is not a
finding of this benchmark, and §16.7 (a) makes the observation a standing output so a reader can
judge it independently.

A second mechanism points the same way. EXEC-001's `cut` on HCC1395 is
`0.07692307692307693` = exactly **1/13**, and on COLO829 `0.1` = **1/10** and
`0.0967741935483871` = **3/31** (`execution/raw/*.json`). At a cut of 1/13, any read with
`t <= 12` and a **single** discordant call is kept, because `1/12 > 1/13`. For a share of kept
reads the entire "methylation pattern" evidence is one discordant modification call, which carries
no pattern at all and is the signature of call noise. §16.7 makes the joint distribution of `d`
and `t` among kept ALT reads a required output so that share is visible rather than argued about.

**THE DECLARED SET.** `P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}`. All five are deterministic, all five are
built in the same BAM pass on the same scorable set, all five use the same strict `> cut` rule,
and all five are swept over the same count ladder as B1. **None of them uses the DIRECTION of any
per-read methylation call**, which is what makes them pattern-blind; C4c uses the CONFIDENCE of
those calls and §10.2's invariance proof below is what establishes that confidence carries no
pattern. Adding three arms costs no new file and no new I/O: `ref_calls` (`run_sample.py` lines
30-35) already reads every `(qpos, p)` from `rd.modified_bases` and discards `p` after
thresholding at `ML`, and `rd.query_alignment_length` and `rd.mapping_quality` are already on the
record the pileup returns (`MIN_MQ = 20` is already filtered on in §9).

```text
C4a  score_C4a(read) = NM(read) / read.query_alignment_length
     NM is the aligned-mismatch tag already present in the source BAMs. Where the tag is absent
     the executor computes the mismatch count deterministically from
     get_aligned_pairs(matches_only=True, with_seq=True); n_reads_missing_NM is recorded per
     sample (§22.1) and a sample with more than 1% missing is flagged NM_SPARSE and its C4a leg
     returns UNDECIDABLE for that sample.
     What it knows: the read's alignment error rate. What it does not know: methylation, at all.

C4b  POSITIONAL-AMBIGUITY comparator (renamed at iteration 8; it is NOT a per-read call-quality
     comparator, and the iteration-7 name invited exactly that misreading)
     score_C4b(read) = n_amb(read) / n_cov(read)
     n_cov  = the number of the read's aligned modification-call positions that lie in the
              consensus set `cons` (coverage >= MIN_COV)
     n_amb  = of those, the number whose CONSENSUS beta lies inside CONF_BAND, i.e.
              CONF_LO <= cons[r] <= CONF_HI — the positions the protocol calls NOT confident
     n_cov >= t >= MIN_CPG = 5 for every scorable read, so the denominator is never zero.
     What it knows: how much of the read's genomic FOOTPRINT sits at ambiguous consensus
     positions. What it does not know: the read's own modification calls.
     INVARIANCE, stated as APPROXIMATE rather than exact (minor finding adopted): `cons` is built
     from the calls of every read touched at the candidate set, this one included
     (run_sample.py lines 70-77), so at MIN_COV = 10 a single read can contribute up to one tenth
     of a position's beta and could in principle move it across CONF_LO or CONF_HI. The
     invariance is therefore exact only in the limit of large per-position coverage. The realised
     per-position coverage distribution is emitted (§22.1 `cons_cov_quantiles`) so the bound is
     readable rather than asserted. The iteration-7 claim "invariant to every modification call ON
     the read" is corrected here on the same principle that deleted the analogous exact-sounding
     C3 claim in §10.1.

C4c  MODIFICATION-CALL UNCERTAINTY comparator (NEW at iteration 8; resolves MAJ-L1-01)
     For each of the read's aligned modification-call positions r that lies in the CONFIDENT set
     `conf` (the same t positions that form score_b1's denominator), let p be the ML probability
     byte pysam returns in rd.modified_bases, 0 <= p <= 255. Define
         u(p)         = 1 - | 2*p/255 - 1 |            in [0, 1]
         score_C4c    = mean of u(p) over the read's t confident-consensus positions
     t >= MIN_CPG = 5 for every scorable read, so the denominator is never zero.
     What it knows: how close this read's modification calls sat to the ML decision boundary.
     PROOF THAT IT IS PATTERN-BLIND, which is the property that makes it an admissible control:
         u(p) = u(255 - p) for every p. Replacing any call's probability p by 255 - p flips the
         thresholded call (p >= ML becomes p < ML and conversely, for ML = 128 and integer p,
         with the single boundary case p = 127/128 mapping onto itself) and leaves score_C4c
         EXACTLY unchanged. score_C4c is therefore invariant under flipping ANY subset of the
         read's calls to their confident opposites, so it carries ZERO information about WHICH
         positions are called methylated. It is a pure uncertainty statistic.
     WHY IT IS THE ADVERSARY THE PANEL NAMED: an uncertain call is a call that disagrees with a
     confident consensus at close to chance rate, so score_C4c is correlated with d/t BY
     CONSTRUCTION while knowing no pattern at all. C4a cannot see it (alignment mismatch is a
     different noise channel) and C4b cannot see it (C4b is a property of the footprint, not of
     the calls). Without C4c, leg (iv) was satisfiable by the confound it exists to exclude.

C4d  READ LENGTH comparator (NEW at iteration 8)
     score_C4d(read) = read.query_alignment_length, the same integer that is C4a's denominator.
     What it knows: how long the read is. What it does not know: methylation, at all.
     Direction: see the DIRECTION RULE below — C4d is swept in BOTH directions.

C4e  MAPPING QUALITY comparator (NEW at iteration 8)
     score_C4e(read) = read.mapping_quality, already filtered at MIN_MQ = 20 by §9.
     What it knows: the aligner's confidence in the read's placement. What it does not know:
     methylation, at all. Swept in BOTH directions, as C4d is.
     DEGENERACY GUARD: MAPQ is heavily atomic on these data (a large share of reads at 60). If
     fewer than 5 distinct score_C4e values are present among the scorable reads of a sample, or
     if the attainable kept-ALT set has fewer than 3 points inside the evaluable retention range,
     C4e is flagged MAPQ_DEGENERATE for that sample and its contribution to leg (iv) returns
     UNDECIDABLE there — never HOLDS, never a refutation. The count of distinct values is emitted.
```

**DIRECTION RULE.** C4a, C4b and C4c keep the **high** tail, the same direction as B1: a noisier
read has a higher mismatch rate, sits on more ambiguous positions, and carries less confident
calls, exactly as it has a higher `d/t`. C4d and C4e have **no a priori direction** — either long
or short reads, either high or low MAPQ, could be the library-discriminating property — so each is
swept in **both** directions (`C4d+`, `C4d-`, `C4e+`, `C4e-`) and enters leg (iv) as the better of
its two directions **for the comparator**, i.e. the worse case for B1. Choosing the direction
after the curves are seen would be goalpost-moving; taking the worse case for B1 in advance is
not, and it is the same convention leg (iv) already applies across members of P.

**TAG AND FIELD AVAILABILITY IS MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED (resolves X-02).** §14's TAG PRECHECK
samples reads from every input before the 28 passes and records the presence and count of `NM` and
`MD`, and the presence of `query_alignment_length` and `mapping_quality`. `pysam`'s
`get_aligned_pairs(matches_only=True, with_seq=True)` **requires an MD tag and raises otherwise**;
it does not fetch a reference. Therefore:

```text
NM present            C4a computed from the tag.
NM absent, MD present C4a computed from get_aligned_pairs(matches_only=True, with_seq=True).
NM and MD both absent C4a is NOT COMPUTED. Every C4a leg returns UNDECIDABLE, on every sample of
                      that individual, and the fact is reported. The executor MAY NOT open the
                      reference to recover NM (§14).
NM_SPARSE             fires off the MEASURED per-sample count of reads missing NM, above 1%.
                      EV-0052 records that the CRAM conversion was run with store_md=1 and
                      store_nm=1, so NM/MD on the decoded records may be a product of the
                      conversion rather than an original annotation; the precheck measures what
                      is actually there rather than assuming either way.
```

**Every member of P is a control, not a candidate method.** No result may report any of them as
deployable.

**Why five and not one, and what the set does NOT establish.** No single pattern-blind twin is
obviously the right one, so requiring B1 to beat a set removes the choice of twin as a free
variable and is strictly harder than requiring it to beat any one member. S6 leg (iv) and S5 leg
(d) are written against the **worse case for B1 over the whole of P** (§20.3, §20.4), and §21 F12
fires on **any** member. **This does not establish that no read property explains B1's advantage.**
P has five named members; the class is open; §18.1 and §24 items 16 and 21 say so, and the
library-of-origin confound in particular has no comparator in this design.

### 10.3 The threshold as a swept parameter (POST HOC §1.1; addresses QC-02)

For the retention sweep of §15.5, and **for that analysis only**, the per-sample cutoff is
evaluated over a *ladder of attainable operating points* (§15.5.2) instead of at the single locked
percentile 95.

```text
- The ladder point is ARM-SPECIFIC and, for the sweep only, SAMPLE-SPECIFIC. Reaching a common
  realised kept-ALT count requires different cutoffs in different arms and in different
  replicates, which is the entire point of the sweep (§15.5.4 states the matching rule and why
  sample-specific thresholds are a NARROWING change, not a loosening one).
- WITHIN EACH z COMPUTATION a single threshold rule applies to every sample of that individual —
  blanks and evaluated sample alike — exactly as v1.0 §10 requires. **What a "rule" IS, and how it
  transfers from the sample where it was chosen to the other samples of the same z computation, is
  defined normatively in §15.5.4 as the GLOBAL KEPT-READ COUNT TRANSFER** (resolves MAJ-L1-03,
  X-03). The iteration-7 draft asserted that a rule exists without saying what a rule is, which
  left at least four inequivalent transfers available and let an executor pick. The sweep varies
  the rule BETWEEN z computations, never inside one. This is the amendment MAJ-04 required; it is
  scoped to §15.5 and applies to no criterion evaluated at the operating point.
- B1, C1, C3 and every member of P keep the frozen `> cut` rule; C2 keeps the top-k rule.
- the gate, the candidate universe, the quality thresholds, the consensus, the confident-position
  set, the scorable set, the blank construction, both variance constructions and the call
  threshold 3.0 are UNCHANGED at every ladder point.
```

**Nesting, and why the sweep is well defined.** Lowering the cut, or raising k, produces a kept set
that is a **superset** of the kept set at the stricter threshold. Realised retention is therefore
monotone non-decreasing along the ladder, in every arm, in every sample. This is a property of the
rules, not an assumption; the executor must **verify** it and report any violation as an
implementation fault (§21 F10), not interpret it.

### 10.4 Arms summary after this revision

```text
B0          SNV only, no filter                                    UNCHANGED (v1.0 §9)
B0_scorable B0 restricted to scorable ALT reads; the R = 1 anchor  NEW, POST HOC (§15.5.7)
B1          SNV + per-read methylation score                       UNCHANGED (v1.0 §10)
C1          permuted-score control, correspondence destroyed       UNCHANGED (v1.0 §10)
C2          CpG-density control, top-k, now with a seed band       band NEW, POST HOC (§10.6)
C3          density-STRATIFIED permuted-score control              NEW, POST HOC (§10.1)
C4a         per-read alignment mismatch rate                       NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
C4b         positional-ambiguity fraction of the read's footprint   NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
C4c         per-read modification-call UNCERTAINTY                  NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
C4d         read length (swept in both directions)                  NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
C4e         mapping quality (swept in both directions)              NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
P           the declared pattern-blind SET {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}  NEW, POST HOC (§10.2)
```

### 10.5 C3 informativeness diagnostic, and what it is measured on

C3 holds density fixed at the *stratum* level, not read by read. If methylation pattern and CpG
density are near-deterministically related within a stratum, C3's permutation is close to the
identity and the control loses power **in the conservative direction** — it makes `B1 > C3` harder
to satisfy, not easier. That is the benign case.

The iteration-6 draft triggered on "median within-stratum score variance below 1% of the global
score variance". The panel is right that this is the wrong statistic: what determines whether the
permutation can move the kept set is **how many ALT reads lie in strata that straddle the cut**. A
stratum with large variance lying wholly above or wholly below the cut randomises nothing; a
stratum with small variance straddling the cut randomises a great deal. The trigger is therefore
rewritten:

```text
Per sample, per ladder point, report:
    n_straddling_strata          strata containing at least one read on each side of the cut
    n_alt_exchangeable           scorable ALT reads lying in straddling strata
    within_stratum_score_range   MIN and MAX of score_C2 per stratum (how wide "fixed" is)
    stratum_width_max            max over realised strata of (score_C2 MAX / score_C2 MIN)
    n_strata, n_merges           realised stratum count and the merges §10.1 step 2b made
    within_stratum_score_var     retained as a descriptive column only

POWER TRIGGER: if, at a target, `n_alt_exchangeable` is below 20 on either individual, C3 is
reported as UNINFORMATIVE at that target and S6 leg (ii) — the C3/NULL-2 leg — returns UNDECIDABLE
there, never HELD and never a refutation.

VALIDITY TRIGGER (NEW at iteration 8; resolves MAJ-L1-02). The power trigger asks whether the
permutation CAN move anything. It says nothing about whether density is actually held fixed. So,
additionally, at a target:
    if  stratum_width_max > STRATUM_WIDTH_MAX = 1.25
    OR  any realised stratum's [t_min, t_max] straddles 1/cut — the value at which a SINGLE
        discordant call changes the keep decision, so that within that stratum `d/t > cut`
        systematically prefers low-t reads
then leg (ii) returns UNDECIDABLE at that target on that individual — never HELD, and never a
refutation. Both quantities are emitted per sample per ladder point and both are GATING, not
descriptive. STRATUM_WIDTH_MAX is fixed here, before EXEC-002, and frozen by §20.8 clause 3.
```

**What the two triggers together buy, stated exactly.** With exact-t strata the expected value of
`stratum_width_max` is 1 wherever no merge was needed, and departures from 1 measure exactly how
far the realised stratification fell short of "fixed density". `B1 > C3` therefore excludes CpG
density **at the realised stratum resolution**, and that resolution is a REPORTED NUMBER rather
than an adjective (§18.1).

The constant 20 is fixed here, before EXEC-002, and frozen by §20.8 clause 3. It is the count
below which a permutation over the exchangeable set cannot produce a 5th-to-95th-percentile band
narrower than one kept read at the observed `scorable_ALT` of 35-63 (§15.5.1).

> The iteration-6 draft sent an uninformative C3 to leg (iii). Leg (iii) is the C1 leg. **The
> reference is corrected to leg (ii).** A near-identity C3 makes `Z(C3)` approximately `Z(B1)`, so
> leg (ii) would otherwise fail and produce a negative mechanism verdict from a control that never
> moved.

Additionally, `score_B1 = d / t` takes at most `t + 1` distinct values within a stratum of fixed
`t`, and `MIN_CPG = 5`, so ties are dense. The tie-handling rule of §15.5.8 (b) applies to C3 as
it does to C1.

### 10.6 C2's tie-break band (POST HOC §1.1; resolves MAJ-02)

`score_C2` is integer-valued with heavy ties, and C2's top-k boundary is resolved by a seeded
shuffle of the tied block (`run_sample.py` lines 110-117). C2 is the comparator that killed v1.0's
mechanism claim, and in the iteration-6 draft it was the only arm with no uncertainty of any kind.
Its realised numbers are one-read objects: HCC1395's C2 blank ALT retention 0.037706 is the mean
over five TF0 replicates of kept counts **0, 1, 1, 1, 1** — four reads in total — and COLO829's
0.021429 is **0, 1, 0, 0, 2** — three reads (`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`).

```text
C2 is evaluated over 200 TIE-BREAK SEEDS, indexed j = 0 .. 199.
    j = 0 uses EXEC-001's seed EXACTLY (§23.2), so the PCT = 95 slice is byte-identical to
          EXEC-001 and the reproduction check of §15.5.7 V-a and §21 F11 is untouched.
    j = 1..199 use the derivation in §23.2.
The BAM pass is shared, so this costs nothing beyond aggregation arithmetic: the tie-break
reorders a stored index array.

Emitted for C2 exactly as for C1 and C3:
    retention_ratio_p05 / p50 / p95        across seeds
    zlo_min_p05 / p50 / p95                across seeds
    n_tied_at_k                            per sample: the number of reads tied at the top-k
                                           boundary, so the size of the seed-dependent block is
                                           visible rather than inferred
The point value reported beside the band, and used for F11, is j = 0.
```

S5 leg (b) and S6 leg (i) are evaluated against this band (§20.3, §20.4). The band is combined
with the paired bootstrap of §15.5.8 by taking, for each bootstrap resample, a seed index drawn
uniformly from 0..199, so seed variation and sampling variation enter the same interval.

**What the combined interval is an interval FOR, and the required decomposition (minor finding
adopted).** Averaging the seed into the resample makes `delta_lo` / `delta_hi` and
`retention_ratio_lo` / `retention_ratio_hi` intervals for a **seed-averaged C2**, while the POINT
value, §21 F11's reproduction check and §20.10's one-read sensitivity argument all concern the
**j = 0** comparator. Those are two different objects and the protocol says so rather than letting
one stand in for the other. REQUIRED: the seed-only and bootstrap-only components are reported
separately —
```text
seed_only_p05 / p95        the percentile band across the 200 seeds at the OBSERVED candidate set
boot_only_p05 / p95        the percentile band across the 2000 resamples at seed j = 0
combined_p05 / p95         the joint band, which is what S5 leg (b) and S6 leg (i) read
```
so the 200-seed spread on four kept blank reads is **visible** rather than absorbed. Where
`seed_only` spans more than half the width of `combined`, the row is flagged `SEED_DOMINATED` and
that flag must be printed beside any statement of that leg's outcome.

## 11. Features and evidence provenance

**UNCHANGED**, carried over from v1.0 §11, with three rows appended:

| Evidence | Source | Used by | Available at inference? | Leakage risk | Missingness |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Density stratum index per read | derived from `score_C2`, sample under test only | C3 | yes | none — sample-internal, identical to C2's input | same scorable set as B1 |
| Per-read alignment mismatch rate | `NM` tag, else `MD` via `get_aligned_pairs(with_seq=True)`, on the sample's own CRAM | C4a | yes | none — sample-internal, no new file | MEASURED by §14's tag precheck; `n_reads_missing_NM` recorded; >1% flags NM_SPARSE; NM and MD both absent -> C4a not computed, every C4a leg UNDECIDABLE |
| Positional ambiguity of the read's footprint | the sample's own consensus and the read's covered positions | C4b | yes | none — sample-internal, identical inputs to B1 | same scorable set as B1 |
| Per-read modification-call uncertainty | the ML probability bytes of `rd.modified_bases`, already read and discarded by `ref_calls` | C4c | yes | none — sample-internal, identical inputs to B1 | same scorable set as B1 |
| Read length | `read.query_alignment_length` on the sample's own CRAM | C4d | yes | none — sample-internal, no new file | present on every aligned record |
| Mapping quality | `read.mapping_quality`, already filtered at MIN_MQ = 20 by §9 | C4e | yes | none — sample-internal, no new file | present on every aligned record; MAPQ_DEGENERATE flagged below 5 distinct values |

`uses_truth = FALSE` for every new feature, as for every other. C3 and every member of P open
**no file** B1 does not open. The DECODE REFERENCE (§6.1) is opened by every worker, is new
relative to EXEC-001, and is declared and justified in §14.

## 12. Experimental controls

**UNCHANGED**, carried over from v1.0 §12, with §12.1 appended.

### 12.1 Controls added at iterations 6 and 7

- **Methylation pattern versus CpG density.** C3 holds density fixed at the resolution of an
  EXACT score_C2 value, subject to the merges §10.1 step 2b records and the VALIDITY trigger of
  §10.5 (§10.1). C2 alone could not: QC-01 measured C2 reproducing B1's S4 signature to within
  0.6% / 0.3%. C3 does not ISOLATE methylation pattern; it removes CpG density at the realised
  stratum resolution, which is a reported number (§18.1).
- **Methylation pattern versus named read properties.** The declared set
  `P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}` tests five named methylation-pattern-blind read properties
  (§10.2). C3 alone could not: a within-stratum permutation destroys the read-to-error-rate
  correspondence as thoroughly as the read-to-pattern one.
  **The iteration-7 sentence "C4a and C4b isolate it" is DELETED. It was false.** C4a is alignment
  mismatch — a different noise channel from modification-call noise — and C4b is a property of the
  read's genomic footprint rather than of its calls, so neither could see the property that
  GENERATES `score_b1`. C4c is installed for exactly that property (§10.2). Even with five arms
  the class of pattern-blind read properties is **OPEN** and this design tests NAMED MEMBERS of
  it; local coverage, allele-specific germline methylation and **library of origin** remain
  uncontrolled (§18.1, §24 items 16 and 21).
- **Count scale / position on the precision curve.** The retention sweep (§15.5) removes it as a
  free variable by comparing arms at matched realised ALT retention. Because every filtered arm
  shares one scorable ALT set by construction (v1.0 §10, §12), matching realised retention **in a
  single sample** is identically matching the kept-ALT count in that sample. That equivalence is
  exact per sample and is **not** exact for a mean over replicates, which is why §15.5.4 matches
  per replicate on the integer count rather than on a replicate mean.
  QC-02 measured B1 counting 8-10x as many reads as either control at the locked operating point.
- **Both matching directions.** The sweep is evaluated on two axes (§15.5.4), so no conclusion can
  rest on the choice of which level the retention is matched at.
- **Tie-break noise in the deciding comparator.** §10.6.
