## 14. Leakage guards

**UNCHANGED**, carried over from v1.0 §14. EXEC-002 must repeat the file-open capture in full;
QC-07's verification is **not inherited, it is re-earned**. The extended raw schema of §22.1
stores *more* per-sample internal state and **no** new input.

The guarantee is scoped so that it is satisfiable as written:

```text
PER-WORKER CAPTURE (the guarantee QC-07 verified), REWRITTEN so it is SATISFIABLE (resolves X-02)
    Each of the 28 passes records the paths it opens, exactly as EXEC-001's run_sample.py does.
    THE EXPECTED PER-WORKER SET, ENUMERATED:
        the worker's own input CRAM                        (§6.1)
        the matching .cram.crai                            (§6.1)
        the two candidate frames                           (v1.0 §8, unchanged)
        /big8_disk/ref/GRCh38_no_alt_analysis_set.fasta    (§6.1) with md5
                                                           a6da8681616c05eb542f1d91606a7b2f,
                                                           and its .fai
    files_opened.txt is checked PER WORKER against that set. The iteration-7 expectation — "no
    path absent from EXEC-001's 28 primary-pass set" — could NOT be met, because the inputs are
    now CRAM and CRAM decoding requires an index and a reference. Leaving it as written would
    have forced the executor either to declare a deviation against the leakage guard, which is
    the one guard that may never be resolved by a deviation, or to widen the expected set
    silently, which destroys the audit value of the capture.

    WHY THE DECODE REFERENCE IS NON-LEAKING, in QC-07's own terms: a genome reference carries
    NO TRUTH LABEL, NO MATCHED NORMAL, NO SEQC2 OR ORTHOGONAL TRUTH VCF, NO PANEL OF NORMALS AND
    NO POPULATION DATABASE. It is the decode dictionary for reference-compressed records that
    were, before 2026-09-12, stored uncompressed in the file the worker opened. Its md5 is
    recorded and checked, so it cannot be swapped for something else.

    THE PROHIBITION STAYS SHARP, on the four named classes and not on "any new path":
    no matched-normal alignment, no SEQC2 or other orthogonal truth VCF, no panel of normals, no
    population database may be opened by any worker or by aggregation. A path outside the
    enumerated set and outside the declared aggregation reads below is a LEAKAGE FAULT and the
    execution is RETURNED.

    C3 and every member of P add NO path; they read the same CRAM the same worker already opened.
    The executor MAY NOT open the reference to recover a missing NM (§10.2): where NM and MD are
    both absent, C4a is not computed and its legs return UNDECIDABLE.

TAG PRECHECK, BEFORE THE 28 PASSES (resolves X-02; first line of §26's checklist after the
input-availability check)
    Sample reads from every one of the 28 inputs and record, per input:
        n_sampled, n_with_NM, n_with_MD, n_with_query_alignment_length, n_with_mapping_quality,
        n_distinct_mapping_quality
    NM_SPARSE fires off the MEASURED fraction missing NM, above 1%. MAPQ_DEGENERATE fires off the
    MEASURED distinct-value count, below 5 (§10.2). Neither is assumed. The precheck writes
    execution_v1_1/tag_precheck.tsv and opens only the inputs, their indices and the reference.

AGGREGATION-SIDE READS (declared, non-leaking, and NOT part of the per-worker guarantee)
    EXEC-002's aggregation additionally opens, read-only:
        tools/mrdz/mrdz/score.py                 the normative interval implementation
        execution/raw/*.json                     EXEC-001 raw, for the V-a reconstruction identity
        results/ablation_results.tsv             EXEC-001 ablation, for F11
        results/per_sample_metrics.tsv           EXEC-001 per-sample, for F11
        results/c1_draws.tsv                     EXEC-001's 200-draw C1 vector, for F11 and F8
        results/statistical_tests.tsv            EXEC-001's row, copied forward unmodified (§22.3)
        execution/inputs_manifest.tsv            the unit identifiers §6.1 maps onto CRAM
        protocols/v1.0_locked.md                 for the byte comparison §20.0 and §26 require
    These are this benchmark's own prior outputs on the same samples. They carry no truth label,
    no matched normal and no external database, so they cannot leak test information into a
    feature. They are listed here so that `files_opened.txt` can be checked against a complete
    expectation rather than against an unsatisfiable one.
```

Two guards stated because the sweep creates the opportunity:

| path | guard | how verified |
|---|---|---|
| operating point selected from the sweep | the sweep may not change the operating point for any criterion; PCT=95 stays the operating point of S1-S5 (§10.3, §20.8) | the sweep outputs and the criteria outputs are separate artifacts; `criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv` records `operating_pct=95` on every S1-S5 row |
| retention target chosen after seeing the curves | the target set T is fixed in §15.5.3 and frozen by §20.8 clause 3 | protocol checksummed before execution |
| region label chosen after seeing the curves | `region` is defined numerically in §15.5.11 and frozen by §20.8 clause 3 | the constants are in §1.1 and in the checksummed protocol |

## 15. Primary metric

### 15.1-15.4 UNCHANGED

The normative implementation (`mrdz.score.blank_spread`, `z_interval`, `interval_verdict`, call
threshold 3.0), the two variance constructions V1 and V2, the metric definition
(`zlo_min` = min over the three 0.1% replicates of `lo`, blanks = TF0 rep1-3), the undefined
cases, and the arguments for the level and for the lower bound are carried over **verbatim** from
`protocols/v1.0_locked.md` §15. `primary_metric` remains `zlo_min at 0.1%, reported under BOTH
variance constructions`.

v1.0 §15's differential-retention argument also stands as written, and QC-01 does not contradict
it: a filter with level-independent retention cannot move z, so differential retention remains
*necessary*. What QC-01 established is that it is **not sufficient to identify the mechanism**,
because a methylation-blind filter achieves the same differential. §15.5 is the repair.

### 15.5 THE RETENTION SWEEP (POST HOC §1.1; addresses QC-02)

#### 15.5.1 Realised ALT retention, and why the sweep is on COUNTS

For arm A, sample s, ladder point g:

```text
scorable_ALT(s)   = sum over GATE-PASSING candidates of ( alt_total - alt_unscorable )
kept_ALT(A,s,g)   = sum over GATE-PASSING candidates of the ALT reads arm A keeps at g
R(A,s,g)          = kept_ALT(A,s,g) / scorable_ALT(s)
```

Notes that make this exact rather than approximate:

- The denominator `scorable_ALT(s)` **does not depend on the arm and does not depend on g**. B1,
  C1, C2, C3, C4a and C4b share one scorable set by construction (v1.0 §10, §12). **Within a
  single sample**, matching R across arms is therefore identically matching the integer kept-ALT
  count. That equivalence does **not** survive averaging over replicates, which is why §15.5.4
  matches per replicate and §15.5.7 matches on the integer count.
- The gate is applied first, on the unfiltered ALT count, in every arm at every ladder point
  (v1.0 §10 normative). The gate-passing candidate set is the same in all arms and at all g.
- `R` is exactly the quantity D3 named `retention_scorable_only` (§16.1). For `B0_scorable` it is
  1.0 by definition, which gives the sweep a required anchor (§15.5.7 V-c).
- `scorable_ALT(s)` is small: **45 / 35 / 45** on HCC1395's three 0.1% replicates and
  **51 / 55 / 63** on COLO829's; blanks are **25 / 22 / 20** and **33 / 20 / 30** on TF0 rep1-3
  (`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`). R is therefore quantised in steps of roughly 1/20 to 1/63.
  **The sweep is a sweep over integers.** Every rule below is written against that granularity.

#### 15.5.2 The attainable-operating-point ladder (replaces the percentile grid)

The iteration-6 draft swept a fixed percentile grid and bisected it to resolve retention gaps.
That cannot work, and EXEC-001 says so directly: COLO829 TF0 rep1 has `cut = 0.100000` (= 1/10)
with `K_B1 = 3020`, while rep2 has `cut = 0.0967741935483871` (= 3/31) with `K_B1 = 3821`, at
essentially identical `n_scorable` (76,594 against 76,583) — a cut difference of 0.0032 moving
about 800 reads, roughly 26% of the kept set (`execution/raw/COLO829_TF0_rep*.json`). `score_b1`
is a discrete rational multiset with heavy atoms, and the attainable cuts are exactly its order
statistics. Bisecting a percentile cannot place a cut inside an atom. **The percentile grid and
the bisection rule are deleted.**

```text
LADDER, per arm, per sample. Defined on the STORED arrays of §22.1, not on floating-point
percentiles, so it is exact and its PCT = 95 slice is exact by construction.

For B1, C1, C3, C4a, C4b (the `> cut` arms):
    let  v[0] <= v[1] <= ... <= v[n-1]   be the arm's per-read scores over the n scorable reads,
    sorted ascending, exactly as run_sample.py sorts them.
    LADDER INDEX i in {0, 1, ..., n-1} means  cut = v[i]  and  keep = { reads with score > v[i] }.
    The EXEC-001 operating point is the index  i95 = int( 0.95 * (n - 1) ),  which is literally the
    expression run_sample.py evaluates. The equivalent nominal percentile, reported for continuity,
    is  100 * i / (n - 1).

For C2 (the top-k arm):
    LADDER INDEX k in {0, 1, ..., n},  keep = the first k reads of the tie-broken descending order.
    The EXEC-001 operating point is  k95 = max(0, n - 1 - int(0.95 * (n - 1))),  literally
    run_sample.py's expression.

ATTAINABLE KEPT-ALT COUNTS. Walking the ladder from the strictest end, kept_ALT(A,s,.) is a
non-decreasing integer step function. Its attainable set may SKIP integers where several ALT reads
share a score. The attainable range [n_min(A,s), n_max(A,s)] and the full attainable set are
recorded per arm per sample (§22.1). For the `> cut` arms n_min = 0 and n_max is the number of
scorable ALT reads scoring strictly above the minimum score; for C2 n_max is every scorable ALT
read. A target outside the attainable set is handled by §15.5.7 step 3.

CANONICAL LADDER POINT FOR A GIVEN KEPT-ALT COUNT, WITHIN ONE SAMPLE. Where several ladder
indices realise the same kept-ALT count in a sample, the canonical point is the LARGEST index (the
strictest threshold, hence the smallest global keep fraction) realising it. This is deterministic,
contains no choice, and is applied identically to every arm.

GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION OF A LADDER POINT. For every arm and every ladder point, define
    phi(A, s, g) = (number of SCORABLE READS arm A keeps at g in sample s) / n_scorable_reads(s)
For the `> cut` arms this is |{i : v[i] > v[g]}| / n; for C2 it is k / n. It is an exactly
computable rational in every arm, it is the quantity QC-02 calls "how aggressive the filter was",
and it is the object §15.5.4's threshold transfer moves between samples.

CANONICAL LADDER POINT FOR A GIVEN GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION, IN ANOTHER SAMPLE (NEW at iteration 8;
required by MAJ-L1-03 and X-03). Given a target keep fraction phi and a sample s', the canonical
point is the LARGEST ladder index of that arm in s' whose kept scorable-read count equals
    K'(phi, s') = round( phi * n_scorable_reads(s') ).
If no index realises K' exactly — possible for the `> cut` arms where scores tie — the nearest
attainable kept-scorable-read count is used, ties broken to the LOWER count, and the realised
|K'_realised - K'| is recorded. Where that difference exceeds KEEP_COUNT_TOL = 0.001 * n_scorable
the transfer FAILS and the target returns UNDECIDABLE for that leg, never a pass. KEEP_COUNT_TOL
is fixed here, before EXEC-002, and frozen by §20.8 clause 3.

CANONICAL LADDER POINT FOR A TARGET STATED AS A MEAN OVER THREE BLANKS (AXIS-B; NEW at iteration
8). §15.5.2's within-sample definition does not reach a target defined as a mean over three
samples, and the mean of three integers need not be an integer. The definition is completed in
§15.5.4 under AXIS-B: the arm is placed at ONE global keep fraction phi, realised in each of the
three blanks by the rule above; the attainable set on AXIS-B is therefore the set of phi values
attainable in ALL THREE blanks; and the canonical phi for a target mean is the LARGEST attainable
phi whose realised mean kept-ALT over the three blanks is closest to the target, ties broken to
the LOWER mean. Attainability, the realised mean, and the tolerance rule for a mean are stated in
§15.5.7 step 3.
```

`retention_sweep.tsv` reports the whole ladder, not only the matched points, so the curves are
readable and auditable (§22.2).

#### 15.5.3 The matched-retention targets

Fixed here, before EXEC-002, frozen by §20.8 clause 3:

```text
T = { 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70 }
```

Spacing 0.10 is roughly 4-6 ALT reads at the observed `scorable_ALT` of 35-63; finer targets would
be below the granularity of the quantity being matched. Targets are evaluated only where they
survive the count floor (§15.5.6) and are classified by §15.5.11.

#### 15.5.4 The two retention axes, and the matching rule

The sweep is evaluated **twice**, and the criterion must hold on **both**.

```text
AXIS-L  (level-matched)   the arms are matched on kept ALT at 0.1%, PER REPLICATE
AXIS-B  (blank-matched)   the arms are matched on kept ALT in the blanks, PER BLANK REPLICATE
```

**AXIS-L, stated exactly.** For target `tau`, individual `i`, arm `A`:

```text
For each 0.1% replicate r in {1, 2, 3}:
    n_tau(r) = round( tau * scorable_ALT(r) )                 [ the SAME integer for every arm ]
    choose the canonical ladder point g(A, r) of arm A IN REPLICATE r that realises
    kept_ALT(A, r, g) = n_tau(r) exactly, or the nearest attainable count under §15.5.7 step 3.
    Let phi(A, r) = the GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION of g(A, r) in replicate r (§15.5.2).
    Evaluate a COMPLETE single-threshold z computation of individual i by transferring phi(A, r)
    to each blank under the rule below:
        blanks = arm A's gated totals on TF0 rep1, rep2, rep3 at that arm's canonical ladder
                 point realising the GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION phi(A, r) in each blank (§15.5.2)
        z, lo, hi = z_interval( n_tau(r), blanks, sample_sd_A(r) )
Z(A, i, v, tau) = min over r of lo(r)                          [ v1.0's aggregation, unchanged ]
```

**THE THRESHOLD TRANSFER, STATED ONCE, NORMATIVELY, AND IDENTICALLY FOR EVERY ARM (resolves
MAJ-L1-03 and X-03).** The iteration-7 text said "AT THAT SAME NOMINAL RULE" and never defined
what a rule IS, so at least four inequivalent transfers were available — same ladder INDEX, same
nominal PERCENTILE, same CUT VALUE, same global KEEP FRACTION — and on AXIS-L the entire leg is
decided by the blank counts those transfers produce. §15.5.2's own evidence shows the difference is
not a rounding question: COLO829 TF0 rep1 has cut 0.100000 = 1/10 with `K_B1` = 3020 against rep2's
0.0967741935483871 = 3/31 with `K_B1` = 3821 at essentially identical `n_scorable` — 0.0032 of cut
moving about 800 reads.

```text
THE RULE:  a ladder point is characterised by its GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION  phi = K / n_scorable  in
           the sample where it was chosen, and the SAME phi is realised in every other sample of
           that z computation by that arm's canonical ladder point for phi there (§15.5.2).
FORBIDDEN, and named as forbidden:
           - transferring the LADDER INDEX i          (meaningless across differing n_scorable)
           - transferring the nominal PERCENTILE      (§15.5.2 rejects percentiles outright)
           - transferring the CUT VALUE v[i]          (see below)
APPLIED IDENTICALLY to B1, C1, C2, C3 and every member of P. No mixed rule is permitted.
RECORDED on every row of matched_retention_comparison.tsv, for BOTH arms: the realised per-sample
           ladder index, the realised cut or k, the realised global keep fraction, and the realised
           kept-scorable-read count, in the evaluated sample and in each of the three blanks.
VALIDATED by V-e (§15.5.7): an assertion that the two compared arms' blank thresholds were derived
           by this rule and no other.
```

**Why the keep-fraction transfer and not the cut-value transfer.** Review lens 4 preferred
transferring the cut VALUE, on the ground that a cut is a stored float64 order statistic and needs
no re-derivation. That is true for the `> cut` arms — and the cut-value transfer **does not exist
for C2**, whose threshold is a COUNT `k` and never a score cut. A rule that transfers a cut for B1
and something else for C2 compares the two arms at two different kinds of threshold, which is
precisely QC-02's finding ("matched the rule, did not match the aggressiveness") reintroduced at
the blank end of the comparison. The keep-fraction transfer is exactly attainable in **every** arm,
is arm-neutral, is in the same count coordinates as the rest of §15.5, and reduces to EXEC-001's
own operating point when phi is B1's realised keep fraction at PCT = 95. That is why it is chosen,
and the two rejected alternatives are named above so no executor resolves this at the keyboard.

This is the change MAJ-04 required. The iteration-6 draft matched the **mean** of R over the three
0.1% replicates while the statistic is the **min** over them. B1's per-replicate R at 0.1% on
HCC1395 is 0.488889 / 0.714286 / 0.622222 — a spread of 0.225, more than twice T's spacing — and
the replicate carrying the minimum `lo` is rep1 (z_lo +2.2995), the **lowest**-retention replicate
(`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`). Matching the mean therefore left the arms mismatched by up to
0.2 in realised retention at the single replicate that decides the outcome. Matching per replicate
on the integer count removes that entirely.

**Why a sample-specific threshold is a narrowing change and not a loosening one.** It makes the
match tighter and it removes a degree of freedom rather than adding one, because the threshold is
not chosen — it is the unique canonical ladder point realising an integer determined by `tau` and
by `scorable_ALT(r)`. Within each z computation, one nominal rule still applies to every sample of
the individual, exactly as v1.0 §10 requires. At `tau` equal to B1's realised operating retention
the construction reduces to v1.0 exactly.

**AXIS-B, stated exactly.** The matched quantity is the blank triple, which enters every
replicate's z identically, so a single threshold rule per arm per individual suffices:

```text
n_tau_B = round( tau * mean over TF0 rep1-3 of scorable_ALT )        [ a target for a MEAN ]

ATTAINABILITY AND TIE-BREAKING FOR A MEAN (NEW at iteration 8; the iteration-7 text left all
three undefined, and a mean of three integers need not be attainable at all):
  1. The arm is placed at ONE global keep fraction phi, realised in each of the three blanks by
     the canonical rule of §15.5.2. The AXIS-B attainable set is the set of phi values attainable
     in ALL THREE blanks.
  2. m(phi) = ( kept_ALT(A, b1, phi) + kept_ALT(A, b2, phi) + kept_ALT(A, b3, phi) ) / 3, a
     multiple of 1/3, NOT necessarily an integer.
  3. The canonical phi for the target is the LARGEST attainable phi minimising | m(phi) - n_tau_B |.
     Ties in that minimum are broken to the SMALLER m(phi), i.e. to the more conservative arm.
  4. TOLERANCE FOR A MEAN, stated because §15.5.7 step 3's one-read tolerance is defined for
     integer counts and not for a mean: the target is evaluated only if
         | m(phi) - n_tau_B | <= 1/3 read   for BOTH compared arms
     — that is, the two arms' blank triples differ from the target by at most ONE read in TOTAL
     across the three blanks. Otherwise the target is TARGET_UNREACHABLE for that leg, never a
     pass and never a refutation.
  5. Additionally require, per blank replicate b:
         | kept_ALT(B1, b) - kept_ALT(comparator, b) |  <=  1 read
     otherwise the target returns UNDECIDABLE for that leg (§15.5.7 step 3).
  6. If NO attainable phi satisfies 4 and 5, the target is TARGET_UNREACHABLE. Never a pass.
All three per-blank counts, m(phi), phi and the realised | m(phi) - n_tau_B | are emitted (§22.2).
```

**The blank set, stated normatively, because two different ones are in play.** AXIS-B uses **TF0
rep1, rep2, rep3** — the three blanks that enter `z_interval` under v1.0 §15, which cannot change.
`retention_ratio` in §16.2 is a different quantity and is computed by `aggregate.py`'s
`ret_of(arm, 'TF0')` over **all five** TF0 replicates; that is what EXEC-001 actually did and what
F11 requires EXEC-002 to reproduce (HCC1395 B1 `retention_blank` 0.363463 = mean of 10/25, 8/22,
8/20, 8/21, 6/22; COLO829 0.092428 = mean of 2/33, 0/20, 4/30, 3/31, 6/35). **S5 and S6 are
therefore anchored on different blank sets, deliberately, and §16.2 requires S5 to hold under
BOTH conventions** so the convention is not a free variable.

**Why both axes, and why neither alone would do.** On AXIS-L the arms keep the same number of ALT
reads in the evaluated sample, so a differential filter reveals itself by keeping *fewer* in the
blank. On AXIS-B the arms keep the same number in the blank, so a differential filter reveals
itself by keeping *more* at 0.1%. Each axis holds one end of the differential fixed. Requiring
dominance on both is strictly harder than requiring either.

**This is not outcome matching.** Review_001's B001 removed a control matched on B1's *answer*.
Realised ALT retention is a property of how aggressive the filter was, measured before any z is
formed, and it is the quantity QC-02 names. No arm is tuned to reproduce another arm's verdict.

**The algebraic reduction on AXIS-L under V2, stated rather than left to be found (MAJ-4).** On
AXIS-L the kept-ALT count `G = n_tau(r)` is matched across arms by construction, and V2 sets
`sample_sd = sqrt(mean_blank)`. Therefore

```text
lo(r) = [ n_tau(r) - mb ] / sqrt(mb)  -  t95(2) * sd_b / sqrt(3) / sqrt(mb)
```

is, with `n_tau(r)` fixed, **a function of the arm's three blank counts alone**. At the operating
point those triples are 10 / 8 / 8 (B1) and 1 / 1 / 1 (C2) on HCC1395, and 2 / 0 / 4 (B1) and
0 / 1 / 0 (C2) on COLO829 (`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`). So on AXIS-L under V2 leg (i) is a
monotone re-expression of the differential-retention comparison QC-01 already measured, computed on
integers between 0 and 10. §20.3's reason 3 ("a filter could have the larger ratio and the worse
zlo_min") is true in general and **false there**.

**THE SAME REDUCTION HOLDS UNDER V1, AND THE SAFEGUARD IS THEREFORE APPLIED SYMMETRICALLY
(resolves MAJ-L1-04).** The iteration-7 draft confined the safeguard to AXIS-L x V2 — "in this one
cell" — and the panel is right that its own measurement refutes the restriction. Under V1
`sample_sd` is the plug-in binomial `sqrt(sum over candidates of dep*p*(1-p))` with ALT counts of
1 or 2 against depth ~25x, and §15.5.9 records `sample_sd/sqrt(G)` at **0.97 +- 0.02** across B1
and C2 rows. With `G` matched to `n_tau` by construction, `sample_sd` is then fixed to within a
few percent across arms, so `lo = (n_tau - mb)/sample_sd - t95 * sd_b / sqrt(3) / sample_sd` is,
to that accuracy, a function of the arm's three blank counts under V1 as well. Confining the
safeguard to V2 was an asymmetry in the PERMISSIVE direction and it is removed.

Consequences, all binding:

```text
- §15.5.6's blank floor is set so that sqrt(mean_blank) is never estimated from fewer than five
  kept blank reads spread over three NON-EMPTY replicates.
- `decided_by` IS COMPUTED UNDER BOTH CONSTRUCTIONS, FROM THE REALISED QUANTITIES, BY THIS RULE,
  FIXED HERE BEFORE EXEC-002 AND FROZEN BY §20.8 CLAUSE 3:
      a row is marked BLANK_TRIPLE iff
          the two arms' matched kept-ALT counts agree to within the §15.5.7 count tolerance
      AND | sample_sd(B1) - sample_sd(comp) | / max(sample_sd(B1), sample_sd(comp))
                                                   <= SAMPLE_SD_TOL = 0.05
      otherwise MATCHED_COUNT.
  SAMPLE_SD_TOL = 0.05 is the natural constant from the 0.97 +- 0.02 measurement: two arms whose
  sample_sd agree within 5% cannot produce a leg-(i) difference that is not a blank-count
  difference.
- **On an individual, if EVERY DISCRIMINATING target carrying a positive leg-(i) result is marked
  BLANK_TRIPLE under EITHER construction, S6 returns UNDECIDABLE on that individual** (§20.4).
  The iteration-7 clause named AXIS-L x V2 only; it now names the determination, not the cell.
- ADVANCE STATEMENT, required by MAJ-L1-04 so the consequence is not discovered afterwards: it is
  possible that applying this rule symmetrically leaves AXIS-L unable to carry a positive leg-(i)
  result AT ALL, on either construction. §15.5.9 states that as a further operating characteristic
  and simulates it. If that is what EXEC-002 finds, the correct report is that AXIS-L was
  uninformative for leg (i) — not that AXIS-L's V1 cells carried the verdict.
```

#### 15.5.5 The curves, the replicate aggregation, and the stochastic arms

```text
Z(A, i, v, tau) is computed EXACTLY as §15.4 defines zlo_min, with the per-replicate matching of
§15.5.4, using the checksummed mrdz.score functions (§23). Reimplementation is forbidden.
```

Replicate aggregation is v1.0's and is **not** changed: **minimum** over the three 0.1%
replicates for `zlo_min`; the three primary blanks for the reference. What changes is that the
arms are now matched at each replicate individually, so the min is taken over matched comparisons.

**Undefined Z, resolved in ONE direction (MAJ-8 e).** The iteration-6 draft said in §15.5.5 that an
undefined Z "is a failure to demonstrate at that grid point and never a pass", and in §15.5.6 that
the same condition excludes the target as `EXCLUDED_UNDERPOWERED`. Those route to opposite
verdicts. **The protocol resolves it here: a `Z` that is undefined because of counts —
`sample_sd = 0`, `mean_blank = 0`, an empty replicate, or a `nan` lower bound — makes the target
`EXCLUDED_UNDERPOWERED` for that leg, with the counts recorded. It is never a pass and never a
refutation, because an undefined statistic cannot refute.** v1.0 §15's rule that an undefined
`zlo_min` is a failure to demonstrate for **S1** is untouched and still binds at the operating
point; this clause governs only S6's targets. The case is live, not hypothetical: COLO829 TF0
rep2 already has `alt_kept = 0`, `sample_sd = 0` and verdict `no estimate` at PCT = 95.

**Zero-spread blank triple (MAJ-8 b).** `blank_sd` over three integer counts is frequently exactly
0, and when it is, the interval half-width is 0 and `lo` jumps discontinuously up to `z`. EXEC-001
already contains an instance (`per_sample_metrics.tsv`, HCC1395 TF0 rep1 C1 V1: `blank_sd = 0.0`,
`z_lo = z_hi = 0.0`). **A target at which either compared arm has `blank_sd = 0` returns
UNDECIDABLE for that leg**, flagged `zero_spread_blank`, and `blank_sd` is a column of both sweep
artifacts.

**Stochastic arms C1 and C3 — PER-DRAW matching is MANDATED (MAJ-06).** One *draw* is a complete
re-run of the arm across all of that individual's samples under one draw index and seed (v1.0
§10). **B = 2000 draws for the POST HOC bands** (§15.5.8); C1 additionally emits its PRESERVED
200-draw aggregation, which is what S1, S4 clause (ii) and F8 read (§15.5.8 b, §20.1, MAJ-L2-01).

```text
Each draw has its own score assignment and therefore its own ladder and its own attainable
count set. Each draw is matched to tau ON ITS OWN LADDER, independently, by the rule of §15.5.4.
Each draw yields its own Z. The band is the 5th and 95th percentile of the PER-DRAW Z.
The count floor of §15.5.6 is applied PER DRAW. `n_draws_undefined` and `n_draws_floored` are
reported per target; if more than 20% of draws are excluded the band is INADMISSIBLE and the
leg returns UNDECIDABLE at that target (§15.5.8 c).
```

The alternative reading — locating `tau` on the median curve and taking the percentile of Z at
fixed ladder points — is **forbidden**. It folds none of the retention variability into the band
and would lower the bar B1 must clear. Per-draw matching is the conservative reading and is the
one this protocol requires.

#### 15.5.6 Count floor (binds QC-04; ONE floor governs both reporting and evaluation)

A target `tau` is **evaluable** for individual `i`, on axis `X`, **for a given leg** — the floor is
applied **per leg**, against that leg's own pair of compared arms, because S6's four legs have four
different comparators:

```text
(a) n_tau >= 5 kept ALT reads at 0.1% for both arms in that leg;
(b) for both arms in that leg: ALL THREE blank replicates have kept_ALT >= 1, AND the three
    blank counts SUM to at least 5.
    WHICH TRIPLE (minor finding adopted; the iteration-7 text left this open and at COLO829's B1
    blank counts of 2 / 0 / 4 it is the difference between evaluable and not): on AXIS-L the
    threshold transfer of §15.5.4 induces a DIFFERENT blank triple for each of the three 0.1%
    replicates, so there are THREE induced triples per arm per individual. **Clause (b) must hold
    for ALL THREE induced triples**, not for one of them and not for the triple belonging to the
    argmin replicate. On AXIS-B there is one triple per arm and clause (b) applies to it directly;
(c) Z is defined (not nan) for both arms under the construction being evaluated;
(d) blank_sd > 0 for both arms (§15.5.5);
(e) the count match of §15.5.7 step 3 succeeds for both arms;
(f) the bootstrap is ADMISSIBLE for both arms at that target: `n_boot_undefined` at or below the
    20% ceiling and `argmin_unstable_frac` at or below ARGMIN_UNSTABLE_MAX = 0.20 (§15.5.8);
(g) for a leg whose comparator is a member of P: the comparator is INFORMATIVE at that sample by
    §16.7 (c)'s RHO_MIN rule, and is not flagged MAPQ_DEGENERATE or NM-uncomputable (§10.2).
```

Clause (b) replaces the iteration-6 rule "mean kept_ALT over TF0 rep1-3 >= 1". A mean of 1 is
satisfied by 0 / 0 / 3, which gives a defined V2 `sample_sd` resting on one informative replicate;
and EXEC-001's COLO829 B1 blank totals are literally **2 / 0 / 4**, so the pathology is present in
the data at the operating point. Clause (b) also sets the SAME five-read bar that §16.5 uses as its
display band, so the protocol no longer refuses to print a descriptive ratio on four blank reads
while withdrawing a mechanism claim on one.

A target failing any of (a)-(e) is recorded as `EXCLUDED_UNDERPOWERED` with the counts that
excluded it, **for that leg**. **Exclusion is never a pass and never a refutation.** If fewer than
**3** targets survive in T for an individual on either axis, for the legs that carry the mechanism
claim, the mechanism verdict for that individual is `MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE` (§20.7), not `HELD`.

#### 15.5.7 Comparison at matched COUNT, and the required validations

For each axis `X`, each target `tau` in T, each individual, each construction, each leg:

```text
1. n_tau is the integer of §15.5.4. NO INTERPOLATION OF Z IS PERFORMED, AT ANY POINT, FOR ANY
   PURPOSE. `Z` is a step function of integer counts — at kept counts of 5 to 9 one read moves
   `z` by roughly 0.5 to 1.0 — and a linear interpolation across a retention gap is not an
   estimate of Z at tau. The iteration-6 interpolated and nearest-point estimates, their
   admissibility condition and their agreement rule are all DELETED.

2. Each arm is placed at its canonical ladder point realising kept_ALT = n_tau EXACTLY, in each
   matched sample. The realised count is recorded, not assumed.

3. WHERE n_tau IS NOT ATTAINABLE for an arm in a matched sample: use the nearest attainable count
   n' (ties broken to the LOWER count, deterministically), and
       if | n' - n_tau | <= 1 read for BOTH compared arms, the target is evaluated and the
          realised counts and their signed difference are recorded;
       otherwise the target returns UNDECIDABLE for that leg, recorded as TARGET_UNREACHABLE with
       the arm's attainable range [n_min, n_max] and the nearest attainable counts.
   TARGET_UNREACHABLE is never a pass and never a refutation, and it counts against §15.5.6's
   three-evaluable-target floor as an exclusion.

4. Every row of matched_retention_comparison.tsv carries, for both compared arms: the ladder
   index, the realised cut or k, the REALISED GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION in the evaluated sample and in
   each blank (§15.5.4), retention, kept_alt, the signed count difference, the three per-replicate
   kept counts, the three per-replicate `lo` values, the three per-replicate paired differences,
   and argmin_replicate. v1.0 §17 already required the per-replicate paired differences; the
   iteration-6 schema dropped them.

5. THE ARGMIN DIAGNOSTIC IS GIVEN A RULE (minor finding adopted). `Z(A,i,v,tau) = min over r of
   lo(r)`, so the paired difference `D` is a difference of two MINIMA and the two minima may come
   from DIFFERENT replicates. Where `argmin_replicate_B1 != argmin_replicate_comp`, the row is
   flagged `ARGMIN_SPLIT` and **the three per-replicate paired differences must be printed beside
   that leg's verdict, in the same block**, wherever the verdict is stated. A leg verdict quoted
   without them, on an ARGMIN_SPLIT row, is non-compliant with this protocol.
```

**Required validations, before any comparison is read.** These check the machinery, not the
hypothesis.

```text
V-a  RECONSTRUCTION IDENTITY (the licence for the re-run).
     For every sample, and for EVERY gate-passing candidate, the kept-ALT count recomputed at the
     PCT = 95 ladder point from the stored arrays must equal the stored EXEC-002 value, AND the
     stored EXEC-001 value:
         from score_b1_num[] / score_c2[] against the v[i95] cut, `>` strict   -> kept_B1
         from c2_order[] against k95                                          -> kept_C2
     ZERO mismatches are permitted, at the per-candidate level, before any curve is read.
     Reconstructing K_B1 and k_C2 at PCT = 95 must also reproduce EXEC-001's stored values
     exactly. A mismatch is §21 F13.
     The iteration-6 draft's V-a compared only aggregate zlo_min values, which the unmodified
     code path reproduces automatically and which therefore tests the BAM pass, not the sweep.
     That check is RETAINED as a second, weaker one: zlo_min(B1) = +2.2995 and zlo_min(B0) =
     +0.7396 on HCC1395 under V1, to 4 decimal places.

V-b  MONOTONICITY. kept_ALT must be monotone non-decreasing along the ladder, in every arm and
     sample (§10.3). Violation is an implementation fault (§21 F10).

V-c  HIGH-RETENTION ANCHOR, restated correctly (X7).
     B0's literal retention is 1.19 to 1.67 because its numerator includes unscorable ALT reads
     (deviation D3), and R = 1 is unattainable for the `> cut` arms because the strict rule always
     drops the minimum-score reads, and for C2 it needs k = n_scorable. So the iteration-6 V-c was
     both false and unreachable.
     The anchor is B0_scorable: G = the scorable ALT reads on gate-passing candidates,
     retention_scorable_only = 1.0 by construction. Its zlo_min is computed once per individual
     per construction. Each arm's curve must converge on THAT value as kept_ALT approaches the
     arm's own attainable maximum n_max, which is reported. Divergence at n_max is an
     implementation fault (§21 F10).

V-d  C1 LEVEL-INDEPENDENCE. C1's realised ALT retention must track its nominal keep fraction
     within its own 5-95 band at every ladder point — the property F8 already checks at PCT = 95.
     F8's band is read from the PRESERVED 200-draw vector j = 0..199 (§20.2, §23.2); V-d's
     ladder-wide check uses the 2000-draw vector and is post hoc. Violation is an implementation
     fault (§21 F10).

V-e  THRESHOLD-TRANSFER IDENTITY (NEW at iteration 8; required by MAJ-L1-03).
     For every row of matched_retention_comparison.tsv, assert that BOTH compared arms' blank
     thresholds were derived by the GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION transfer of §15.5.4 and by no other rule:
         for each arm A and each blank b, the recorded ladder point is the canonical point for
         phi(A, r) in b, and the recorded realised keep fraction satisfies
         | K_realised(b) - round(phi * n_scorable(b)) | <= KEEP_COUNT_TOL * n_scorable(b).
     A row failing this is not evaluated and the failure is an implementation fault (§21 F10).
     This exists because on AXIS-L the whole leg is decided by the blank counts the transfer
     produces, so "the executor used the rule" must be CHECKED, not assumed.

V-f  INPUT REPRODUCTION (NEW at iteration 8; required by X-01).
     Before any curve is read, the per-sample quantities that do not depend on the sweep —
     n_consensus_positions, n_confident_positions, n_scorable_reads, K_B1, k_C2, cut, and every
     per-candidate depth / alt_total / alt_unscorable / alt_scorable_idx / kept_B1 / kept_C2 row —
     must reproduce EXEC-001's stored values EXACTLY from the CRAM inputs of §6.1. This is the
     strongest available check that the 2026-09-12 conversion was lossless for this benchmark's
     purposes, since EV-0052 records that per-file md5_sam_body was never recomputed. A failure
     routes to §21 F11's INPUT-FORMAT branch, not to a silent absorption.
```

#### 15.5.8 The nulls, named as what they are

The iteration-6 draft asserted that `B1 > C2` at matched retention "is a contrast between two
deterministic curves; it carries no uncertainty of its own". **That is false and the sentence is
deleted.** `Z` is a deterministic function of the arm's integer read counts, but those counts are
random: at a target the arms hold roughly `tau * 35-63` kept ALT reads per 0.1% replicate and
`tau * 20-33` kept blank reads. §15.5.9 measures the null spread of the paired difference at 0.83
to 3.1 z-units, against a whole observed advantage at the operating point of `delta_vs_C2` = 2.37
(V1) / 3.68 (V2) on HCC1395 and **0.53 (V1) / 1.03 (V2) on COLO829**
(`results/ablation_results.tsv`). COLO829's advantage is under two thirds of one null standard
deviation under V1. A criterion that reads that as a mechanism verdict is not a criterion.

Four devices, all specified numerically.

```text
(a) NULL-0  PAIRED CANDIDATE BOOTSTRAP — the interval for legs (i) and (iv) (resolves MAJ-1;
    fully specified at iteration 8 by MAJ-L1-05, SV-02, SV-03, SV-04, X-04)
    B = 2000 resamples. One resample:
      - draws the GATE-PASSING candidates of every sample of that individual with replacement,
        at the sample's own size, preserving each candidate's full row (depth, alt reads and
        their scorable indices), so the read-to-candidate structure is preserved;
      - draws one C2 tie-break seed index uniformly from 0..199 (§10.6), so seed variation and
        sampling variation enter the SAME interval (and §10.6's decomposition keeps them
        separately readable);
      - recomputes BOTH compared arms at the matched target on that resample, using the
        checksummed mrdz.score functions;
      - records the PAIRED difference  D* = Z(B1, tau) - Z(comparator, tau).
    The arms are resampled TOGETHER, so the pairing v1.0 §17 relies on is preserved.

    WHAT IS FIXED AND WHAT MOVES — NORMATIVE, because the alternatives give materially different
    interval widths and the iteration-7 text admitted both (MAJ-L1-05, X-04):
      FIXED across every resample, and FORBIDDEN to recompute:
        the scorable-read set; the stored per-read score arrays of §22.1; the cut; the LADDER and
        every arm's attainable kept-ALT set; the density strata of §10.1; the DISCRIMINATING
        evaluable TARGET SET over which §20.4's pooled mean is taken, which is fixed ON THE
        OBSERVED DATA so that the pooled statistic is the same functional in every resample.
        (A cut is an order statistic over 58,879-77,039 SCORABLE READS, which are not the
        candidate set, so the ladder is invariant under candidate resampling. An executor who
        rebuilds the ladder from the resampled candidates' ALT reads produces a different
        interval; that is forbidden here so that no rule has to catch it later.)
      MOVES with the resample:
        the gate-passing candidate rows; scorable_ALT(r); and therefore
            n_tau*(r) = round( tau * scorable_ALT*(r) ),  RE-DERIVED FROM THE RESAMPLE.
        Holding n_tau at its observed value is FORBIDDEN. Re-deriving is the conservative
        reading — it puts the MATCHING noise inside the interval — and it matters: scorable_ALT
        at 0.1% is 45/35/45 and 51/55/63 (§15.5.1), so a resample moves it by order 10% and n_tau
        by one to two reads, against §15.5.7 step 1's measured 0.5-1.0 z-units per read.
      RECORDED per resample, and summarised on the row: the realised n_tau*, the realised
        inter-arm kept-ALT count difference, and the realised argmin replicate of each arm.

    UNDEFINED AND INADMISSIBLE RESAMPLES (resolves SV-03; the iteration-7 draft imposed this on
    the permutation bands and left the bootstrap — where the counts are smallest — unguarded):
      A resample is UNDEFINED at a target if any of the following holds for either arm:
        - a ratio denominator is zero (e.g. zero kept blank ALT reads behind retention_ratio);
        - n_tau* is unattainable within §15.5.7 step 3's tolerance for that arm on the FIXED
          ladder;
        - Z is undefined by the §15.5.5 rule (sample_sd = 0, mean_blank = 0, an empty replicate,
          or a nan lower bound).
      UNDEFINED resamples are EXCLUDED and COUNTED. `n_boot_undefined`, `n_boot_floored` and
      `boot_admissible` are columns of matched_retention_comparison.tsv and of every
      retention-ratio row (§16.2, §22.2). If more than 20% of resamples are undefined the interval
      is INADMISSIBLE, the leg or the S5 ratio comparison returns UNDECIDABLE, and the interval
      MAY NOT be reported as a 90% interval — because an interval computed from the resamples in
      which the denominator happened to survive is conditional on a non-random event that is
      correlated with the quantity being estimated.
      ADVANCE STATEMENT: on C2's three-blank counts (HCC1395 0/25, 1/22, 1/20 — two kept reads;
      COLO829 0/33, 1/20, 0/30 — ONE kept read) the single carrying candidate on COLO829 is absent
      from (21/22)^22 = 36% of resamples, so **COLO829's three-blank C2 ratio interval is expected
      to be INADMISSIBLE**. §20.3 states that as an advance prediction.

    ARGMIN STABILITY (resolves SV-04). `Z` is a MIN over three replicates, and the nonparametric
    bootstrap is not consistent for a min when the argmin is not well separated. The separation is
    measurable and small here: COLO829 C2 under V1 has per-replicate lo = 0.1684 / 0.1676 / 2.0485
    (`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`), an 0.0008 z-unit gap between the two candidate argmins.
    REQUIRED COLUMNS: `argmin_unstable_frac_B1`, `argmin_unstable_frac_comp` — the fraction of
    admissible resamples in which that arm's argmin replicate differs from the observed one.
    ADMISSIBILITY: if either exceeds ARGMIN_UNSTABLE_MAX = 0.20, the target returns UNDECIDABLE
    for that leg, because the bootstrap is not estimating a fixed functional there.

    null_sd — DEFINED NORMATIVELY, because the iteration-7 draft divided the deciding statistic by
    a quantity it never defined (resolves SV-02):
        null_sd*      = the sd of the paired difference computed INSIDE each resample (an inner
                        studentising estimate). This is the estimator used in every STANDARDISED
                        quantity: §20.4's pooled mean of D*/null_sd and §15.5.8 (d)'s family-wise
                        statistic. Studentising inside the resample is what makes the percentile
                        interval approximately second-order accurate, which is why this reading and
                        not the others is chosen.
        null_sd_obs   = the bootstrap sd of D* over the 2000 admissible resamples on the OBSERVED
                        data. Used for the reported POINT statistic and for display only.
      The other two readings — holding null_sd* at null_sd_obs, and importing §15.5.9's simulated
      sd as a constant — are FORBIDDEN and named as forbidden. `null_sd` is added to §1.1's frozen
      register and to §20.8 clause 3.
      **null_sd is a BOOTSTRAP sd of a paired difference. It is NOT a null-model sd.** §15.5.9's
      simulated quantity is renamed `sim_sd_D` so the two cannot be silently treated as one object.

    DECISION RULE at a target:
        leg HOLDS   iff  the EMPIRICAL 5th percentile of D* over admissible resamples > 0
        leg FAILS   iff  the EMPIRICAL 95th percentile of D* < 0
        otherwise   UNDECIDABLE — never the more favourable reading, and never REFUTED
    The decision is on the EMPIRICAL percentile, not on a symmetric 1.645*sd proxy; §15.5.9 must
    simulate the empirical-percentile rule, because the two differ materially for a min-of-min
    functional at kept counts of 5 to 9 (SV-04).
    `delta_point`, `delta_lo`, `delta_hi`, `null_sd_obs`, `n_boot_undefined`, `boot_admissible`,
    `boot_mcse_delta_lo`, `boot_mcse_delta_hi`, `argmin_unstable_frac_B1` and
    `argmin_unstable_frac_comp` are columns of matched_retention_comparison.tsv.

    WHAT THIS RULE IS AND IS NOT STRICTER THAN. The iteration-7 draft asserted "This is strictly
    harder than the point comparison it replaces, in BOTH directions." **That sentence is
    DELETED**: a percentile bootstrap that is too NARROW is EASIER in the support direction, not
    harder, and no proof of coverage was offered. What can be said, and is said instead: requiring
    the 5th percentile of D* to exceed 0 is strictly harder than requiring the point difference to
    exceed 0 WHENEVER the interval has non-negative width, which it does by construction; and
    §15.5.9 RESULT 1 and RESULT 3 report the realised size and power OF THIS RULE AS IMPLEMENTED,
    rather than of a proxy, so the reader is given the operating characteristics instead of an
    adjective.

(b) NULL-1  C1's permutation band — leg (iii)
(c) NULL-2  C3's permutation band — leg (ii)
    THE C1 DRAW SPLIT, NORMATIVE (resolves MAJ-L2-01). Raising C1's draw count changes the MEDIAN
    that three PREREGISTERED objects read — S1's C1 term, S4 clause (ii)'s noise band, and F8's
    band — which is the sideways move qc_001.md rule 1 forbids, and it would deterministically
    fire F11 on a correct run. Therefore:
        DRAWS j = 0..199    are the PRESERVED vector. S1, S4 clause (ii), F8 and every F11-checked
                            C1 column are computed from these and ONLY these, reproducing
                            EXEC-001's 200-draw aggregation bit for bit. Columns carrying them are
                            suffixed `_200` (e.g. zlo_min_c1_200, retention_ratio_p05_200).
        DRAWS j = 0..1999   are the POST HOC vector, used ONLY by S5 leg (c), S6 leg (iii) and the
                            permutation nulls of this section. Columns suffixed `_2000`.
    No artifact may carry an unsuffixed C1 band column. §20.1, §20.2, §21 F8, §21 F11 and §23.2
    restate this where they are read.
    Both are ONE-SIDED PERMUTATION TESTS at alpha = 0.05 with B = 2000 draws, and the protocol
    calls them that (resolves MAJ-7 a). The iteration-6 draft thresholded B1 against the 95th
    percentile of 200 draws while declaring the exceedance fraction "descriptive" and "not a
    p-value". Relabelling a decision device does not change what it is.
    EXACTNESS IS CONDITIONAL and is stated: the test is exact only under the null that the
    permuted quantity carries no information given what the permutation preserves — for C3, that
    methylation PATTERN carries nothing given CpG-density stratum. It is NOT a test against the
    read-quality null; that is what legs (i) and (iv) and §16.7 are for.
    TIES (resolves MAJ-7 b): Z takes few distinct values at these counts. If Z(B1, tau) EQUALS
    the band's 95th percentile, the leg returns UNDECIDABLE at that target. It is never a pass and
    never a failure.
    UNDEFINED DRAWS (resolves MAJ-7 c): `aggregate.py` currently drops `NA` draws silently, so the
    band is conditioned on non-degenerate draws — EXEC-001's HCC1395 C1 V1 band already shows the
    contamination (`zlo_min_p05` = -6.0491 with `retention_ratio_p05` = 0.0). EXEC-002 must report
    `n_draws_undefined` and `n_draws_floored` per target and state that the band is conditional on
    the remainder. If more than 20% of draws are excluded the band is INADMISSIBLE and the leg
    returns UNDECIDABLE at that target.
    B = 2000 (resolves MAJ-7 d): at B = 200 the 95th percentile rests on the 190th/191st order
    statistics. The Monte Carlo standard error of the reported percentile is computed and
    reported per target (`band_p95_mcse`).

(d) FAMILY-WISE CONTROL IN THE REFUTATION DIRECTION (resolves MAJ-3 second half; REBUILT at
    iteration 8 because the iteration-7 construction could not fire — resolves SV-01)
    S6 is read at many targets. §15.5.9 measures that under exact equivalence some target
    "refutes" at a per-target alpha of 0.05 with probability 0.17. A refutation must therefore
    clear a family-wise bar.

    WHAT WAS WRONG, stated so it cannot recur. The iteration-7 rule read: "the null distribution
    of min over targets of D*/null_sd is directly available [from the bootstrap of (a)]. A leg is
    REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE at a target only if its observed D/null_sd falls below the 5th percentile
    of that minimum statistic." **A bootstrap resamples the OBSERVED data; its distribution is
    centred on the observed effect, not on zero.** Writing M_obs for the observed min and M* for
    the bootstrap min, M* is centred near M_obs, so q05(M*) is approximately M_obs - 1.645*se(M),
    which lies BELOW M_obs. The target attaining the observed minimum has D/null_sd = M_obs
    exactly and therefore fails the condition BY CONSTRUCTION, and every other target fails it a
    fortiori. The rule could not fire. Since REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE is the sole gate on L-C, L-D,
    L-E, L-F and F12, every refutation label in the protocol was structurally unreachable, and an
    outcome in which the density control beat B1 outright at matched count — the exact outcome
    QC-02 asked the sweep to look for — would have been reported as "this design could not resolve
    it". That is a benchmark whose strongest negative outcome cannot be reached, which the binding
    scientific rules forbid.

    THE RULE, REBUILT ON A CENTRED REFERENCE:
        The bootstrap of (a) is JOINT across targets — one resample recomputes EVERY target of
        that leg, on the FIXED observed target set. For each admissible resample form the
        CENTRED, STUDENTISED per-target quantity
            W*(t) = ( D*(t) - D_obs(t) ) / null_sd*(t)
        which is a draw under the hypothesis of NO DIFFERENCE at every target, and take
            M* = min over evaluable DISCRIMINATING targets t of W*(t).
        The observed statistic is  M_obs = min over the same targets of  D_obs(t) / null_sd_obs(t).
        A leg is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE at a target only if
            M_obs  <  the 5th percentile of M*   AND   that target attains M_obs.
        A failure that does not clear the family-wise bar is UNDECIDABLE, not REFUTED.
    The equivalent construction by within-sample randomisation — exchanging the two arms' kept-set
    labels at matched count — is permitted as a CHECK and its agreement reported, but the centred
    bootstrap above is the normative rule.
    OPERATING CHARACTERISTICS REQUIRED: §15.5.9 RESULT 4 reports this rule's SIZE under exact
    equivalence and its POWER against the 1.5x and 2x alternatives, on the same 20,000-simulation
    model as RESULT 2 and RESULT 3. **A refutation rule with no stated operating characteristics
    may not decide a verdict in this document**, for the reason §15.5.9's own opening sentence
    gives.
```

**What v1.0 §17 forbids, and what it does not.** v1.0 §17 is carried over and binding: **no
p-value may be computed on the three pseudo-replicates**, because they are subsamples of one
library and their spread understates between-library spread by more than an order of magnitude
(FIND-0018, EV-0038). Nothing above does that. The bootstrap resamples **candidates**, and the
permutation is over **reads within a sample**. Neither repairs the pseudo-replication, and neither
claims to: §15.5.10 is the required sensitivity analysis for that, and §24 item 17 records the
limit. `statistical_tests.tsv` carries `method NONE` on v1.0's rows unchanged and the named
methods on the v1.1 rows (§17).

> The phrase "a preregistered-in-this-document consistency rule" appeared here in iteration 6.
> It is deleted. The correct statement is: **pre-specified in this POST HOC protocol before
> EXEC-002 runs, and NOT preregistered with respect to the EXEC-001 result.**

#### 15.5.9 Operating characteristics of S6, computed BEFORE EXEC-002 (resolves MAJ-3)

An experiment whose outcome is determined by its read counts rather than by the biology cannot
support either verdict it names. The panel required these figures in the protocol. They are
computed here, from EXEC-001's own counts, before EXEC-002 runs.

```text
MODEL, stated so it can be checked or disputed:
  - kept ALT at 0.1% matched EXACTLY between arms: G(r) = round(tau * scorable_ALT(r)), using
    scorable_ALT = 45/35/45 (HCC1395) and 51/55/63 (COLO829)
  - blank kept ALT: one latent uniform per scorable blank ALT read per arm, thresholded at
    q = tau / ratio, over scorable_ALT_blank = 25/22/20 and 33/20/30. Every target is therefore
    a RE-READING OF THE SAME READS at a different threshold, which is the real dependence
    structure; the targets are NOT independent replications.
  - V1 sample_sd = sqrt(G). EXEC-001 measures sample_sd/sqrt(G) at 0.97 +- 0.02 across B1 and C2
    rows, so this is a 3% approximation.
  - V2 sample_sd = sqrt(mean_blank);  t95(df=2) = 4.302653;  lo as in mrdz.z_interval
  - the arms' blank draws are independent. EXEC-001 shows their kept blank sets are largely
    disjoint at the operating point (B1 keeps 2/0/4 where C2 keeps 0/1/0 on COLO829), so this
    is close to the truth and errs toward a WIDER null.
  - the §15.5.6 count floor is applied, AND §15.5.11's REGION FILTER is applied exactly as it
    will be at evaluation, so the tabulated size is the size of the rule §20.4 installs and not
    of a rule without the filter (minor finding adopted; the iteration-7 simulation omitted it,
    and DEGENERATE_HIGH removal from a POOLED mean mechanically raises the pooled statistic)
  - DECISION RULE SIMULATED: THE RULE AS INSTALLED, not a proxy (resolves SV-04). Each simulation
    replicate draws the per-replicate counts, forms `min over r of lo(r)` for both arms, runs the
    2000-resample percentile bootstrap of §15.5.8 (a) inside itself with n_tau re-derived per
    resample, and applies the EMPIRICAL 5th-percentile rule. The iteration-7 tables were computed
    for the proxy "D > 1.645 * sim_sd_D", which is not the rule that decides; for a min-of-min
    functional at kept counts of 5 to 9 the empirical percentile of a left-skewed mixture is not
    the symmetric-normal percentile. The tables printed here are the simulated-ACTUAL ones.
  - 20,000 simulations per cell.
  - `sim_sd_D` is the simulated sd of the paired difference. It is NOT the executor's `null_sd`
    (§15.5.8 a) and is renamed here so the two cannot be conflated (SV-02).
  - BLANK CORRELATION: two models are simulated and BOTH are printed (resolves SV-05).
      MODEL-I   three INDEPENDENT blank replicates, as the iteration-7 draft assumed.
      MODEL-C   the CORRELATED-BLANK model: effective blank count 1, i.e. the three TF0 blank
                draws are perfectly dependent. EV-0045 measures the three TF0 replicates sharing
                ~90% of their normal reads on HCC1395 and ~45% on COLO829, and §15.5.10 quotes
                EV-0036's 11.7-fold understatement of between-library spread, so MODEL-I is the
                assumption the document's own evidence refutes. **MODEL-C's figures are THE
                DESIGN'S STATED SIZE AND POWER.** MODEL-I is printed beside them for continuity
                with the iteration-7 tables.
      Each model is additionally run at the sd-inflation factor f = 11.7 of §15.5.10, so the
      design has stated operating characteristics under its own admitted variance model.
  - the T95 CONSTANT: this simulation uses the exact quantile 4.302652729696142, while the
    normative implementation uses a truncated table (`mrdz/score.py` `_T95`, keyed by the number
    of blanks, so n = 3 gives 4.303). The difference is immaterial to the figures below. It is
    stated here so that a fourth-decimal disagreement between EXEC-002's re-run and this table is
    NOT reported as the "material disagreement" the next bullet means (minor finding adopted).
  - the script that produces this table, with its seed and every constant, is
    `protocols/support/s6_operating_characteristics.py`. It is part of this protocol and is
    checksummed with it. **The FULL 28-cell table, both blank models, both inflation factors, and
    RESULT 4 and RESULT 5, are produced by that script from its recorded seed; the script plus its
    seed — not the abridged printout below — is the FROZEN BASIS §20.8 clause 7 refers to**
    (minor finding adopted). EXEC-002 re-runs it on the REALISED counts and writes
    results_v1_1/s6_operating_characteristics.tsv; a material disagreement with the table below
    is a finding to report, not a reason to re-choose the rule (§20.8 clause 7).

RESULT 1 — per-target size and power, leg (i), AXIS-L, THE RULE AS INSTALLED
(empirical 5th-percentile bootstrap rule; region filter applied; n_tau re-derived per resample;
 NSIM = 2500 outer realisations, B_inner = 300; seed 20260919; f = 1 shown, f = 11.7 below)

      bootstrap distribution narrower than the sampling distribution. EXEC-002 runs the REAL
      per-candidate bootstrap and recomputes RESULT 4 on its realised counts; if the realised size
      is materially different, that is the figure to report, and it is a finding, not a reason to
      re-choose the rule (§20.8 clause 7).

**What these numbers force, and it is a design change, not a comment.**

1. **The iteration-6 S6 was not a criterion, and the iteration-8 measurement confirms it on the
   rule as installed.** The all-target intersection (RESULT 2, f = 1) holds with probability 0.067
   to 0.111 under MODEL-I against a *real* 1.5x advantage, while producing a target-level failure
   somewhere with probability 0.213 to 0.322 under *exact equivalence* — and under MODEL-C the
   spurious-failure rate is 0.577 to 0.580. It returns a non-success under the null and under a
   genuine modest effect alike. The charge that the iteration-6 draft had built a benchmark in
   which essentially every outcome reads as refutation stands.
2. **S6's verdict-bearing rule is therefore the POOLED statistic** (§20.4). **Its measured size is
   NOT 0.05.** RESULT 3, f = 1: size 0.093-0.182 under MODEL-I and 0.269-0.343 under MODEL-C,
   against power of 0.457-0.589 (MODEL-I) and 0.608-0.778 (MODEL-C) at 1.5x, and 0.784-0.870 and
   0.865-0.954 at 2x. Power exceeds size everywhere, so the rule is informative; but a HOLD from a
   rule whose size is 0.27-0.34 under the design's own blank model is WEAK EVIDENCE, and the
   iteration-7 draft's "size 0.05" came from a symmetric-normal proxy under three independent
   blanks, neither of which is what this protocol installs.
   **BINDING, fixed here before EXEC-002:** every statement of an S6 leg HOLDING must print, in
   the same block, RESULT 3's MEASURED SIZE for that cell under MODEL-C, and the sentence that the
   design's stated size is the MODEL-C figure (SV-05). §22.2 carries `rule_size_model_c` as a
   required column on every row bearing a leg verdict, filled from EXEC-002's own re-run of
   `s6_operating_characteristics.py` on its realised counts.
3. **The refutation direction is now family-wise controlled BY A RULE THAT CAN FIRE** (§15.5.8 d).
   The iteration-7 rule could not fire at all (SV-01). The rebuilt centred rule fires, with power
   above size in every cell, but its measured size is 0.158-0.417 rather than 0.05 (RESULT 4), so
   refutation labels are SIZE-QUALIFIED under RESULT 4's binding consequences. The iteration-7
   claim that (d) "removes the 0.17" is withdrawn: what (d) does is give the refutation direction
   a reference distribution that is centred, and a measured size that the reader can see.
4. **This recalibration is between two POST HOC drafts, not against v1.0.** S6 in every form is an
   additional necessary condition, so v1.1 remains strictly harder than v1.0 (§20.9). It is
   declared here, with the numbers that motivated it, rather than applied quietly.
5. **The resolution limit is stated as a limitation, not hidden** (§24 item 18). For **leg (i),
   AXIS-L, pooled**, at f = 1, this design separates a 2x differential-retention advantage from
   none with power 0.78-0.87 (MODEL-I) or 0.87-0.95 (MODEL-C) per cell, and a 1.5x advantage with
   power 0.46-0.59 or 0.61-0.78 — against sizes of 0.09-0.18 and 0.27-0.34 respectively, so the
   separation is far weaker than those power figures alone suggest. Because S7 requires both
   individuals, the UPPER BOUND on a positive **leg (i), AXIS-L, pooled, both individuals** under
   a 1.5x alternative is 0.23 (MODEL-I) or 0.47 (MODEL-C) — RESULT 5. The iteration-7 figure "of
   order 0.06" came from the symmetric-normal proxy and is SUPERSEDED by these; where it survives
   as a quotation elsewhere in this document it is marked as superseded. **That figure is NOT the power of S6 as installed**, which
   is a 16-fold per-individual conjunction and is UNKNOWN AND LOWER (RESULT 5). **A non-holding S6
   therefore routes to UNDECIDABLE unless the refutation side clears its own family-wise band**
   (§20.7). Reading "the mechanism is withdrawn" off a failure to clear a bar this design cannot
   clear would be as unsupportable as reading a success off it — which is why §20.7 distinguishes
   WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE from WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE.
6. **What EXEC-002 can and cannot establish, with the size stated under the right model.** The
   iteration-7 draft justified the run on the sentence "the size is controlled at 0.05 in both
   directions". **That sentence is REMOVED** (SV-05): 0.05 was the MODEL-I, f = 1,
   three-independent-blanks figure, and §15.5.10 and §24 item 17 of this same document say that
   model is wrong by a factor of 11.7 in blank spread and that the effective replicate count is
   nearer one than three. The design's stated size is the **MODEL-C** figure printed above, and
   every S5 and S6 interval is **CONDITIONAL ON THIS BLANK LIBRARY** — the candidate bootstrap
   resamples WITHIN samples and no resampling of a library sequenced once can generate
   between-library variance. What remains true, and is why the run is still worth its budget
   (§24 item 18, restated there without the deleted claim): a detectable alternative exists on the
   2x column and on COLO829's non-differential-C2 column; the refutation direction now has stated
   operating characteristics (RESULT 4) where it previously could not fire at all; and the sweep
   is the only way to address QC-02's question at all. What EXEC-002 may not do is report a
   mechanism verdict its own operating characteristics cannot support.
7. **AXIS-L may be unable to carry a positive leg-(i) result at all**, under EITHER construction,
   because §15.5.4's symmetric BLANK_TRIPLE determination marks a row whenever the arms' matched
   counts and their `sample_sd` agree — which at matched `G` they do to within 3% under V1 and
   exactly under V2. The script reports P(AXIS-L carries a non-BLANK_TRIPLE positive leg (i)).
   **If that probability is near zero, the correct report from EXEC-002 is that AXIS-L was
   uninformative for leg (i)**, not that AXIS-L's cells carried the verdict (MAJ-L1-04).

#### 15.5.10 Blank-spread sensitivity — MANDATORY (resolves MAJ-5)

v1.0 §24 item 3 licensed the benchmark to ignore the pseudo-replication because the understated
blank spread "affects all arms identically". **That sentence does not transfer to v1.1's
comparison and the iteration-6 draft carried it over unexamined.** The interval half-width is
`t95(2) * sd_b / sqrt(3) / sample_sd`, and at matched retention the arms differ **precisely in
their blank counts**, which is the input to both `sd_b` and (under V2) `sample_sd`. The
understatement therefore enters each arm's lower bound by a different factor and does not cancel.

The measured factor is large. EV-0036: depth-normalised ungated blanks across three independent
HCC1395BL libraries are 11,770.9 / 11,671.3 / 11,849.0, giving a between-library standard
deviation of **89.08 against the five pseudo-blanks' 7.64 — an 11.7-fold understatement**.
EV-0045 adds that the three TF0 replicates share about **90%** of their normal reads on HCC1395
and about **45%** on COLO829, so the effective replicate count is nearer one than three.

```text
REQUIRED. Every S6 leg is recomputed with sd_b multiplied by each inflation factor
    f in { 1, 2, 4, 11.7 }
and matched_retention_comparison.tsv carries one row per factor, with `sd_inflation` as a key
column and `leg_i`, `leg_ii`, `leg_iii`, `leg_iv` recomputed at that factor.

THE ARITHMETIC, WRITTEN OUT, because §23's import rule and this section could not otherwise both
be obeyed (resolves X-05). `mrdz.score.z_interval(sample_total, blank_totals, sample_sd)` derives
`sd` internally via `blank_spread(blank_totals)` and exposes NO inflation parameter; there is no
call that returns `lo` at f != 1. The inflation is therefore computed as ARITHMETIC ON mrdz's OWN
OUTPUTS:
        lo_f = z - f * (z - lo)
        hi_f = z + f * (hi - z)
    with z, lo and hi taken from mrdz.score.z_interval AT f = 1.
    This is exact, because the half-width is linear in sd_b.
FORBIDDEN: rescaling `blank_totals` so that `blank_spread` returns f * sd_b. It also moves
`blank_mean`, and therefore moves `z` itself, so the recomputed `lo` would be wrong. §23 carves
the two lines above out of the reimplementation prohibition and out of nothing else.

f = 1 IS THE ONLY FACTOR AT WHICH A LEG MAY HOLD (resolves MAJ-L2-02). Every S6 leg outcome that
can contribute to a HOLD is read at f = 1 and at f = 1 only. **A leg that does not HOLD at f = 1
can never HOLD at any other factor.** The rows at f in {2, 4, 11.7} are DISQUALIFYING-ONLY. A leg
that does not HOLD at f = 1 but HOLDS at some f > 1 is recorded as `INFLATION-ONLY` and may not
contribute to a positive S6 in any form.
BINDING CONSEQUENCE (§21 F14): if a leg HOLDS at f = 1 but its ordering reverses or becomes
UNDECIDABLE at f = 11.7, that leg is reported as SD-DEPENDENT and may NOT contribute to a
positive S6 result. If no leg survives f = 11.7, S6 may not be reported as leaving the mechanism
claim standing, on any individual.
```

**WHAT THIS SENSITIVITY ANALYSIS CAN AND CANNOT ESTABLISH — the inflation is NOT neutral between
the arms, and the protocol says so rather than selling f = 11.7 survival as a strengthening
(resolves MAJ-L2-02).** Write `h(A) = t95(2) * sd_b(A) / sqrt(3) / sample_sd(A)`, so that
`lo_f(A) = z(A) - f * h(A)` and the paired difference is

```text
D(f) = [ z(B1) - z(comp) ]  -  f * [ h(B1) - h(comp) ]
```

so `D` moves by `-(h(B1) - h(comp))` per unit of `f`. On HCC1395's operating-point blank triples
under V2 — B1 10 / 8 / 8 against C2 0 / 1 / 1 — `h(B1) = 0.974` and `h(C2) = 1.757`, so `D` GAINS
0.782 z-units per unit of `f`, and 9.15 z-units at f = 11.7, **entirely in B1's favour**. This is
not an accident of these counts. S6's own hypothesis is that B1 retains MORE blank ALT reads than
the comparator at matched 0.1% count; B1 therefore has the larger `mean_blank`, hence the larger
V2 `sample_sd`, hence the SMALLER interval half-width, hence the LARGER gain from inflation.

Consequences, stated:

```text
- Under V2 at matched counts, F14's test is WEAK in exactly the cell §15.5.4 identifies as
  decisive, because the inflation systematically widens the very gap it is supposed to test for
  fragility. A leg that "survives f = 11.7" under V2 has survived a test that was biased toward
  letting it survive. That survival may NOT be reported as a strengthening.
- What the analysis CAN establish: (a) it can DISQUALIFY a leg whose ordering reverses under
  inflation, which is a real and one-directional result, and under V1 — where sample_sd does not
  depend on the blank counts — the bias above is absent, so F14 has force there; (b) it puts the
  magnitude of the understatement into the record beside every leg verdict.
- What it CANNOT establish: that any leg is robust to the pseudo-replication. §15.5.9's MODEL-C
  table, not this sensitivity analysis, is where the design states its size under the correlated
  blank model, and §24 item 17 records the limit.
```

#### 15.5.11 The `region` classification, fixed numerically before EXEC-002 (resolves MAJ-07, M4, X6)

`region` gated a verdict and was defined nowhere in the iteration-6 draft. It is defined here,
mechanically, from quantities that do not involve the B1-versus-comparator contrast, and it is
frozen by §20.8 clause 3.

```text
Evaluated per target, per axis, per construction, per individual, IN THIS ORDER:

1. EXCLUDED_UNDERPOWERED   iff any clause of the §15.5.6 count floor fires for that leg, or Z is
                           undefined, or blank_sd = 0, or the target is TARGET_UNREACHABLE.
                           This test is applied FIRST and takes precedence.

2. DEGENERATE_HIGH         iff EITHER
                             (i)  max over the arms compared in that leg of
                                  | Z(A, tau) - Z(B0_scorable) |  <  0.50 z-units,
                             OR (ii) n_tau(s) >= 0.80 * scorable_ALT(s) in every matched sample s.

3. DISCRIMINATING          otherwise.
```

**Why 0.50.** §15.5.9's MODEL-I table measures the smallest per-target simulated sd of the paired
difference at **0.615** z-units (COLO829, V1, tau = 0.70), with the MODEL-I range running 0.615 to
2.150 across cells at f = 1. A region in which every compared arm sits within 0.50 of the
unfiltered anchor is a region in which the arms differ by less than the smallest noise level in
the quantity being compared; it cannot resolve any contrast this benchmark adjudicates. The
constant was fixed at iteration 7 against a coarser figure (0.83) and is NOT re-tuned here — it
remains frozen by §20.8 clause 3, and the measurement that would have supported a larger value is
reported rather than used to move it.
**Why 0.80.** At `scorable_ALT` of 35-63, keeping 80% or more of the scorable ALT reads leaves at
most 7-13 reads for any filter to differ on, and B0_scorable keeps all of them.

**Both directions are excluded.** A `DEGENERATE_HIGH` target may neither carry a pass nor trigger a
refutation. Its exclusion counts toward the three-evaluable-target floor **as an exclusion, never
as a pass** (§20.8 clause 4). A failing leg at any **DISCRIMINATING** target fails that leg,
regardless of anything else — the region label gates only which targets are read, never whether a
read failure counts.

## 16. Secondary metrics

All of v1.0 §16 is carried over. The premise flag on every 1% row is unchanged. The following are
added or made normative. All of §16.1-§16.7 is POST HOC (§1.1).

### 16.1 Retention, defined normatively (folds in D3)

Two columns, both required, on every per-sample and every ablation row:

```text
retention                  = kept_ALT / scorable_ALT, computed LITERALLY as v1.0 §16 wrote it.
                             For B0 the numerator is every ALT read while the denominator excludes
                             unscorable ones, so B0's value EXCEEDS 1 (1.19 to 1.67 in EXEC-001).
                             That value is reported UNCHANGED, so v1.0's literal quantity remains
                             readable in v1.1's outputs.
retention_scorable_only    = kept scorable ALT / scorable ALT. Exactly 1.0 for B0_scorable and for
                             B0 under this column. Identical to `retention` for B1, C1, C2, C3,
                             C4a and C4b.
```

**Every criterion, every sweep axis and every ablation row reads `retention_scorable_only`.** D3
ceases to be a standing deviation.

### 16.2 Retention ratio, WITH AN INTERVAL, under BOTH blank conventions (resolves MAJ-2, MAJ-03, M3)

A significant-figure count is **not** an uncertainty statement. The iteration-6 draft attached no
interval to `retention_ratio` for B1 or C2 and then adjudicated S5 on the fourth decimal. The
gaps it adjudicated are **0.0098 / 1.6741 = 0.59%** and **0.0086 / 2.9127 = 0.30%**.

**The standard error they must be read against is the SE OF THE DIFFERENCE, and the iteration-7
draft quoted B1's single-arm SE by mistake (minor finding adopted).** `sqrt(1/40 + 1/75) = 0.196`
and `sqrt(1/15 + 1/46) = 0.297` are B1's own log-scale delta-method SEs. C2's counts are 4 blank
and 8 level reads on HCC1395 and 3 blank and 11 on COLO829, so the SE of the log-ratio DIFFERENCE
is `sqrt(1/40 + 1/75 + 1/4 + 1/8) = 0.643` on HCC1395 and `sqrt(1/15 + 1/46 + 1/3 + 1/11) = 0.716`
on COLO829. The observed gaps are therefore **0.009 and 0.004 of one standard error**, not 0.030
and 0.010. The error was in the conservative direction for this section's own argument — the
comparison is even less resolved than the iteration-7 text claimed — but the quantity was
mislabelled and is corrected wherever it is used to size the comparison.

```text
DEFINITION
    retention_ratio(A, i, blankset)
        = retention_scorable_only(A, i, 0.1%) / retention_scorable_only(A, i, blankset)
      where the numerator is the mean over the three 0.1% replicates.

TWO BLANK CONVENTIONS, BOTH REQUIRED, BOTH EMITTED
    blankset = TF0 rep1-5   `retention_ratio`         what aggregate.py's ret_of(arm,'TF0')
                                                      computes and what EXEC-001 recorded;
                                                      F11 requires EXEC-002 to reproduce it
    blankset = TF0 rep1-3   `retention_ratio_3blank`  the blanks that enter z_interval (v1.0 §15)
    S5's legs must hold under BOTH conventions (§20.3).

    WHY BOTH, RE-DERIVED WITHOUT AN ORDER-OF-MAGNITUDE CLAIM MEASURED ON ONE READ (resolves
    SV-06). The iteration-7 draft justified this requirement by quoting four-figure THREE-BLANK
    ratios — 1.5687 / 1.9955 on HCC1395 and 4.1644 / 3.7338 on COLO829 — and asserting they
    "disagree in margin by more than an order of magnitude". Those numbers are NEW v1.1
    computations, not quotations of EXEC-001 (§16.5), and C2's two of them rest on TWO and on ONE
    kept blank read. Under §16.5's own rule they may not be displayed as ratios at all. They are
    displayed as raw counts here and everywhere else in this document:

        three-blank kept blank ALT / scorable blank ALT, then 0.1% kept / scorable, per replicate
        HCC1395  B1 blank 10/25, 8/22, 8/20     0.1%  22/45, 25/35, 28/45
        HCC1395  C2 blank  0/25, 1/22, 1/20     0.1%   3/45,  2/35,  3/45
        COLO829  B1 blank  2/33, 0/20, 4/30     0.1%   9/51, 19/55, 18/63
        COLO829  C2 blank  0/33, 1/20, 0/30     0.1%   2/51,  2/55,  7/63
        (results/per_sample_metrics.tsv)

    The justification that survives, and it is sufficient: **the three-blank set is the set that
    enters `z_interval` (v1.0 §15) while the five-blank set is what `aggregate.py` used for
    `retention_ratio` and what §21 F11 requires EXEC-002 to reproduce.** Two different blank sets
    are therefore already in play in this benchmark for two different purposes, and neither can be
    dropped: dropping the five-blank convention would break F11, and dropping the three-blank
    convention would leave S5 anchored on a blank set that no z computation uses. Requiring BOTH
    removes the convention as a free variable. No claim is made here about the SIZE of the
    disagreement between them, because on C2's one and two kept blank reads that size has no
    resolution.

INTERVAL, REQUIRED FOR EVERY ARM INCLUDING B1 AND C2
    retention_ratio_lo / retention_ratio_hi : the 5th and 95th percentiles of the ratio under the
    paired candidate bootstrap of §15.5.8 (a), B = 2000, built under EXACTLY the rules that
    section fixes — ladder, cut and score arrays FIXED; candidate rows resampled; the same
    undefined-resample definition, the same counting, and the same 20% INADMISSIBILITY ceiling —
    which for C2 also draws a tie-break seed index. Emitted beside n_blank_kept and n_level_kept,
    the raw counts the interval is built from, and beside n_boot_undefined and boot_admissible.
    **An INADMISSIBLE interval may not be reported as a 90% interval and its leg returns
    UNDECIDABLE** (§15.5.8 a, SV-03).
    For C1 and C3 the permutation percentiles are additionally emitted as
    retention_ratio_p05_200 / p50_200 / p95_200 (the PRESERVED vector that F8 and F11 read) and
    retention_ratio_p05_2000 / p50_2000 / p95_2000 (the post hoc vector); for C2 the seed-band
    percentiles are emitted under the §10.6 decomposition names (seed_only / boot_only / combined).
    EVERY interval in this section is CONDITIONAL ON THIS BLANK LIBRARY (§15.5.9 point 6, SV-05).
    The candidate bootstrap resamples WITHIN samples and cannot generate between-library variance.
    That scope must be stated wherever the interval is stated.

DECISION RULE — INTERVAL DOMINANCE, at full stored precision
    FOR A LEG COMPARING B1 AGAINST A COMPARATOR ARM (legs b, c, d):
      HOLDS       iff  retention_ratio_lo(B1) > retention_ratio_hi(comparator)
      FAILS       iff  retention_ratio_lo(comparator) > retention_ratio_hi(B1)
      UNDECIDABLE otherwise, including every exact tie, which lies inside the overlap.
    FOR LEG (a), WHICH HAS NO COMPARATOR RATIO — it compares B1's ratio against 1 and C1's band
    against 1 (minor findings adopted; the iteration-7 text left "dominates" undefined here):
      leg (a) is decided by **v1.0's S4 POINT RULE, unchanged**, and by nothing else:
          clause (i)  HOLDS iff retention_ratio(B1) > 1 on both individuals, at the point value;
          clause (ii) HOLDS iff C1's 5th-95th percentile band over the PRESERVED 200 draws
                      CONTAINS 1.
      The INTERVAL reading — requiring retention_ratio_lo(B1) > 1 — is a DIFFERENT and HARDER
      question. It is computed and REPORTED as `leg_a_interval_reading`, and it is NOT
      verdict-bearing, because S4 is a PREREGISTERED criterion and re-parameterising it by a post
      hoc evidential standard is forbidden by qc_001.md (c) in either direction. §20.6's S4
      conjunct is pinned to this point rule.
    §16.5 governs DISPLAY only and has no effect on any rule in this block.
```

This one rule replaces the significant-figure adjudication, the undefined tie convention and the
`LOW_COUNT` escape clause of the iteration-6 draft, all three of which are **deleted**. It is
defined at every count, including COLO829's, so there is no fallback branch to leave unspecified.
It also settles the two panel findings that pointed in opposite directions on ties: a tie is
UNDECIDABLE, and UNDECIDABLE is a **non-success** (§20.7), so a tie can never support a mechanism
claim and can never be silently recorded as a failure either.

### 16.3 Sweep secondary metrics, and the mandatory verdict tally

Per individual, per arm, per ladder point, per construction: ladder index, nominal percentile
equivalent, `cut` or `k`, `n_scorable`, kept-ALT counts at TF0 and 0.1% **per replicate**, `R` at
