TF0 and 0.1% **per replicate**, `G`, `sample_sd`, `blank_mean`, `blank_sd`, `z`, `lo`, `hi`,
**verdict per replicate**, `zlo_min`, and for C1/C3/C2 the 5th/50th/95th percentiles across
draws or seeds.

**The per-replicate verdict tally must be printed beside any S6 claim**, in the same block, as
§16.6 already requires for S3. That is not hypothetical: at the locked operating point HCC1395 B1
V1 is `UNRELIABLE / UNRELIABLE / detected`, its headline `zlo_min = +2.2995` comes from an
`UNRELIABLE` replicate, and all three COLO829 B1 0.1% replicates are `UNRELIABLE` under V1
(`results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`).

**A REPORTING OBLIGATION IS NOT ENOUGH, and at iteration 8 it becomes an ADMISSIBILITY CONDITION
(minor finding adopted).** Under the iteration-7 rules a positive S6 resting entirely on cells in
which every contributing replicate is `UNRELIABLE` was admissible. It is not now:

```text
`unreliable_frac` — the fraction of the contributing per-replicate verdicts that are UNRELIABLE —
is a required column on every matched_retention_comparison.tsv row.
Where unreliable_frac = 1.0 for the B1 arm at a target, that target is flagged ALL_UNRELIABLE.
A leg may not HOLD in a cell in which EVERY DISCRIMINATING evaluable target is ALL_UNRELIABLE;
that cell returns UNDECIDABLE. ALL_UNRELIABLE targets are NOT excluded from the refutation
direction — an undetermined call may still be beaten by a comparator — so this condition removes
passes only, which is the direction qc_001.md rule 1 requires.
```

### 16.4 Detection-outcome reporting rule (binds QC-03)

Every **human-readable** artifact and every summary that states a `zlo_min` result **must
reprint, verbatim and complete, the four-cell detection table**. For machine-readable artifacts
the requirement is discharged by a reference column, because `retention_sweep.tsv` states
`zlo_min` on every row and cannot contain a text block, and a rule the executor must silently drop
is how a mandatory-context rule dies (minor finding adopted): every TSV row stating a `zlo_min` or
a v1.1 verdict carries `v1_0_registered_outcome` (value `H1 SUPPORTED`) and `detection_table_ref`
(value `results_v1_1/registered_outcome_v1_1.txt`).

```text
                 V1        V2
HCC1395 B0       1%        1%
HCC1395 B1       1%        1% and 0.1%
COLO829 B0       1%        1%
COLO829 B1       1%        1%
```

(Source: `qc/qc_001.md` QC-03, derived from `results/ablation_results.tsv`
`levels_detected_replicate_rule`.)

**EXEC-002 must compute its own detection table** and print it beside this one. Any difference is
handled under F11 as an implementation fault, not absorbed silently.

**Forbidden**: any unqualified sentence of the form "methylation enables 0.1% detection". The
detection outcome changes in **one of four** individual x construction cells. Any sentence about
detection must name that cell. This is a reporting rule with no escape clause.

### 16.5 Significant-figure floor — a DISPLAY convention only (binds QC-04)

```text
>= 30 kept blank ALT reads behind the denominator   -> at most 4 significant figures
>= 10                                               -> at most 3 significant figures
>=  5                                               -> at most 2 significant figures
<   5                                               -> NOT displayed as a ratio. Displayed as the
                                                       two raw counts.
```

**This governs DISPLAY. It does not govern any decision.** Every criterion is evaluated by the
interval rule of §16.2 at full stored precision. The iteration-6 draft used §16.5 as an evidential
rule and then decided the same leg on the fourth decimal in §20.10; that contradiction is removed
by making §16.5 a presentation rule with no evidential role.

The bands are a **convention**, not a derivation, and the protocol says so rather than implying
otherwise. Their only justification is that they are monotone in the count and coarse enough that
a reader is not invited to read a four-figure ratio off three reads. They were set before the
worked examples below were checked and they are not tuned to them.

**The worked examples of the iteration-6 draft were computed on the wrong blank set and are
corrected here.** `retention_ratio` is built on **all five** TF0 replicates (§16.2), so:

```text
HCC1395 B1 blank kept ALT = 10 + 8 + 8 + 8 + 6 = 40 reads  -> 4 significant figures permitted
HCC1395 C2 blank kept ALT =  0 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 =  4 reads  -> NOT displayed as a ratio
COLO829 B1 blank kept ALT =  2 + 0 + 4 + 3 + 6 = 15 reads  -> 3 significant figures permitted
COLO829 C2 blank kept ALT =  0 + 1 + 0 + 0 + 2 =  3 reads  -> NOT displayed as a ratio
(counts from results/per_sample_metrics.tsv; the iteration-6 draft used 26 and 6 and was wrong)
```

Every row carries `n_blank_kept`, `n_blank_kept_3`, `n_level_kept` and `sig_figs_allowed` so a
reader can apply the rule independently.

**Carve-out for quotation, CORRECTED at iteration 8 because the iteration-7 provenance claim was
false (resolves SV-06).** Two different classes of four-figure number appear in this document and
the iteration-7 carve-out described both as quotations of EXEC-001. They are not.

```text
CLASS 1 — VERBATIM QUOTATIONS of EXEC-001 artifacts, retained for audit. Not compliant displays
          under this section, and may not be re-quoted as compliant displays.
    the FIVE-BLANK retention ratios 1.6741 / 1.6839 (HCC1395) and 2.9127 / 2.9041 (COLO829)
        — results/ablation_results.tsv, and qc_001.md's QC-01 table
    zlo_min +2.2995 and +0.7396; delta_vs_C2 2.3725 / 3.6784 / 0.5255 / 1.0340
        — results/ablation_results.tsv
    C1's 0.857 [0.00, 1.96] and 1.161 [0.32, 2.35]  — qc_001.md F8 paragraph, 200-draw vector

CLASS 2 — NEW v1.1 DESIGNER COMPUTATIONS, not present in any EXEC-001 artifact. The header of
          results/ablation_results.tsv has no retention_ratio_3blank column; the quantity is
          introduced by §16.2 of this document.
    the THREE-BLANK ratios the iteration-7 draft printed as 1.5687 / 1.9955 (HCC1395) and
    4.1644 / 3.7338 (COLO829).
    §16.5's OWN RULE IS APPLIED TO THEM: C2's rest on 0+1+1 = TWO and 0+1+0 = ONE kept blank
    read, below the 5-read band, so they are NOT displayed as ratios anywhere in this document.
    They are displayed as the raw counts listed in §16.2, and §20.3 and §20.10 follow that.
    B1's three-blank quantities rest on 26 and 6 kept blank reads respectively and are displayed
    at 3 and 2 significant figures where they are needed at all.
```

A number in CLASS 2 may not be re-quoted as a ratio by any artifact, any summary or any
downstream record. The iteration-7 carve-out's final sentence was the only thing standing between
`3.7338` and propagation, and it could not do that job while mislabelling where the number came
from.

### 16.6 S3 reporting rule (binds QC-05)

Wherever S3's outcome is stated, the near-miss must be stated beside it in the same block:

```text
S3 HELD — no blank returns `detected` in any arm under either construction.
Reported beside it, always: under V2 on COLO829, TF0 rep3 returns UNRELIABLE at [-1.64, +3.15]
and TF0 rep5 returns UNRELIABLE at [+0.69, +4.31]. Neither is a false detection; both straddle
the call threshold, which is what UNRELIABLE is for; both sit in the single-digit-count regime
of §16.5.
```

### 16.7 Read-quality coupling diagnostics — REQUIRED (resolves MAJ-01 ii and iii)

These are outputs, not criteria. They exist so that the read-quality explanation is **visible**
rather than argued about, and §20.7 requires them to be reported beside any mechanism statement.

```text
(a) ALT ENRICHMENT IN THE BLANK, per arm, per TF0 replicate, per individual:
        alt_enrichment_tf0 = retention_scorable_only(arm, TF0 rep) / (global keep fraction)
    where the global keep fraction is K_arm / n_scorable_reads for that sample.
    B1's value on HCC1395 TF0 rep1 is 0.400 / (2895 / 58990) = 0.400 / 0.04908 = 8.15.
    ALT reads in a pure-normal blank under the 1 <= alt <= 2 gate are basecall or alignment errors
    by construction, so an enrichment above 1 in a blank is coupling between the score and a read's
    propensity to carry a spurious ALT base, with no tumour methylation involved.
    THIS NUMBER MUST BE REPORTED AND EXPLAINED, on every individual, at TF0 and at 0.1%, for every
    arm. It may not be omitted from the evidence summary.

(b) THE (d, t) JOINT DISTRIBUTION OF KEPT ALT READS, emitted as `kept_read_d_t_joint.tsv`, one row
    per (individual, sample, arm, d, t) with a count, separately at TF0 and at 0.1%:
        d = the read's discordant confident-position count (score_b1 numerator)
        t = the read's confident-position count            (score_b1 denominator, = score_C2)
    Required derived columns in the evidence summary:
        frac_kept_with_d_eq_1     the share of kept ALT reads whose ENTIRE evidence is ONE
                                  discordant modification call
        median_t_kept             and the same for the unkept scorable ALT reads
    Why: EXEC-001's `cut` is 0.07692307692307693 = exactly 1/13 on HCC1395 and 0.1 = 1/10 and
    0.0967741935483871 = 3/31 on COLO829 (execution/raw/*.json). At a cut of 1/13 any read with
    t <= 12 and a SINGLE discordant call is kept, because 1/12 > 1/13; only reads with t >= 13
    need d >= 2. A reader must be able to see whether kept reads carry a coherent pattern or one
    call, and no criterion in this protocol can show them that.

(c) SCORE CORRELATIONS, per sample: Spearman correlation of score_b1 against EVERY member of
    `P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e}` (both swept directions for C4d and C4e), over the scorable
    reads, and over the scorable ALT reads separately.
    **THIS IS NOT DESCRIPTIVE. IT HAS A CONSEQUENCE, mirroring §10.5's treatment of an
    uninformative C3 (resolves MAJ-L1-01 item 5).** The iteration-7 draft required the correlation
    to be reported and said a near-zero value "must be stated", but attached no consequence — so a
    leg could HOLD, and contribute to a positive S6, in a sample where the correlation showed it
    had tested nothing.

    ```text
    RHO_MIN = 0.10, fixed here before EXEC-002 and frozen by §20.8 clause 3.
    Where | Spearman(score_b1, score_X) | over the SCORABLE ALT READS of a sample is below
    RHO_MIN, comparator X is UNINFORMATIVE for that sample: its contribution to S5 leg (d) and
    S6 leg (iv) returns UNDECIDABLE there — NEVER HOLDS. It may still contribute a REFUTATION,
    because a comparator that beats B1 while being uncorrelated with score_b1 is a stronger
    result against the mechanism, not a weaker one.
    If EVERY member of P is UNINFORMATIVE on a sample, leg (iv) is UNDECIDABLE on that individual
    and the fact is reported beside any mechanism statement.
    ```
    A high correlation is not a defect; it is the quantity that makes a member of P an informative
    comparator rather than a decorative one. RHO_MIN = 0.10 is the level below which a rank
    association over the 20-63 scorable ALT reads of a sample is indistinguishable from none.
```

## 17. Statistical analysis

v1.0 §17 is carried over and **binding** in what it forbids: no significance test on the three
pseudo-replicates, no p-value whose denominator is the pseudo-blank spread, effect sizes and
intervals reported, decision by pre-specified consistency rule. §15.5.8 states why the v1.1
devices do not violate it and where they stop.

**What changes, and it is a change, declared rather than smuggled.** The iteration-6 draft
thresholded B1 against the 95th percentile of a permutation distribution while recording
`method NONE`. That record was false. v1.1's rows name their methods:

```yaml
comparison:
  - B1 - B0   in zlo_min at 0.1%, within individual, under V1 and V2      # v1.0, unchanged
  - B1 - C1   same                                                        # v1.0, unchanged
  - B1 - C2   same                                                        # v1.0, unchanged
  - B1 - C3   same                                                        # NEW, post hoc
  - B1 - X for every X in P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d+, C4d-, C4e+, C4e-}     # NEW, post hoc
  - retention_ratio(B1) - retention_ratio(C2), both blank conventions     # NEW, post hoc (S5)
  - Z(B1, tau) - Z(C2, tau) at every tau in T, AXIS-L and AXIS-B          # NEW, post hoc (S6 i)
  - Z(B1, tau) - [95th pct of C3 2000-draw band at tau], same scope       # NEW, post hoc (S6 ii)
  - Z(B1, tau) - [95th pct of C1 2000-draw band at tau], same scope       # NEW, post hoc (S6 iii)
  - Z(B1, tau) - max over X in P of Z(X, tau), same scope                 # NEW, post hoc (S6 iv)
null_hypothesis: each difference is <= 0
effect_size: the paired difference in zlo_min, in z units, reported per individual WITH THE THREE
             PER-REPLICATE PAIRED DIFFERENCES THAT PRODUCED IT (v1.0 §17, restored in §22.2)
confidence_interval: Student-t 95% on the blank mean, propagated onto z (mrdz.z_interval), plus
             the bootstrap and permutation intervals of §15.5.8

statistical_tests.tsv:
  LOCATION AND REGISTER, stated because the iteration-7 draft left this artifact homeless
  (resolves PH-05 c): the file EXEC-002 writes is `results_v1_1/statistical_tests.tsv`.
  EXEC-001's single row is COPIED IN UNMODIFIED, carrying `posthoc = PREREGISTERED_V1_0`.
  `results/statistical_tests.tsv` is a READ-ONLY EXEC-001 artifact (§22.4) and is not written.
  Every v1.1 row carries `posthoc = POST_HOC_2026-09-19`.
  v1.0 rows                       method: NONE          reason: unchanged, v1.0 §17
  S5 legs, S6 legs (i) and (iv)   method: "paired candidate bootstrap, B=2000, one-sided,
                                           alpha=0.05, 90% interval on the paired difference"
  S6 legs (ii) and (iii)          method: "one-sided permutation test, B=2000, alpha=0.05,
                                           within-stratum exchangeability (C3) / full
                                           exchangeability (C1)"
  every v1.1 row                  reason: names the exact null and states that the test does NOT
                                          address the pseudo-replication, which §15.5.10 handles
  multiple_testing: family-wise control in the refutation direction only (§15.5.8 d); the support
                    direction is an intersection-union rule, whose size is bounded by the
                    per-comparison alpha and whose power is reported in §15.5.9
```

Every arm is computed on the same BAM in the same pass, so every comparison is paired at the
sample level. **v1.0 §17's clause that "the pseudo-replication affects all arms identically" does
not extend to the retention-matched cross-arm comparison** (§15.5.10, §24 item 17).

## 18. Ablation plan

v1.0 §18 carried over, extended:

```text
B0             SNV evidence only                                          v1.0
B1 - B0        what adding the methylation filter does                    v1.0
B1 - C1        what read-to-score correspondence adds                     v1.0
B1 - C2        what methylation pattern adds over CpG density             v1.0
B1 - C3        what score-to-read correspondence adds WITHIN a density stratum   NEW, post hoc
B1 - C4a/C4b   what the score adds over per-read QUALITY                  NEW, post hoc
B1 - C2 | n    the same contrast at matched kept-ALT count                NEW, post hoc
B1 - C3 | n    the same, against the density-stratified null              NEW, post hoc
B1 - C4 | n    the same, against the read-quality comparators             NEW, post hoc
```

### 18.1 What each contrast licenses, and what it does not

```text
B1 > C2 at the locked PCT=95      licenses nothing about mechanism. QC-02: decided at an
                                  8-10x count difference.

B1 > C2 at matched COUNT          licenses: the advantage is not explained by HOW MANY reads the
                                  filter keeps. Does NOT license: that methylation pattern rather
                                  than some other read-level property is responsible.

B1 > C3 at matched COUNT          licenses: the advantage is not explained by CpG DENSITY AT THE
                                  REALISED STRATUM RESOLUTION, by filter aggressiveness, or by
                                  counting statistics, because C3 preserves those three.
                                  THE RESOLUTION IS A REPORTED NUMBER, NOT AN ADJECTIVE: strata
                                  are exact score_C2 values merged only to clear STRATUM_MIN = 30
                                  reads (§10.1), and `stratum_width_max`, `n_strata` and
                                  `n_merges` are emitted per sample. Where the realised width
                                  exceeds STRATUM_WIDTH_MAX or straddles 1/cut, leg (ii) is
                                  UNDECIDABLE at that target (§10.5) — because within a wide
                                  stratum `d/t > cut` prefers low-t reads by arithmetic alone, so
                                  a density-only filter could otherwise satisfy the leg.
                                  Does NOT license — known to be uncontrolled by C3:
                                    - per-read basecall / modification-call error rate
                                    - per-read modification-call CONFIDENCE
                                    - read length
                                    - local coverage
                                    - allele-specific germline methylation
                                    - mapping quality
                                    - LIBRARY OF ORIGIN
                                  A within-stratum permutation destroys the read-to-error-rate
                                  correspondence exactly as thoroughly as the read-to-pattern
                                  correspondence, so a filter knowing ONLY error rate passes C3.
                                  Does NOT license: that the mechanism is "methylation identifies
                                  tumour reads" in general — at most for THIS candidate set,
                                  THESE two cell lines, at THIS level.

B1 > every member of P            licenses: the advantage is not explained by any of the FIVE
   at matched COUNT               NAMED methylation-pattern-blind read properties this design
                                  measures — alignment mismatch rate, positional ambiguity of the
                                  footprint, modification-call uncertainty, read length, mapping
                                  quality. Does NOT license: that NO read property explains it.
                                  P has five named members; THE CLASS IS OPEN.

                                  THE LIBRARY-OF-ORIGIN LIMITATION, stated here because no
                                  criterion in this protocol addresses it (MAJ-L1-01). v1.0 §6
                                  builds the mixtures by subsampling tumour into that individual's
                                  own matched normal, so tumour reads and normal reads come from
                                  TWO DIFFERENT LIBRARIES. ANY read property that discriminates
                                  library of origin — including properties not in P and not named
                                  above — produces both differential retention AND a matched-count
                                  z advantage, with no tumour biology involved. **NO v1.1 OUTCOME,
                                  INCLUDING THE MOST POSITIVE ONE, DISTINGUISHES "methylation
                                  identifies tumour-derived reads" FROM "some read property
                                  identifies the spiked-in library."** §24 item 21 carries this
                                  forward as a standing limitation of the benchmark.

S5 (ratio contrast)               necessary, not sufficient — §20.3 states why.
```

> The iteration-6 draft wrote here that `B1 > C3` "is not explained by ... read length, because
> C3 preserves all of them". **That is struck.** C3 preserves each read's density STRATUM, not its
> length: within a stratum of equal confident-position count, length varies inversely with local
> CpG density. The claim was unsupported and it is withdrawn.

Phasing and CNV arms remain **unavailable, not omitted** (v1.0 §18).

## 19. Low-signal / dilution analysis

**UNCHANGED**, carried over from v1.0 §19. All four levels computed and reported separately;
pooling forbidden; a detection at 0.01% is first a suspicion of defect.

**The exploratory COLO829 chr1 arm is NOT re-run in EXEC-002.** EXEC-001 ran all 14 passes and D5
records the outcome: the arm is degenerate — 97,789 consensus positions against the primary run's
1,153,835, every blank yielding zero kept ALT reads, `sample_sd = 0`, verdict `no estimate` —
exactly the consensus-limited failure review_003's N010 predicted in advance. That result stands
as recorded, is reported unchanged, and decides nothing in §20.

The retention sweep is **not** applied to the exploratory arm. An arm with zero kept ALT reads in
every blank has no retention curve.

## 20. Success criteria

### 20.0 The v1.0 preregistered outcome, reproduced verbatim (MANDATORY BESIDE ANY v1.1 VERDICT)

```text
# BM-0001-methyl-gate-mrd — registered outcome under protocol v1.0 §20/§21
# Mechanical application of the preregistered rules. No interpretation.

S1: HELD
S2: HELD
S3: HELD
S4: HELD

S1 holds for individuals: ['COLO829', 'HCC1395']
S1 holds for constructions: ['V1', 'V2']

REGISTERED_OUTCOME: H1 SUPPORTED
```

(`results/registered_outcome.txt`, EXEC-001, preserved unmutated.)

**This block must be printed in full, unedited, in `results_v1_1/registered_outcome_v1_1.txt`, in
the evidence summary, and in every HUMAN-READABLE artifact that states a v1.1 verdict — inside the
explicit fences §22.5 specifies, and never as the first verdict line of a file.** A v1.1 verdict
reported in a human-readable artifact without it is non-compliant with this protocol.

**For MACHINE-READABLE artifacts the requirement is discharged by reference, because a TSV cannot
contain a twelve-line text block and a rule the executor must silently drop is not a rule (minor
finding adopted).** `criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv`, `matched_retention_comparison.tsv` and
`retention_sweep.tsv` each carry, on every row stating a v1.1 verdict or a `zlo_min`:
`v1_0_registered_outcome` with the literal value `H1 SUPPORTED`, and `detection_table_ref` with
the literal value `results_v1_1/registered_outcome_v1_1.txt`.

**v1.0 §20's named outcomes, reprinted BYTE FOR BYTE from `protocols/v1.0_locked.md` lines
713-731.** They remain binding wherever they apply.

> The iteration-7 draft labelled its copy of this block "reprinted in full" and it was ABRIDGED
> (PH-01). Every clause it dropped was the clause stating what the non-success MEANS — "not
> methylation identifying reads", "not methylation pattern", "the z-win is attributable to filter
> aggressiveness rather than to methylation selecting the tumour-derived reads" — and the
> introducing injunction **"which may not be relabelled"** was dropped entirely. That is the exact
> failure `qc_001.md` constraint (b) exists to prevent, in the one block a downstream reporter
> copies rather than re-derives. The full text is restored below.

--- BEGIN V1.0 NAMED OUTCOMES (protocols/v1.0_locked.md, PRESERVED VERBATIM) ---
Named outcomes, which may not be relabelled:

```text
S1 holds but S4 fails                         -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED. Not a success. The
                                                 z-win is attributable to filter aggressiveness
                                                 rather than to methylation selecting the
                                                 tumour-derived reads.
S1 holds under V1 but not V2 (or vice versa)  -> SD-MODEL DEPENDENT. Not a success.
S1 holds on HCC1395 only                      -> NOT REPLICATED. Not a success. This is the
                                                 outcome FIND-0021's COLO829 replication
                                                 failure makes a live possibility.
S1 fails against C1                           -> REFUTED: the gain is filter aggressiveness,
                                                 not methylation identifying reads.
S1 fails against C2                           -> REFUTED: the gain is CpG density, not
                                                 methylation pattern.
S2 fails                                      -> B1 inadmissible regardless of S1.
S3 fails                                      -> B1 inadmissible regardless of S1.
zlo_min UNDEFINED anywhere it is needed       -> FAILURE TO DEMONSTRATE, never a pass.
```
--- END V1.0 NAMED OUTCOMES ---

**FENCING RULE, identical to §22.5's rule for `results/registered_outcome.txt`.** Both v1.0 blocks
reprinted in this section — the registered outcome above and the named outcomes here — travel
inside their explicit BEGIN/END fences and **may not be paraphrased, truncated, abridged or
re-wrapped in any artifact, summary, log, deck or record.** §26 requires a BYTE COMPARISON of both
reprinted blocks against their sources before EXEC-002 reports anything.

### 20.1 PREREGISTERED criteria — carried over VERBATIM, unchanged

```text
S1 (PRIMARY)
    at 0.1%, on BOTH individuals, under BOTH variance constructions V1 and V2:
        zlo_min(B1) > zlo_min(B0)
    AND zlo_min(B1) > zlo_min(C1)      [C1 = median over 200 draws]
    AND zlo_min(B1) > zlo_min(C2)

S2 (NO REGRESSION)
    No level detected by B0 under the frozen replicate rule becomes undetected under B1,
    on either individual, under either construction.

S3 (NO FALSE DETECTION)
    Leave-one-out over all five TF0 replicates per individual: each blank is scored against
    the mean and spread of the other four, using that held-out blank's OWN sample_sd exactly
    as for any evaluated sample — the plug-in binomial under V1, sqrt(mean of the other four)
    under V2. Evaluated under BOTH constructions.
    S3 FAILS if ANY of the ten blanks returns `detected` under EITHER construction.
```

These three are **not touched** by this revision, in either direction. Their EXEC-001 outcomes
(all HELD) stand as recorded and EXEC-002 must reproduce them exactly at PCT=95 (§15.5.7 V-a).

**S1's C1 TERM IS PINNED TO THE PRESERVED 200-DRAW VECTOR (resolves MAJ-L2-01).** S1's text says
`[C1 = median over 200 draws]`, and §10.1 raises C1 to B = 2000 for the post hoc nulls. A median
over 2000 draws is NOT the median over 200, so reading S1 against the 2000-draw vector would
change a PREREGISTERED criterion's numeric input in an uncontrolled direction — a configuration in
which the 200-draw median sits just above `zlo_min(B1)` (S1 FAILS under v1.0) and the 2000-draw
median sits just below it (S1 PASSES under v1.1) is admitted by such a reading on identical data.
That is precisely the sideways move `qc_001.md` rule 1 forbids, and S1 is on forbidden list (c).

```text
NORMATIVE: S1's C1 term is  zlo_min_c1_200  — the median over draws j = 0..199 ONLY, EXEC-001's
preserved 200-draw aggregation, reproduced bit for bit. The 2000-draw vector MAY NOT be read by
S1, by S4 clause (ii), or by F8. It is used ONLY by S5 leg (c), S6 leg (iii) and §15.5.8's
permutation nulls, and every column carrying it is suffixed `_2000`.
No artifact may carry an unsuffixed C1 aggregate column (§22.2, §22.3).
```

### 20.2 S4 — PREREGISTERED, PRESERVED VERBATIM, STILL BINDING

```text
S4 (DIFFERENTIAL RETENTION — the mechanism)
    For B1, on BOTH individuals:
        ALT retention at 0.1%  >  ALT retention at TF0 (blank)
    and this must NOT hold for C1 beyond its own sampling noise — C1's retention is
    level-independent by construction, so a C1 ratio distinguishable from 1 would indicate an
    implementation fault rather than a control.
    Report the retention ratio  r = retention(0.1%) / retention(TF0)  for ALL FOUR arms, with
    C1's 5th and 95th percentiles across its 200 draws as the noise band.
```

**Status under v1.1: PRESERVED, EVALUATED, AND STILL A NECESSARY CONDITION (§20.6).** What is
demoted is what S4 **LICENSES**, not whether it **BINDS**.

**S4 clause (ii)'s noise band is `retention_ratio_p05_200` / `p95_200` — the PRESERVED 200-draw
band, not the 2000-draw one (MAJ-L2-01).** S4 is preregistered and its own text says "across its
200 draws". Its EXEC-001 values are 0.857 with [0.00, 1.96] on HCC1395 and 1.161 with [0.32, 2.35]
on COLO829. Note that `p05 = 0.00` on HCC1395 is driven by degenerate draws that §15.5.8 (c) now
requires EXEC-002 to exclude or flag; **that exclusion applies to the POST HOC 2000-draw band
only.** S4's and F8's preregistered band is reproduced with EXEC-001's silent-drop behaviour
intact, because changing it would re-define a preregistered criterion. The difference between the
two treatments is emitted as `band_admissible_2000` beside the preserved band, and reported.

**S4 is decided by v1.0's POINT rule, not by §16.2's interval rule (minor finding adopted).**
§20.3's leg (a) quotes S4's text and §20.6 keeps S4 as a conjunct; both are pinned to the point
rule of §16.2's leg-(a) block. The harder interval reading is computed and reported as
`leg_a_interval_reading` and is NOT verdict-bearing, because re-parameterising a preregistered
criterion by a post hoc evidential standard is forbidden in either direction.

> The iteration-6 draft wrote that S4 "decides nothing" and dropped it from §20.6's conjunction.
> That created a data configuration failing v1.0's success condition and passing v1.1's — the bar
> moving sideways rather than up, which is the one thing `qc_001.md` rule 1 forbids. **Both clauses
> of S4 are restored to the conjunction**, and the word "demoted" is confined to licensing.

S4 no longer **carries** the mechanism claim, because QC-01 established that it cannot: C2, which
uses no methylation pattern, satisfies it identically (HCC1395 1.6741 against 1.6839; COLO829
2.9127 against 2.9041 — CLASS 1 quotations under §16.5's carve-out). Passing S4 is necessary and says only
that B1's filter is level-dependent. The mechanism claim moves to S5, S6 and S7, which are
**additional** necessary conditions on top of S4, not replacements for it.

### 20.3 S5 — POST HOC (§1.1). Differential retention must EXCEED the density control's

```text
S5 (DIFFERENTIAL RETENTION, CONTROLLED)
    Evaluated PER INDIVIDUAL (S7 does the cross-individual conjunction), at the locked
    operating point PCT = 95, under BOTH blank conventions of §16.2, at full stored precision.
    THE DECIDING RULE DIFFERS BY LEG AND §16.2 STATES BOTH (minor findings adopted; the
    iteration-7 preamble subjected every leg to interval dominance and then §20.10 adjudicated
    leg (a) by a point comparison):
        leg (a)            v1.0's S4 POINT rule, unchanged, because S4 is PREREGISTERED
        legs (b), (c), (d) §16.2's INTERVAL DOMINANCE rule against a comparator arm

      (a) v1.0's S4, quoted verbatim and in full, BOTH clauses:
          "For B1, on BOTH individuals: ALT retention at 0.1% > ALT retention at TF0 (blank),
           and this must NOT hold for C1 beyond its own sampling noise — C1's retention is
           level-independent by construction, so a C1 ratio distinguishable from 1 would
           indicate an implementation fault rather than a control."
      AND (b) retention_ratio(B1) dominates retention_ratio(C2), where C2's interval is the
              combined tie-break-seed and bootstrap interval of §10.6 and §15.5.8 (a)
      AND (c) retention_ratio(B1) dominates retention_ratio(C3), C3's interval being its
              2000-draw permutation band
      AND (d) retention_ratio(B1) dominates retention_ratio(X) for EVERY X in the declared
              pattern-blind set P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e} (§10.2), i.e. B1 dominates
              max over X in P of retention_ratio_hi(X) — the WORSE CASE FOR B1 over the whole
              set, not over two members of it.
              A member flagged UNINFORMATIVE by §16.7 (c)'s RHO_MIN rule, MAPQ_DEGENERATE, or
              uncomputable because NM and MD are both absent, contributes UNDECIDABLE to leg (d)
              for that sample and NEVER HOLDS.

    Legs (b), (c) and (d) return HOLDS, FAILS or UNDECIDABLE by the §16.2 COMPARATOR rule.
    Leg (a) returns HOLDS or FAILS by v1.0's S4 POINT rule, which §16.2 states explicitly and
    which is NOT re-parameterised by the interval standard.
    UNDECIDABLE is a NON-SUCCESS and is never read as HOLDS. S5 HOLDS on an individual only if
    every leg HOLDS under BOTH blank conventions. An INADMISSIBLE bootstrap interval (§15.5.8 a)
    makes its leg UNDECIDABLE, never HOLDS.
```

> The iteration-6 draft wrote leg (a) as
> `retention_ratio(B1) > retention_ratio(TF0-blank baseline of B1)`, which compares a ratio to a
> ratio of a blank against itself, and §20.10 then silently read it as `> 1.0`. It is replaced by
> v1.0's S4 text, both clauses, verbatim. The `LOW_COUNT` escape clause is **deleted**; §16.2's
> interval rule is defined at every count.

**Why S5 is strictly harder than S4.** S5's leg (a) *is* S4, unchanged, both clauses, decided by
v1.0's own POINT rule — so S4 itself is neither strengthened nor weakened, which is what
`qc_001.md` forbidden list (c) requires. S5 adds legs (b), (c) and (d) as further necessary
conditions, each decided by an interval-dominance rule that is harder to satisfy in the support
direction than the point comparison it replaces. A conjunction with S4 as a conjunct cannot be
satisfied by anything that fails S4.

**S5's leg (b) POINT comparison is DETERMINED IN ADVANCE, and the protocol says so (resolves M2).**
`retention_ratio` is an ablation-row value, and §21 F11 requires EXEC-002 to reproduce EXEC-001's
ablation values exactly. Therefore the point values leg (b) compares are fixed before EXEC-002
runs and cannot be otherwise:

```text
five-blank convention   HCC1395  B1 1.6741  vs  C2 1.6839   -> B1 LOWER   by -0.0098
                        COLO829  B1 2.9127  vs  C2 2.9041   -> B1 higher  by +0.0086
                        (CLASS 1 under §16.5: verbatim quotations of results/ablation_results.tsv)

three-blank convention  CLASS 2 under §16.5 — NEW v1.1 computations, and C2's rest on TWO and on
                        ONE kept blank read, so they are shown AS COUNTS and not as ratios
                        (resolves SV-06). Blank kept/scorable per replicate, then 0.1%:
                        HCC1395  B1  10/25, 8/22, 8/20   vs 0.1% 22/45, 25/35, 28/45
                        HCC1395  C2   0/25, 1/22, 1/20   vs 0.1%  3/45,  2/35,  3/45
                        COLO829  B1   2/33, 0/20, 4/30   vs 0.1%  9/51, 19/55, 18/63
                        COLO829  C2   0/33, 1/20, 0/30   vs 0.1%  2/51,  2/55,  7/63
                        DIRECTION ONLY, which is all these counts support: B1 LOWER on HCC1395,
                        B1 higher on COLO829. No margin is quoted.
(counts from results/per_sample_metrics.tsv)
```

What EXEC-002 adds is the **intervals**, which are not determined in advance and which decide
whether each leg returns FAILS or UNDECIDABLE. Given the counts behind them — 40 and 4 kept blank
reads on HCC1395, 15 and 3 on COLO829 — the corrected SE of the DIFFERENCE (0.643 and 0.716,
§16.2) against observed gaps of 0.009 and 0.004 of one SE, and the one-read sensitivity §20.10
records, the protocol states the expectation openly: **legs (b) are likely to return UNDECIDABLE
rather than FAILS on both individuals**, and UNDECIDABLE is a non-success. S5's outcome will
therefore be **HOLDS on neither individual**.

**TWO FURTHER ADVANCE PREDICTIONS, stated here so they are not discovered afterwards:**

```text
1. (SV-03) COLO829's THREE-BLANK C2 ratio interval is expected to be INADMISSIBLE. Its
   denominator rests on ONE kept blank read, carried by one candidate among n_gate_pass = 22 in
   TF0 rep2, which is absent from (21/22)^22 = 36% of bootstrap resamples — above §15.5.8 (a)'s
   20% undefined ceiling. An INADMISSIBLE interval makes its leg UNDECIDABLE; it is NOT silently
   conditioned on the resamples in which the read survived, because that conditioning event is
   correlated with the quantity estimated. HCC1395's two carrying candidates are jointly absent
   from (27/28)^28 x (25/26)^26 = 13% of resamples, below the ceiling.
2. (MAJ-L2-04) S5 leg (b) CANNOT HOLD on HCC1395 whatever EXEC-002 measures, because F11 freezes
   the point values and lo(B1) <= point(B1) = 1.6741 < point(C2) = 1.6839 <= hi(C2). Therefore
   S5 cannot HOLD, S7 cannot HOLD, and the positive label L-I is CLOSED. §20.6 clause 0b states
   the consequence: this is a finding of the DESIGN, established by EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md
   before EXEC-002 runs, and it may not be presented as an outcome EXEC-002 produced.
```

§20.7 assigns S5's outcome a named label independently of S6.

**Is requiring `B1 > C2` on the ratio sufficient to resolve QC-01? NO.** Three reasons:

1. **The two ratios are ratios of retentions at different absolute levels.** At PCT=95 B1 moves
   0.363 -> 0.608 while C2 moves 0.038 -> 0.063 (HCC1395, five-blank). Comparing 1.67 against 1.68
   compares two arms an order of magnitude apart on the aggressiveness scale.
2. **A ratio is unstable at these counts.** COLO829's B1 blank totals are 2 / 0 / 4 reads (§20.10
   shows a single read flipping the HCC1395 comparison).
3. **A ratio is a summary of retention, not of detection performance.** The claim at issue is about
   `zlo_min`. A filter could have the larger ratio and the worse `zlo_min` — **in general**. Note
   the exception §15.5.4 establishes: on AXIS-L under V2 this reason does **not** apply, because
   the statistic reduces to the blank triple there.

S5 is therefore **necessary and not sufficient**. **S6 is the criterion that answers QC-02's
question**, and §15.5.9 states in advance what it can and cannot resolve.

### 20.4 S6 — POST HOC (§1.1). Matched-count dominance

```text
S6 (MATCHED-COUNT DOMINANCE)
    Evaluated PER INDIVIDUAL, under BOTH variance constructions V1 and V2, on BOTH retention axes
    AXIS-L and AXIS-B, over the DISCRIMINATING, evaluable targets tau in
    T = {0.10, ..., 0.70} (§15.5.3, §15.5.6, §15.5.11), with the arms matched on the INTEGER
    kept-ALT count (§15.5.7), with NO interpolation:

      (i)   Z(B1, tau)  vs  Z(C2, tau)                     paired bootstrap, §15.5.8 (a)
      (ii)  Z(B1, tau)  vs  C3's 2000-draw band            permutation,      §15.5.8 (c)
      (iii) Z(B1, tau)  vs  C1's 2000-draw band            permutation,      §15.5.8 (b)
      (iv)  Z(B1, tau)  vs  max over X in P of Z(X, tau)    paired bootstrap, §15.5.8 (a)
            where P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e} is the DECLARED pattern-blind set of §10.2 and
            C4d, C4e enter at the better of their two swept directions FOR THE COMPARATOR. Leg
            (iv) is therefore the worse case for B1 over the WHOLE set, not over two members.
            A member that is UNINFORMATIVE (§16.7 c), MAPQ_DEGENERATE or uncomputable contributes
            UNDECIDABLE and never HOLDS; if every member is so flagged, leg (iv) is UNDECIDABLE.

    Each leg at each target returns HOLDS, FAILS, UNDECIDABLE or EXCLUDED by the rules of
    §15.5.6, §15.5.8 and §15.5.11.

    THE VERDICT-BEARING RULE, per leg, per (individual x axis x construction) cell:
        POOLED: the leg HOLDS in that cell iff the 5th percentile of the JOINT-BOOTSTRAP
        distribution of the mean over DISCRIMINATING evaluable targets of  D*/null_sd*  exceeds 0,
        where null_sd* is the inner studentising sd defined normatively in §15.5.8 (a), the
        target set is FIXED on the observed data, and the decision is on the EMPIRICAL percentile.
        **Read at sd inflation factor f = 1 AND ONLY AT f = 1** (§15.5.10, MAJ-L2-02). A leg that
        does not HOLD at f = 1 can never HOLD at any other factor; the f in {2, 4, 11.7} rows are
        DISQUALIFYING-ONLY and a leg holding only under inflation is recorded INFLATION-ONLY.
        The leg is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE in that cell only if some DISCRIMINATING target clears the
        CENTRED family-wise bar of §15.5.8 (d), whose size and power are reported in §15.5.9
        RESULT 4. Anything else is UNDECIDABLE.
    S6 HOLDS on an individual only if every leg HOLDS in EVERY cell: both axes, both
    constructions. At least 3 DISCRIMINATING evaluable targets are required per cell, or that
    cell is UNDECIDABLE (§15.5.6).

    S6-STRICT (REPORTED, NOT VERDICT-BEARING, ZERO VERDICT WEIGHT): the same conjunction with
    every leg required to HOLD at EVERY DISCRIMINATING evaluable target individually. Reported per
    cell and per individual. It is strictly stronger than S6 and its outcome is printed beside
    S6's, but it does not by itself produce a verdict, for the reason §15.5.9 gives: its power
    against a real 1.5x advantage is 0.067-0.111 under MODEL-I and its spurious target-level
    failure rate under exact equivalence is 0.213-0.322 under MODEL-I and 0.577-0.580 under
    MODEL-C (§15.5.9 RESULT 2, f = 1), so neither of its outcomes is interpretable.
    **S6-STRICT has ZERO VERDICT WEIGHT and §20.7 attaches NO LABEL to it** (minor finding
    adopted). Because narrative reporting is not protected by §20.7's precedence rule, one further
    binding constraint applies: **S6-STRICT's outcome may never be stated without S6's outcome and
    §15.5.9's power figures for S6-STRICT in the same block, and a holding S6-STRICT never
    upgrades a headline or any applicable label.**
```

**Why S6 is strictly harder than anything in v1.0.** S6 is purely additive: no v1.0 criterion is
relaxed to accommodate it, and S1-S4 are evaluated exactly as before at PCT=95. Adding further
necessary conditions can only reduce the set of results that pass. S6 additionally forces B1 to
operate at counts far from its locked operating point (B1's realised R at 0.1% is
0.488889 / 0.714286 / 0.622222 on HCC1395 and 0.176471 / 0.345455 / 0.285714 on COLO829 at
PCT=95): at tau = 0.20 on HCC1395, B1 keeps 9 / 7 / 9 ALT reads at a cutoff it was never tuned
for. **That is where B1 can lose, and it is meant to be.**

**The count floor removes the most aggressive targets, and the protocol says which ones in
advance.** At HCC1395's `scorable_ALT` of 45 / 35 / 45, tau = 0.10 gives 4 / 4 / 4 kept reads,
below the floor of 5, so **tau = 0.10 is excluded on AXIS-L on HCC1395 before EXEC-002 runs**.
§15.5.9 gives the simulated survival probability of every other target. The advertised
"B1 operates where it can lose" therefore applies to tau = 0.20 and 0.30, not to 0.10, and the
protocol states that rather than letting the exclusion be discovered afterwards.

**On AXIS-L the statistic reduces to the blank triple under BOTH constructions** (§15.5.4, as
corrected at iteration 8 by MAJ-L1-04: exactly under V2, and to within a few percent under V1
because EXEC-001 measures `sample_sd/sqrt(G)` at 0.97 +- 0.02 with G matched by construction).
Every row carries `decided_by`, computed symmetrically by §15.5.4's rule. **If, on an individual,
every DISCRIMINATING target carrying a positive leg-(i) result is marked BLANK_TRIPLE under EITHER
construction, S6 returns UNDECIDABLE on that individual**, because the leg has then re-expressed
the differential-retention comparison QC-01 already measured — and found to be a tie to within
0.6% / 0.3% — rather than added to it. The iteration-7 clause named one cell; the rule now names
the determination, not the cell, so a verdict cannot rest on a variance-construction label.

**A cell in which every DISCRIMINATING evaluable target is ALL_UNRELIABLE returns UNDECIDABLE**
(§16.3). This removes passes only.

**The blank-spread sensitivity of §15.5.10 binds S6**: a leg whose ordering does not survive the
11.7-fold inflation is SD-DEPENDENT and may not contribute to a positive S6 (§21 F14). §15.5.10
also states, with the algebra, that under V2 at matched counts the inflation runs in B1's favour
by construction, so F14's test is WEAK in that cell and surviving f = 11.7 there may not be
reported as a strengthening.

### 20.5 S7 — POST HOC (§1.1). Replication of the mechanism result, named separately

```text
S7 (MECHANISM REPLICATES)
    S5 and S6 are each evaluated WITHIN an individual (§20.3, §20.4). S7 is the cross-individual
    conjunction:
        S7 HOLDS iff  S5 HOLDS on HCC1395 AND S5 HOLDS on COLO829
                 AND  S6 HOLDS on HCC1395 AND S6 HOLDS on COLO829.
    Any mixed outcome — including a leg-level split, e.g. S5 holding on one individual and S6 on
    the other — is MECHANISM NOT REPLICATED, which is a NON-SUCCESS, and the applicable per-
    individual labels are reported beside it (§20.7).
    No magnitude is compared across individuals (v1.0 §8, §24 item 2).
```

> The iteration-6 draft scoped S5 and S6 over "BOTH individuals" and then defined S7 as "S5 and S6
> hold on both individuals", which added no condition and made §20.7's split row unreachable. S5
> and S6 are now **per-individual** and S7 does the conjunction, so the split outcome is reachable
> and S7 is not vacuous. S7 is not claimed to be "strictly harder" than anything (§20.9) — it is
> the criterion that makes the split outcome NAMEABLE, which is its whole purpose.

S7 exists because FIND-0021's read-classification result already failed to replicate at full
magnitude on COLO829 (AUC 0.91 against 0.72, both registered replication thresholds failed) and
because COLO829 is the individual whose counts are single-digit (QC-04).

**REPORTING REQUIREMENT, which is what belongs in a success-criterion section.** Wherever a
`NOT REPLICATED` outcome (L-G) is stated, §15.5.9's power figure must be printed beside it, with
its scope label attached verbatim — **"leg (i), AXIS-L, pooled, both individuals"** — as RESULT 5's
UPPER BOUND on a positive result on both individuals: **<= 0.23 under the independent-blank model
and <= 0.47 under the correlated-blank model at a 1.5x alternative**, together with RESULT 3's
measured SIZE for the same rule (0.09-0.18 and 0.27-0.34) and RESULT 5's statement that the power
of S6 AS INSTALLED is unknown and strictly lower. (Iteration 7's "of order 0.06" is superseded by
these measurements.) The iteration-7 draft went further and asserted, inside this section, that "a NOT
REPLICATED outcome here carries very little evidential weight against the mechanism". **That
sentence is removed from §20.5**: it is an interpretation asserted normatively inside a criterion
(AGENTS.md rule 8), and it is the one sentence in the document that discounts a v1.0 NAMED
non-success outcome in advance. The judgement it expressed is moved to §24 item 18 and is phrased
there as what the power figure PERMITS rather than as a conclusion about weight.

### 20.6 The verdict rule

```text
CLAUSE 0 (BINDING CEILING). No v1.1 outcome may raise the project's claim above what v1.0
registered. v1.1's criteria are POST HOC (§1.1); passing a necessary condition written AFTER the
confounding measurement was seen means only that the design failed to kill the claim on one
further test. The maximum positive v1.1 result is:
    "the mechanism claim SURVIVED the post hoc tests of §1.1 and is NOT ESTABLISHED; a
     PREREGISTERED replication is required before it may be called established."
The phrases "SUPPORTED IN FULL" and "MECHANISM ESTABLISHED" may not appear in any v1.1 artifact.

CLAUSE 0b (THE POSITIVE BRANCH IS CLOSED — a finding of the DESIGN, not of EXEC-002; resolves
MAJ-L2-04). §21 F11 requires EXEC-002 to reproduce EXEC-001's `retention_ratio` values exactly.
On HCC1395 under the five-blank convention those are B1 1.6741 and C2 1.6839, so
point(B1) < point(C2), and §16.2's dominance rule gives
    retention_ratio_lo(B1) <= point(B1) < point(C2) <= retention_ratio_hi(C2)
for ANY interval EXEC-002 computes. **S5 leg (b) therefore cannot HOLD on HCC1395, S5 cannot
HOLD, S7 cannot HOLD, and label L-I is UNREACHABLE.** This is arithmetic on preserved numbers. It
is settled BEFORE EXEC-002 runs, by EXEC-001 plus `qc_001.md`, and it is stated here rather than
left as a "likely" in §20.3 and §20.10.

Three consequences, all binding:

```text
1. The mechanism half of H1 is WITHDRAWN on the strength of EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md. That
   withdrawal may NOT be attributed to EXEC-002 or presented as an outcome of the retention
   sweep. Any artifact stating it must cite §20.6 clause 0b as its basis.
2. EXEC-002's PURPOSE IS NARROWER THAN THE ITERATION-7 DRAFT IMPLIED: it determines WHICH
   non-success label applies — L-B1 alone, a more severe L-C / L-E / L-F refutation, or L-H
   MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE — and it produces the diagnostics (§16.7, §15.5) that the next
   preregistration needs. It cannot change the withdrawal.
3. The withdrawal is therefore reported in one of TWO distinct forms, never merged (§20.7):
   WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE and WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE. An UNDECIDABLE comparison is not evidence
   against the mechanism, and §24 item 18 forbids making a negative mechanism claim from a
   failure to clear a bar this design cannot clear.
```

**This is NOT a licence to loosen S5 or §16.2.** Loosening is the direction `qc_001.md` forbids
and it is not taken. What changes is what the protocol may CLAIM about its own positive branch.

H1's METRIC HALF        decided by S1, S2, S3 — PREREGISTERED, decided by EXEC-001: HELD, HELD,
                        HELD. v1.1 does not reopen it. EXEC-002 must reproduce it, or F11 fires.

H1's MECHANISM HALF     decided by S4 (preregistered, binding) together with S5, S6, S7
                        (POST HOC, not yet decided).

THE CONJUNCTION, restored to v1.0's form and extended:
    The mechanism claim is NOT WITHDRAWN only if
        S1, S2, S3 and S4 all hold   [v1.0 §20, verbatim, unchanged; S4 by v1.0's POINT rule]
    AND S5, S6 and S7 all hold       [POST HOC, §1.1]
    and even then the outcome is "NOT WITHDRAWN — NOT ESTABLISHED" under clause 0.
    CLAUSE 0b records that this conjunction is UNSATISFIABLE on this data, before EXEC-002.
Any other combination is one of the named non-success outcomes in §20.7 / §21.
```

Every statement of a v1.1 verdict must carry, in the same block: the §20.0 v1.0 block verbatim
inside §22.5's fences, the POST HOC citation of §1.1, the §16.4 detection table verbatim, the
§16.3 verdict tally, and the §16.7 (a) ALT-enrichment diagnostic.

### 20.7 Named outcomes — a MAP, not a ladder (resolves M2, M3)

The iteration-6 branch list was conditionally nested on S5, so on the data §20.10 predicts, two
branches fired at once and the strongest labels — `REFUTED` — were structurally unreachable. It is
replaced by a **map with a precedence rule**.

```text
RULE 1  EVERY applicable label is computed and reported. None is chosen in preference to another.
        criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv carries `branch_labels_applicable` with the complete set, per
        individual, and the evidence summary lists them all.
RULE 2  S4, S5, each S6 leg and S7 are evaluated INDEPENDENTLY. No label is gated on another
        criterion's outcome. REFUTED is reachable whatever S5 does.
RULE 3  The HEADLINE is the most severe applicable label, by this fixed precedence:
            REFUTED  >  UNSUPPORTED  >  NOT REPLICATED  >  UNDECIDABLE  >  NOT WITHDRAWN
        The other applicable labels are printed under it, in full.
        RULE 3b (NEW at iteration 8; resolves PH-03): **no label whose trigger includes an
        UNDECIDABLE comparison may outrank MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE.** A post hoc comparison that
        could not be resolved is not evidence, and it may not be headlined as a stronger negative
        than the undecidability it rests on.
RULE 4  Where a preregistered rule (v1.0 §20, §21 F1-F8) and a post hoc rule disagree about the
        same data, THE PREREGISTERED RULE CONTROLS — **IN THE FAILURE DIRECTION ONLY** (minor
        finding adopted). A PREREGISTERED FAILURE always controls and can never be overridden by a
        post hoc rule. A PREREGISTERED PASS NEVER overrides a post hoc UNDECIDABLE: the post hoc
        criteria are ADDITIONAL necessary conditions, so an unresolved one leaves the conjunction
        unsatisfied. The iteration-7 wording could have converted a post hoc UNDECIDABLE on S5 leg
        (a) back into v1.0's point-comparison HELD, which would have contradicted §20.9's own
        listing of the interval rule as a source of extra strictness. v1.1 may never be more
        permissive than v1.0, and this scoping is what makes that true in both directions.
RULE 5  EVERY label string carries its REGISTER SUFFIX, in every artifact, in
        `V1_1_OUTCOME`, in `branch_labels_applicable` and in every propagated copy:
            "(v1.0 §21 F7 — PREREGISTERED)"   for a label emitted by a preregistered rule
            "(POST HOC §1.1, via <criterion>)" for a label emitted by a post hoc rule
        A reader meeting the string "MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED" must be able to tell a preregistered
        decided failure from a post hoc comparison resting on 40 vs 4 kept blank reads. An
        unsuffixed label is non-compliant with this protocol.
```

```text
LABELS, each attached to its own condition, each reachable on its own

L-A  S4 fails while S1 holds, by v1.0's POINT rule
     -> "MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED (v1.0 §21 F7 — PREREGISTERED)". Binding.

L-B1 SOME S5 leg FAILS on an individual by the §16.2 DETERMINED interval rule — the comparator's
     lower limit exceeds B1's upper limit
     -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED (POST HOC §1.1, via S5) ON THAT INDIVIDUAL. The two arms ARE
        distinguishable, against B1: a methylation-blind filter's differential retention dominates
        B1's. NOT A SUCCESS.

L-B2 NO S5 leg FAILS, but at least one is UNDECIDABLE on an individual
     -> routes to L-H, MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE (POST HOC §1.1, via S5). NOT A SUCCESS and NOT a
        refutation. B1's differential retention is not DISTINGUISHABLE from a methylation-blind
        filter's at these counts — which is a statement about the resolution of the comparison,
        not about the mechanism.
     > The iteration-7 draft had ONE label L-B, triggered by "any leg FAILS or is UNDECIDABLE",
     > returning MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED. On identical data it fired together with L-H and RULE 3
     > headlined the stronger negative, so a post hoc UNDECIDABLE was published under v1.0's
     > PREREGISTERED label string — and §20.10 pre-committed to exactly that outcome. Its gloss,
     > "not distinguishable from what a methylation-blind filter achieves", was true for the
     > UNDECIDABLE trigger and FALSE for the FAILS trigger, where the arms are distinguishable.
     > The label is split, UNDECIDABLE is removed from the failure trigger, and RULE 3b prevents
     > the precedence ladder from recreating the problem (PH-03).

L-C  S6 leg (i) is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE in any cell on an individual, by the CENTRED family-wise
     rule of §15.5.8 (d), whose size and power are in §15.5.9 RESULT 4
     -> "MECHANISM REFUTED BY THE DENSITY CONTROL (POST HOC §1.1, via S6 leg (i))", on that
        individual. At matched kept-ALT count
        the CpG-density control beats B1 beyond the family-wise band: the gain is read
        composition, not methylation pattern. NOT A SUCCESS.

L-D  S6 leg (i) HOLDS but leg (ii) is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE
     -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED — PATTERN NOT ISOLATED. B1 beats the density arm but not a
        permutation that preserves density and destroys pattern. NOT A SUCCESS.

L-E  S6 leg (iv) is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE against ANY member of P
     -> "MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED — PATTERN-BLIND READ PROPERTY (POST HOC §1.1, via S6 leg (iv))".
        A filter knowing only ONE NAMED methylation-pattern-blind read property — alignment
        mismatch, footprint ambiguity, modification-call uncertainty, read length or mapping
        quality — matches or beats B1 at matched count. The MEMBER OF P that produced it must be
        named in the label wherever it is stated. This is the explanation MAJ-01 and MAJ-L1-01
        identified and §16.7 (a) measures at 8.15x in a blank. NOT A SUCCESS.

L-F  S6 leg (iii) is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE
     -> REFUTED BY THE PERMUTATION CONTROL. v1.0 F2's outcome, now at matched count.
        NOT A SUCCESS.

L-G  S5 and S6 both HOLD on one individual and not the other
     -> "MECHANISM NOT REPLICATED (POST HOC §1.1, via S7)". NOT A SUCCESS. Reported with
        §15.5.9's figures beside it, carrying their scope label verbatim — "leg (i), AXIS-L,
        pooled, both individuals" — namely RESULT 5's upper bound of <= 0.23 (independent blanks)
        or <= 0.47 (correlated blanks) at a 1.5x alternative, RESULT 3's measured size for the
        same rule, and RESULT 5's statement that the power of S6 as installed is unknown and
        strictly lower. §24 item 18 states what those figures permit to be concluded. Under
        CLAUSE 0b this label is unreachable on this data.

L-H  Fewer than 3 DISCRIMINATING evaluable targets in a cell; or zero DISCRIMINATING targets; or
     every positive leg-(i) target marked BLANK_TRIPLE under either construction (§15.5.4); or
     every DISCRIMINATING target ALL_UNRELIABLE (§16.3); or C3 uninformative or stratum-invalid
     per §10.5; or every member of P uninformative per §16.7 (c); or a permutation band
     inadmissible per §15.5.8 (c); or a bootstrap interval inadmissible or argmin-unstable per
     §15.5.8 (a); or L-B2 fires; or a leg that does not survive §15.5.10's inflation
     -> "MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE (POST HOC §1.1)". NOT A SUCCESS, and NOT a refutation. Recorded as
        a limit of this design, with the counts and the §15.5.9 operating characteristics that
        produced it. Propagates as WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE, never as a negative finding.

L-I  S1, S2, S3, S4 hold AND S5, S6, S7 all hold
     -> "MECHANISM NOT WITHDRAWN — POST HOC, NOT ESTABLISHED (POST HOC §1.1)" (§20.6 clause 0).
        **UNREACHABLE ON THIS DATA (§20.6 clause 0b).** It is retained as a named branch so that
        the map is complete and so that the rule under which it WOULD have fired is on the record,
        not because EXEC-002 can reach it.
        Required outputs of this branch, not optional: a PREREGISTERED follow-up protocol
        specifying the replication that would be needed to call the mechanism established, written
        before any further claim is made; and the propagation specified below, which for this
        branch is as binding as it is for a withdrawal. Scope-limited by §16.4: the detection
        outcome still changes in one of four cells.

L-J  Implementation faults (§21 F10-F13)
     -> execution is RETURNED to the executor, not interpreted. No verdict is issued.
```

**WITHDRAWAL, IN TWO DISTINCT FORMS THAT MAY NOT BE MERGED (resolves MAJ-L2-04, PH-03).** If the
headline is anything other than L-I, the mechanism half of H1 is WITHDRAWN — but *on what basis*
is part of the finding, and the iteration-7 draft swept an UNDECIDABLE into an affirmative
negative claim, which §24 item 18 and §15.5.9 point 5 of the same document forbid.

```text
WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE       headline is L-A, L-B1, L-C, L-D, L-E, L-F or L-G.
                            Sentence: "The mechanism half of H1 is WITHDRAWN ON EVIDENCE: <the
                            headline label with its register suffix> . The claim that methylation
                            identifies tumour-derived reads is not established by this benchmark
                            and is withdrawn from the project's evidence base."
WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE    headline is L-H (including via L-B2).
                            Sentence: "The mechanism half of H1 is WITHDRAWN AS UNDECIDABLE: this
                            benchmark did not resolve the comparison. The mechanism claim is NOT
                            ESTABLISHED BY THIS BENCHMARK AND IS NOT SHOWN FALSE. No negative
                            mechanism claim is made from a failure to clear a bar this design
                            cannot clear (§15.5.9, §24 item 18)."
IN EITHER CASE              the withdrawal's BASIS is §20.6 clause 0b — EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md —
                            and EXEC-002 determined only WHICH label applies. That sentence is
                            required beside the withdrawal, so the withdrawal is not attributed to
                            a sweep that could not have changed it.
```

The v1.0 registered outcome `H1 SUPPORTED` is NOT edited, NOT retracted and NOT relabelled — it is
reported verbatim (§20.0, §22.5) beside the withdrawal.

**PROPAGATION — BINDING FOR EVERY HEADLINE, NOT ONLY FOR A WITHDRAWAL (resolves PH-04).** A result
that lives only inside `results_v1_1/` has not been reported. As a required output of EXEC-002's
reporting step, the headline propagates to all four targets below. The iteration-7 draft bound
only the withdrawal branch, leaving the POSITIVE branch — where the post hoc qualifier is
load-bearing and most likely to be dropped in transit — with no rule at all.

```text
orchestration/research_state.yaml   `current_hypothesis` STATUS line; `known_evidence` and/or
                                    `contradictory_evidence`; `open_questions` (the two
                                    QC-01 / QC-02 questions are answered or recorded as
                                    undecidable)
research/benchmarks/BM-0001-methyl-gate-mrd/workflow_state.yaml   last_status, blocking_issues
FIND-0021 and its refs              the read-classification finding whose generalisation this
                                    benchmark was testing
research/research-os.json           wherever the mechanism claim is carried

WHAT IS WRITTEN, per branch:
  WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE / WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE
      the branch sentence above, VERBATIM, with the headline label AND its register suffix;
      the literal marker POST_HOC_2026-09-19;
      a pointer to §20.0's v1.0 block;
      §16.4's sentence naming the one individual x construction cell in which the detection
      outcome changed.
  L-I  (unreachable on this data, §20.6 clause 0b, but specified so the rule exists)
      the §20.7 label VERBATIM, with its register suffix:
          "MECHANISM NOT WITHDRAWN — POST HOC, NOT ESTABLISHED (POST HOC §1.1)";
      the literal marker POST_HOC_2026-09-19;
      an explicit pointer to the §20.0 v1.0 block;
      §16.4's sentence naming the one individual x construction cell in which the detection
      outcome changed;
      and the sentence: "A post hoc criterion cannot promote a claim. A PREREGISTERED replication
      is required before the mechanism may be called established."

BINDING, FOR EVERY BRANCH:
  **No v1.1 result may be entered into research_state.yaml `known_evidence`, into
  research/research-os.json, or into any FIND record without the POST_HOC_2026-09-19 marker and
  §16.4's restriction in the SAME entry.**
  research_state.yaml's `current_hypothesis` STATUS line currently reads that the mechanism half
  is UNDERDETERMINED because QC-01 showed S4 is satisfied identically by C2. That line may be
  REPLACED only by one of the branch sentences above, never struck in favour of a sentence saying
  the mechanism survived its controls — which is the edit §20.6 clause 0 exists to prevent.
```

### 20.8 Anti-goalpost-moving clauses (binding)

**EVERY FREEZE IN THIS SECTION IS RELATIVE TO EXEC-002 ONLY (resolves PH-02).** Every constant and
every rule frozen here is **POST HOC with respect to EXEC-001** (§1.1) and **none of it may be
described as preregistered.** The heading "FROZEN BEFORE EXECUTION" in clause 3 means *before
EXEC-002 runs*, not *before the result that motivated it was seen* — the result was seen, on
2026-08-31, and `qc_001.md` reported it. Clause 7 in particular is a choice made from §15.5.9's
model, which is calibrated on EXEC-001's realised counts, and it is registered as post hoc in §1.1
with the rest.

```text
1. The primary level does not move. 0.1% was fixed from the frozen prior record (v1.0 §15).
   Unexpected behaviour at 1% or 0.01% is a finding to report, never a reason to re-select.
   (v1.0 §20, carried over unchanged.)
2. The operating point does not move. PCT = 95 remains the operating point of S1-S5. The sweep is
   an ANALYSIS over the threshold. If the sweep shows some other threshold would serve B1 better,
   that is a FINDING TO REPORT and a candidate for a FUTURE PREREGISTRATION. It may not be adopted
   here, and no criterion in this protocol may be evaluated at any other operating point except
   S6, which is evaluated at matched count by construction.
3. FROZEN BEFORE EXECUTION (meaning before EXEC-002; see the heading clause), and part of the
   checksummed protocol: the target set T (§15.5.3); the ladder definition (§15.5.2); THE
   THRESHOLD TRANSFER RULE and KEEP_COUNT_TOL (§15.5.4, §15.5.2); the matching rule (§15.5.4); THE
   BLANK_TRIPLE DETERMINATION AND SAMPLE_SD_TOL (§15.5.4); every count floor (§15.5.6); the
   count-match tolerance and the AXIS-B mean tolerance (§15.5.7); B, alpha, the undefined-draw
   ceiling, THE BOOTSTRAP UNDEFINED-RESAMPLE CEILING, ARGMIN_UNSTABLE_MAX, THE `null_sd`
   ESTIMATOR AND THE CENTRED FAMILY-WISE REFERENCE (§15.5.8); the inflation factors AND THE RULE
   THAT f = 1 IS THE ONLY FACTOR AT WHICH A LEG MAY HOLD (§15.5.10); STRATUM_MIN and
   STRATUM_WIDTH_MAX (§10.1); RHO_MIN (§16.7 c); AND THE `region` CLASSIFICATION AND ITS CONSTANTS
   0.50 AND 0.80 (§15.5.11). The complete list with values is §1.1. None may be added, dropped,
   re-spaced or re-valued after any curve is seen.
4. Exclusion of a target by the count floor, by TARGET_UNREACHABLE, or by DEGENERATE_HIGH is never
   a pass and never improves a verdict. DEGENERATE_HIGH is equally never a refutation.
5. No criterion may be evaluated on the exploratory COLO829 chr1 arm.
6. If EXEC-002 fails to reproduce EXEC-001 at PCT=95, the discrepancy is investigated as an
   implementation fault before any sweep output is read.
7. The choice between S6 and S6-STRICT as the verdict-bearing rule is made HERE, before EXEC-002,
   on the operating characteristics of §15.5.9, and is frozen. It may not be revisited after the
   curves are seen, in either direction. THE FROZEN BASIS is
   `protocols/support/s6_operating_characteristics.py` together with its recorded SEED and the
   full output it produces — not the abridged printout in §15.5.9 (minor finding adopted). THIS
   CHOICE IS POST HOC and is registered in §1.1: it was made from a model calibrated on EXEC-001's
   realised counts, and §20.9 records that against iteration 6 it is EASIER in the support
   direction.
```

### 20.9 Strictness argument, criterion by criterion (required by qc_001.md rule 1)

The comparison that `qc_001.md` rule 1 governs is **v1.1 against v1.0**. Recalibration between two
post hoc drafts of v1.1 is a separate matter and is declared separately below.

```text
AGAINST v1.0 — the comparison that binds
S1, S2, S3   TEXT unchanged, verbatim. Neither easier nor harder — AND THEIR INPUTS ARE PINNED.
             S1's C1 term reads the PRESERVED 200-draw median `zlo_min_c1_200`, not the post hoc
             2000-draw vector, because a median over 2000 draws is not the median over 200 and
             substituting it would change a preregistered criterion's numeric input in an
             uncontrolled direction (§20.1, MAJ-L2-01). The iteration-7 line "S1, S2, S3
             unchanged, verbatim. Neither easier nor harder" was true of the TEXT and silent
             about the INPUT; it is corrected here.
S4           unchanged, verbatim, still evaluated, AND STILL A CONJUNCT OF §20.6, decided by
             v1.0's POINT rule, with clause (ii)'s band read from the PRESERVED 200-draw vector.
             Demoted only in what it LICENSES, which removes a claim rather than adding one.
S5           = S4 (both clauses, verbatim) AND (b) AND (c) AND (d), decided by interval dominance
             rather than by a point comparison. A conjunction with S4 as a conjunct, evaluated by
             a harder rule. Strictly harder.
S6           purely additive; no existing criterion relaxed. Strictly harder. Binds B1 at counts
             far from its locked operating point.
S7           purely additive. It does not raise the bar by itself — it makes the SPLIT outcome
             NAMEABLE so it cannot be reported as partial success. The iteration-6 claim that S7
             is "strictly harder" was false and is withdrawn; S7 is strictly ADDITIVE, which is
             what qc_001.md rule 1 requires of it.
C3, C4a, C4b new controls that can only produce new ways to fail.
§16.1-§16.7  reporting rules that constrain claims. They cannot create a pass. §16.2's interval
             rule replaces a point comparison with a dominance requirement: harder.
§15.5.6      a count floor that can only remove evaluable targets and, if it removes too many,
             forces UNDECIDABLE rather than a pass. Raised at iteration 7.
§15.5.10     a sensitivity requirement that can only disqualify a leg.
§20.6 cl. 0  a ceiling on the positive outcome that did not exist in iteration 6.
Net          every path from EXEC-002 to a non-withdrawal passes through strictly more necessary
             conditions than the path v1.0 defined, and the best available outcome is weaker than
             the one v1.0 already registered.

BETWEEN ITERATION 6 AND ITERATION 7 — declared, not smuggled
S6's verdict-bearing rule moved from the all-target intersection to the pooled statistic with
family-wise-controlled refutation. Against iteration 6 that is EASIER to satisfy in the support
direction and HARDER in the refutation direction. The reason is §15.5.9, computed before EXEC-002
and printed in this document: the intersection has power 0.026-0.051 against a real 1.5x advantage
and a spurious target-level failure rate under exact equivalence of 0.213-0.322 (MODEL-I) and
0.577-0.580 (MODEL-C), so neither of its outcomes was interpretable. (Iteration 7 quoted 0.026-0.051
and 0.17 from the symmetric-normal proxy; iteration 8's measurement of the rule as installed is
above.) The intersection is RETAINED and REPORTED as S6-STRICT, with ZERO verdict
weight and a binding citation rule (§20.4), and §20.8 clause 7 freezes the choice. Against v1.0,
S6 in either form is purely additive, so the binding constraint is untouched.
The iteration-7 claim that retaining S6-STRICT means "nothing is lost" is PRESENTATIONAL ONLY and
is withdrawn as an argument (minor finding adopted): a reported label with no verdict weight does
not replace a verdict-bearing one.

BETWEEN ITERATION 7 AND ITERATION 8 — declared, not smuggled
F9 IS MATERIALLY WEAKER THAN ITS ITERATION-6 FORM, and the iteration-7 draft declared the S6
recalibration but not this one (minor finding adopted). The iteration-6 F9 was a bare POINT
comparison, which FIRES on EXEC-001's HCC1395 numbers (C2 1.6839 > B1 1.6741). The interval form
will not fire on a gap of 0.009 of one standard error. WHY THAT IS SAFE, stated rather than
assumed: no escape results, because L-B2 routes any merely-UNDECIDABLE S5 leg to L-H, and §20.6
clause 0b independently closes the positive branch. The change converts an unresolvable
point-comparison "failure" into an honest UNDECIDABLE; it does not convert a failure into a pass.
THE REFUTATION DIRECTION IS STRICTLY HARDER TO REACH IN ONE SENSE AND STRICTLY EASIER IN ANOTHER,
and both are declared: §15.5.8 (d)'s centred rule can now FIRE AT ALL, where the iteration-7 rule
was structurally unfirable (SV-01), which makes refutation reachable rather than impossible; and
§15.5.9 RESULT 4 reports its size, which the iteration-7 rule never had. A rule that could not
fire was not "strict"; it was broken, and repairing it is not a loosening.
S6 LEG (iv) AND S5 LEG (d) ARE STRICTLY HARDER than at iteration 7: the max is taken over five
named comparators instead of two, and F12 fires on any of them.
LEG (ii) IS STRICTLY HARDER: exact-t strata plus a VALIDITY trigger that can only return
UNDECIDABLE, never HELD.
```

### 20.10 What would have happened to EXEC-001 under the new criteria (audit note, full precision)

Computed from EXEC-001's `results/ablation_results.tsv` and `results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`,
which are preserved unmutated. **This is not a v1.1 result** — S6 is not computable from EXEC-001's
saved outputs (§22.1) — and it is recorded only so that the direction of the redesign is
checkable. It is a **full-precision audit note, explicitly exempt from §16.5's display rule** under
that section's carve-out.

```text
S5 leg (a)  [= v1.0's S4, both clauses, DECIDED BY v1.0's POINT RULE — §16.2 names that rule
             explicitly for leg (a), so this audit note and §20.3 now answer the same question.
             The iteration-7 draft subjected "every S5 leg" to interval dominance in §20.3 and
             then adjudicated leg (a) by a point comparison here; that ambiguity is closed.]
            clause (i)  HCC1395 1.6741 > 1.0 : holds.  COLO829 2.9127 > 1.0 : holds.
                        (POINT rule. The harder INTERVAL reading — retention_ratio_lo(B1) > 1 —
                        is computed and reported as leg_a_interval_reading and is NOT
                        verdict-bearing, because S4 is preregistered.)
            clause (ii) C1's ratio is 0.857 with a 5-95 band of [0.00, 1.96] on HCC1395 and
                        1.161 with [0.32, 2.35] on COLO829 — the PRESERVED 200-draw band; both
                        contain 1, so C1 is NOT distinguishable from level-independence and
                        clause (ii) holds.
            -> leg (a) HELD, as v1.0 recorded.

S5 leg (b)  B1 vs C2, POINT values, both blank conventions:
            five-blank   HCC1395  1.6741 vs 1.6839   B1 LOWER   by -0.0098  (0.030 of one SE)
                         COLO829  2.9127 vs 2.9041   B1 higher  by +0.0086  (0.010 of one SE)
            three-blank  DIRECTION ONLY, from the counts in §16.2 and §20.3, because C2's
                         three-blank denominators are TWO and ONE kept blank read and §16.5
                         forbids displaying them as ratios (SV-06):
                         HCC1395  B1 LOWER than C2.   COLO829  B1 higher than C2.
            Under v1.1's INTERVAL rule these are decided by the intervals EXEC-002 computes, not
            by the point values. The margins are 0.009 and 0.004 of one standard error of the
            DIFFERENCE (§16.2, corrected), COLO829's three-blank C2 interval is expected to be
            INADMISSIBLE (§20.3), and the arms' intervals will certainly overlap — so the
            expected outcome is **UNDECIDABLE on both individuals, not FAILS on one**. Leg (b)
            therefore does not HOLD; no leg FAILS; so L-B1 does NOT fire and **L-B2 fires, routing
            to L-H**.
            EXPECTED HEADLINE, stated before EXEC-002 runs: **MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE (POST HOC
            §1.1)**, propagating as WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE — unless S6 produces a
            REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE label, which by RULE 3 would take precedence.
            > The iteration-7 draft wrote "EXPECTED HEADLINE: MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED" two sentences
            > after establishing that the expected outcome is UNDECIDABLE rather than FAILS. That
            > was a post hoc UNDECIDABLE published under v1.0's PREREGISTERED label string, and
            > §20.7's L-B split plus RULE 3b now prevent it (PH-03). The withdrawal itself is not
            > at issue: §20.6 clause 0b establishes it from EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md. What changes
            > is that an UNRESOLVED comparison is reported as unresolved.

            SEED SENSITIVITY, which is why leg (b) has a band at all (§10.6, MAJ-02):
            C2's HCC1395 blank kept counts are 0 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 — four reads. Had the tie-break
            kept ONE more blank ALT read on TF0 rep1 (1/25 instead of 0/25), C2's five-blank
            retention becomes 0.045706 and its ratio falls from 1.6839 to
            0.063492 / 0.045706 = 1.3891 — and B1's 1.6741 would WIN. One read, selected by a
            seed hashed with a filename, reverses the comparison. A criterion that reads that as
            evidence about mechanism is QC-01's failure mode restored, which is exactly why
            iteration 7 gives C2 a band and evaluates by interval dominance.

S5 leg (c)  C3 band       not computable from EXEC-001 — C3 did not exist.
S5 leg (d)  C4a/C4b       not computable from EXEC-001 — the arms did not exist.
S6          not computable from EXEC-001 — no per-read scores were stored (§22.1).
S7          not computable without S5 and S6 on both individuals.

CONTEXT FOR S6, from numbers already in hand: the whole observed advantage at the operating point
is delta_vs_C2 = 2.3725 (V1) and 3.6784 (V2) on HCC1395, and 0.5255 (V1) and 1.0340 (V2) on
COLO829 (results/ablation_results.tsv). §15.5.9's MODEL-I table puts the simulated sd of that
difference at 0.615-2.150 z-units at f = 1. COLO829's V1 advantage is under two thirds of one null sd.
```

So on the numbers already in hand, **the new criteria make the observed result harder to keep, S5
cannot hold on HCC1395 as a matter of arithmetic (§20.6 clause 0b), and the protocol's own
expected headline before EXEC-002 runs is MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE, propagating as
WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE.** No criterion in v1.1 was chosen in a direction that rescues
`H1 SUPPORTED`, and the expected outcome is on the record before the run rather than announced
after it. What EXEC-002 can still change is WHICH non-success label applies — in particular
whether any of the refutation labels L-C, L-D, L-E or L-F becomes eligible under §15.5.8 (d)'s
rebuilt, centred family-wise rule, which at iteration 7 could not fire at all.

## 21. Failure criteria

v1.0 §21 **F1-F8 are carried over verbatim, unchanged, and REMAIN BINDING.** That includes F7
("S4 fails while S1 holds -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED") and F8 (the C1 retention-band check).
**Where a preregistered failure criterion and a post hoc verdict rule disagree about the same
data, the PREREGISTERED criterion controls** (§20.7 RULE 4). The iteration-6 draft removed S4 from
its verdict conjunction while carrying F7 over verbatim, leaving two contradictory rules for the
same data and letting a reporter pick; §20.2 and §20.6 remove the contradiction by restoring S4 to
the conjunction, and this clause fixes the precedence in case any other disagreement is found.

F8 was evaluable in EXEC-001 given D4 and did not fire: C1's retention ratio is 0.857 with a 5-95
band of [0.00, 1.96] on HCC1395 and 1.161 with [0.32, 2.35] on COLO829, both bands containing 1 as
the permutation control's construction requires.

**F8 READS THE PRESERVED 200-DRAW BAND, `retention_ratio_p05_200` / `p95_200`, AND ONLY THAT
(MAJ-L2-01).** F8 is preregistered; the 2000-draw vector is post hoc; substituting it would change
a preregistered failure criterion's numeric input. Note that HCC1395's `p05 = 0.00` is driven by
degenerate draws which §15.5.8 (c) requires EXEC-002 to exclude or flag **in the post hoc 2000-draw
band only** — F8's band is reproduced with EXEC-001's behaviour intact, and the difference is
reported as `band_admissible_2000` beside it rather than folded into F8.

Six failure criteria are added. All are POST HOC (§1.1).

```text
F9   retention_ratio(C2) DOMINATES retention_ratio(B1) on either individual, under either blank
     convention, by the INTERVAL rule of §16.2 — that is, C2's lower limit exceeds B1's upper
     limit
         -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED. The differential-retention signature is not specific to
