```text
            methylation pattern.
     F9 uses the SAME interval that S5 leg (b) uses. The iteration-6 F9 was a bare point
     comparison of the two numbers QC-01 measured as a tie to within 0.3-0.6%, i.e. it would have
     fired on 0.01-0.03 of one standard error. That form is deleted.

F10  ANY of:
       - kept_ALT is not monotone non-decreasing along the ladder, in any arm or sample (V-b);
       - C1's realised ALT retention departs from its nominal keep fraction beyond its own
         5-95 band at any ladder point (V-d);
       - an arm's curve does not converge on B0_scorable's zlo_min at that arm's attainable
         maximum count n_max (V-c)
         -> IMPLEMENTATION FAULT in the sweep. Execution is RETURNED, not interpreted.

F11  EXEC-002 at the PCT = 95 ladder point does not reproduce EXEC-001, on these NAMED columns at
     these NAMED precisions:
         zlo_min                          exact to 4 decimal places
         z, z_lo, z_hi                    exact to 4 decimal places
         retention, retention_scorable_only, retention_blank, retention_level
                                          exact to 6 decimal places
         retention_ratio                  exact to 4 decimal places
         alt_total, alt_scorable, alt_unscorable, alt_kept, G, n_gate_pass,
         n_scorable_reads, K_B1, k_C2     EXACTLY, as integers — FOR THE DETERMINISTIC ARMS
                                          B0, B1, C2 ONLY.
         C1's alt_kept, G and sample_sd   exact to 4 decimal places, NOT as integers. `aggregate.py`
                                          lines 129-133 derive C1's per-sample values as
                                          `np.median` over the draws, and the median of an even
                                          number of integers need not be an integer, so demanding
                                          integer equality would trip F11 on C1 rows in an
                                          otherwise correct run (minor finding adopted).
                                          C1's values are computed from draws j = 0..199 (§23.2).
         cut                              exactly, as stored float64
         levels_detected_replicate_rule   exact string match
         every C1 aggregate column        read from the `_200` vector; a `_2000` column is never
                                          compared against EXEC-001 (§20.1, §23.2)
     EXPLICITLY EXEMPT, so that F11 and §16.5 cannot contradict: `sig_figs_allowed` and any
     DISPLAY-rounded rendering of a ratio. §16.5 governs display only (§16.5), so a display
     difference is not a reproduction failure and a reproduction check is not a display.
         -> IMPLEMENTATION FAULT. Execution is RETURNED, not interpreted. No v1.1 verdict is
            issued on a run that cannot reproduce the run it extends.

     INPUT-FORMAT BRANCH (NEW at iteration 8; required by X-01, because without it a live and
     foreseeable outcome had no route but "execution is RETURNED", which would strand the
     benchmark). EV-0052 records that the 28 inputs were converted BAM -> CRAM on 2026-09-12, that
     per-file `md5_sam_body` is preserved in the conversion metadata but was NEVER RECOMPUTED, and
     that whole-file fidelity is therefore not established. So a reproduction failure may be
     conversion-induced rather than sweep-induced. On any F11 failure the executor must FIRST
     run the §15.5.7 V-f INPUT REPRODUCTION check, which compares only quantities that do not
     depend on the sweep:
         V-f PASSES, F11 fails   -> IMPLEMENTATION FAULT in the sweep. Execution is RETURNED.
         V-f FAILS               -> INPUT-FORMAT FAULT. Execution is HALTED and REPORTED, not
                                    returned to the sweep author. The report names the affected
                                    samples and quantities, and recommends recomputing
                                    md5_sam_body for those files against EV-0052's recorded
                                    values. No v1.1 verdict is issued either way.
     Both outcomes are valid benchmark outcomes and neither may be re-described as a pass.

F12  S6 leg (iv) is REFUTATION-ELIGIBLE at any DISCRIMINATING target on either individual
     against **ANY MEMBER** of the declared pattern-blind set P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e} — that
     member matches or beats B1 at matched count, beyond the CENTRED family-wise band of
     §15.5.8 (d)
         -> MECHANISM UNSUPPORTED — PATTERN-BLIND READ PROPERTY. NOT A SUCCESS. A filter with no
            methylation PATTERN information whatever reproduces B1's advantage, so the advantage
            is not attributable to the methylation pattern on the read. **The member of P that
            fired must be named wherever F12 is stated.** F12 fires on ONE member; it does not
            require all five.
         A member that is UNINFORMATIVE under §16.7 (c), MAPQ_DEGENERATE, or uncomputable because
         NM and MD are both absent (§10.2) may still FIRE F12 — an uncorrelated comparator that
         beats B1 is a stronger result against the mechanism — but may never contribute a HOLD.

F13  The §15.5.7 V-a RECONSTRUCTION IDENTITY fails: rebuilding the PCT = 95 kept sets from the
     stored arrays does not reproduce the stored per-candidate kept_B1 and kept_C2 exactly, for
     every gate-passing candidate of every sample, with zero mismatches
         -> IMPLEMENTATION FAULT. Execution is RETURNED. No curve may be read. This is the check
            that actually tests the sweep's reconstruction logic; the aggregate-level equality
            that the iteration-6 draft used passes automatically under an unmodified code path.

F14  (a) A leg of S6 that HOLDS at sd inflation factor 1 reverses its ordering or becomes
         UNDECIDABLE at factor 11.7 (§15.5.10)
         -> that leg is SD-DEPENDENT and may NOT contribute to a positive S6. If no leg survives
            on an individual, S6 returns UNDECIDABLE on that individual and the result may not be
            reported as leaving the mechanism claim standing.
     (b) NEW at iteration 8 (MAJ-L2-02): a leg that does NOT HOLD at f = 1 but HOLDS at some
         f > 1
         -> that leg is INFLATION-ONLY and may NOT contribute to a positive S6 IN ANY FORM. The
            iteration-7 draft named only direction (a), leaving the complement unspecified and the
            unspecified reading permissive — and §15.5.10's algebra shows the inflation runs in
            B1's favour under V2 at matched counts by construction, so the permissive reading was
            also the biased one. f = 1 is the only factor at which a leg may HOLD.
```

Any of F1-F14 is a valid benchmark outcome and must be preserved verbatim. None may be
re-described as partial success. **A benchmark in which every outcome reads as success is not a
benchmark**; §21 exists so that this one cannot become that. **Nor is a benchmark in which no
outcome can fail**: iteration 8's single most important repair is §15.5.8 (d), because at
iteration 7 F12 and every refutation label were structurally UNREACHABLE (SV-01), so the strongest
negative outcome this design names could not have been emitted on any data. Iteration 7 added
three more ways for it to fail and none for it to pass — and §15.5.9 is the protocol's own evidence that iteration 6
had over-corrected into a design in which almost every outcome read as refutation, which is the
same defect wearing the opposite sign.

## 22. Expected outputs

v1.0 §22's tree is carried over. The following are added or amended. All of §22 is POST HOC
(§1.1) except where it restates v1.0.

### 22.1 Extended raw schema (REQUIRED — the sweep is not computable without it)

**The feasibility constraint, verified against `execution/raw/*.json` and
`execution/scripts/run_sample.py`:** EXEC-001's raw rows carry
`[depth, alt_total, alt_unscorable, alt_scorable_idx, kept_B1, kept_C2]` and sample-level
`[n_scorable_reads, K_B1, k_C2, cut, n_consensus_positions, n_confident_positions, ...]`.
`run_sample.py` computes `score_b1[name] = d/t` and `score_c2[name] = t` per read (lines 80-92)
and **discards both after forming the kept sets** (lines 119-143). The stored file therefore holds
counts and indices at one threshold and no per-read scores at any threshold. **A retention sweep
cannot be computed from the EXEC-001 raw outputs.** The 28 primary BAM passes must be re-run.

EXEC-002's `run_sample.py` must emit, per sample, **parallel arrays of length `n_scorable_reads`**,
indexed by the **same integer index space** the existing `alt_scorable_idx` uses (the name-sorted
list of scorable reads, `scorable = sorted(score_b1)`):

```text
score_b1_num[]   int    the per-read B1 score NUMERATOR   d  (discordant confident positions)
score_c2[]       int    the per-read C2 score            t  (confident positions) — the B1
                        score DENOMINATOR, already an int in EXEC-001
c2_order[]       int    the read's position in C2's tie-broken descending order under seed j=0
                        (seeded shuffle then stable sort by -score_c2, exactly the order
                        run_sample.py builds), so "kept by C2 at k" is exactly c2_order < k
c2_tie_rank[]    int    the read's rank WITHIN its score_c2 tie block, emitted as a DIAGNOSTIC.
                        It does NOT by itself reproduce the seed-j order, because a read's rank
                        within its j=0 tie block does not determine its order under j != 0. The
                        normative reproduction path is §23.2's: shuffle `list(range(n_scorable))`
                        under seed_j and STABLE-sort by -score_c2[i]; because Python's shuffle
                        permutes positions and the stored index space is order-isomorphic to the
                        name-sorted `scorable` list, that is exactly reproducible in aggregation
                        from indices alone (minor finding adopted).
density_bin[]    int    the §10.1 EXACT-t density stratum index, after §10.1 step 2b's merges
score_c4a[]      float64, repr round-trip   NM / query_alignment_length            (§10.2)
score_c4b_num[]  int    n_amb, the ambiguous-band position count                   (§10.2)
score_c4b_den[]  int    n_cov, the consensus-covered position count                (§10.2)
score_c4c_num[]  float64, repr round-trip   sum over the read's t confident-consensus positions
                        of u(p) = 1 - |2p/255 - 1|, the modification-call UNCERTAINTY numerator.
                        The denominator is score_c2[] = t.                          (§10.2 C4c)
query_alen[]     int    read.query_alignment_length — score_C4d, and C4a's denominator (§10.2)
mapq[]           int    read.mapping_quality — score_C4e                            (§10.2)
call_conf_hist[] int[8] per read, the count of its confident-consensus positions falling in each
                        of 8 equal bins of |2p/255 - 1|. This is the CALL-CONFIDENCE SUMMARY
                        required by MAJ-L1-01 item 4: it lets an untested member of the
                        pattern-blind class be diagnosed later without re-reading the inputs.
```

**TEN ARRAYS** (the iteration-7 text said "nine" and listed eight; the count is corrected here and
in §23.1, which derives its size estimate from it). Counting `call_conf_hist[]` as one array of
8-vectors: `score_b1_num`, `score_c2`, `c2_order`, `c2_tie_rank`, `density_bin`, `score_c4a`,
`score_c4b_num`, `score_c4b_den`, `score_c4c_num`, `query_alen`, `mapq`, `call_conf_hist` — twelve
entries, of which two (`score_c4b_den`, `c2_tie_rank`) are diagnostics.

**EXACT SCORES, not rounded ones (resolves X4).** `score_b1` is defined as
`score_b1_num[i] / score_c2[i]` evaluated in float64 — **literally the expression
`run_sample.py` evaluates (`d / t`)** — so it is bit-identical to EXEC-001's. The iteration-6
schema specified "float, 6 decimal places", which is lossy for a rational whose own order
statistics define the cut under a strict `>` rule: two distinct scores rounding to the same
6-decimal value become tied, and a tie at the cut is dropped rather than kept. `score_c4a` is the
only float stored directly and must round-trip through `repr`. **Thresholds are LADDER INDICES,
not floating-point percentiles** (§15.5.2), so no cut value is ever re-derived from a rounded
number, and the PCT = 95 slice is exact by construction. The executor must assert this by the
reconstruction identity of §15.5.7 V-a.

Plus, at sample level:

```text
schema_version               "1.1"
i95_b1, k95_c2               the EXEC-001 ladder indices, computed by run_sample.py's own
                             expressions, stored so the reproduction slice is unambiguous
attainable_alt_counts        per arm, FOR THE ARMS COMPUTABLE IN THE BAM PASS — B1, C2 at seed
                             j = 0, and every member of P: the sorted set of attainable kept-ALT
                             counts, and [n_min, n_max]                            (§15.5.2)
                             The STOCHASTIC arms' attainable sets are NOT computable here and are
                             computed and stored IN AGGREGATION, per draw for C1 and C3 and per
                             seed for C2 at j != 0, in c1_draws_v1_1.tsv, c3_draws.tsv and
                             c2_seed_draws.tsv (minor finding adopted; the iteration-7 schema
                             listed this as a BAM-pass key for every arm, which is not possible)
attainable_keep_fractions    per arm, per sample: the attainable global keep fractions phi and
                             the realised kept-scorable-read count at each, which §15.5.4's
                             threshold transfer requires                            (§15.5.2)
n_tied_at_k                  reads tied at C2's top-k boundary at k95               (§10.6)
density_bin_sizes            realised stratum sizes, length 10 or fewer
density_bin_score_min/max    within-stratum MIN and MAX of score_C2                 (§10.5)
density_bin_score_var        within-stratum variance of score_b1, per stratum, descriptive
score_b1_global_var          global variance of score_b1 over scorable reads, descriptive
n_alt_exchangeable           scorable ALT reads in cut-straddling strata, at i95     (§10.5)
n_reads_missing_NM           reads whose NM tag was absent; >1% flags NM_SPARSE      (§10.2)
n_reads_missing_MD           reads whose MD tag was absent; NM and MD both absent on a sample
                             means C4a is NOT COMPUTED there and every C4a leg is UNDECIDABLE
n_distinct_mapq              distinct score_C4e values; below 5 flags MAPQ_DEGENERATE (§10.2)
n_strata, n_merges           realised exact-t stratum count and the merges §10.1 step 2b made
stratum_width_max            max over realised strata of score_C2 MAX / MIN           (§10.5)
cons_cov_quantiles           the 5th / 50th / 95th percentile of per-position consensus coverage
                             over the confident set, which bounds C4b's approximate invariance
                             (§10.2)
input_format, input_path, input_md5_ref    the CRAM opened, and the decode reference's md5 (§6.1)
```

Everything else in the raw schema is **unchanged**, including every existing key, so EXEC-001's
outputs remain readable by the same code path and the two runs are directly comparable.

**Why this schema and not the alternatives.** Storing the arrays makes the whole sweep, both
permutation controls, all 2000 draws, the 200 C2 tie-break seeds, the bootstrap and every ladder
point computable **in aggregation, from one BAM pass per sample** — the property v1.0 §23 relied
on to get four arms from 28 passes. Storing only the ALT reads' scores would not suffice: C1 and
C3 permute over *all* scorable reads, and the cut at any ladder point requires the full score
distribution. Recomputing from the BAM at each ladder point would multiply the pass count.

**Size, measured rather than asserted.** `n_scorable_reads` is 58,879 on HCC1395 TF1e-3 rep1 and
76,594 on COLO829 TF0 rep1 (EXEC-001 raw). The twelve array entries of the block above at that
length are a few megabytes per sample as JSON — `call_conf_hist[]` is 8 small integers per read
and roughly doubles the per-sample figure — so of order 200 MB across 28 samples. `/big8_disk` currently holds **1.7 TB free of
27 TB (94% used)**, measured 2026-09-19. The addition is four orders of magnitude below the
headroom.

### 22.2 New result artifacts

All are written under `results_v1_1/` (§22.4).

```text
results_v1_1/
├── retention_sweep.tsv                  the full ladder, per individual x arm x ladder point
├── matched_retention_comparison.tsv     per individual x axis x target x construction x leg
│                                        x sd_inflation
├── c3_draws.tsv                         the full 2000-draw vector for C3
├── c1_draws_v1_1.tsv                    the full 2000-draw vector for C1 (EXEC-001's 200-draw
│                                        c1_draws.tsv is preserved unmutated under results/)
├── c2_seed_draws.tsv                    the 200 tie-break-seed vector for C2 (§10.6)
├── kept_read_d_t_joint.tsv              the (d, t) joint distribution of kept ALT reads (§16.7 b)
├── read_quality_coupling.tsv            §16.7 (a) enrichment and §16.7 (c) correlations
├── s6_operating_characteristics.tsv     §15.5.9's table as computed for EXEC-002's realised counts
├── criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv         S1-S7 and S6-STRICT, with posthoc, operating_pct and
│                                        branch_labels_applicable columns
├── statistical_tests.tsv                EXEC-001's single row copied in UNMODIFIED carrying
│                                        posthoc = PREREGISTERED_V1_0, plus the v1.1 named-method
│                                        rows (§17). results/statistical_tests.tsv is READ-ONLY
├── tag_precheck.tsv                     §14's measured NM / MD / MAPQ presence, per input
├── input_availability.tsv               §26's first check: every §6.1 path, its size, its index,
│                                        and the decode reference md5
└── registered_outcome_v1_1.txt          laid out per §22.5
```

`retention_sweep.tsv` columns. **The key is `individual x arm x ladder_index x
variance_construction`, and the level-dependent columns are explicitly marked**, because the
iteration-6 schema mixed a per-level column (`level`) with a per-individual one (`zlo_min`) under
one key and was ambiguous to read:

```text
PER-INDIVIDUAL (constant within the key):
    benchmark_id, individual, arm, ladder_index, pct_equivalent, keep_fraction, variance_construction,
    zlo_min, zlo_min_p05_2000, zlo_min_p50_2000, zlo_min_p95_2000, argmin_replicate,
    zlo_min_c1_200, zlo_min_p05_200, zlo_min_p95_200   (C1 rows only; the PRESERVED vector)
    n_draws_undefined, n_draws_floored, band_p95_mcse, band_admissible_2000,
    monotonicity_ok, converges_to_b0_scorable,
    v1_0_registered_outcome, detection_table_ref, posthoc
PER-LEVEL / PER-REPLICATE (one row per level x replicate, marked by the `level` and `replicate`
columns, which are part of the key for these columns):
    level, replicate, n_scorable_reads, cut_or_k, n_scorable_alt, kept_alt,
    retention, retention_scorable_only, G, sample_sd, blank_mean, blank_sd, z, lo, hi, verdict
```

`matched_retention_comparison.tsv` columns:

```text
benchmark_id, individual, axis, tau, variance_construction, leg, comparator, sd_inflation,
n_tau, ladder_index_B1, ladder_index_comp, pct_equiv_B1, pct_equiv_comp,
retention_B1, retention_comp, kept_alt_B1, kept_alt_comp, kept_alt_signed_diff,
kept_alt_B1_rep1, kept_alt_B1_rep2, kept_alt_B1_rep3,
kept_alt_comp_rep1, kept_alt_comp_rep2, kept_alt_comp_rep3,
kept_blank_B1_rep1..3, kept_blank_comp_rep1..3,
lo_B1_rep1, lo_B1_rep2, lo_B1_rep3, lo_comp_rep1, lo_comp_rep2, lo_comp_rep3,
paired_diff_rep1, paired_diff_rep2, paired_diff_rep3,
argmin_replicate_B1, argmin_replicate_comp,
z_B1, z_comp, delta_point, delta_lo, delta_hi, null_sd_obs, familywise_bar, refutation_eligible,

n_alt_exchangeable, c3_informative,
decided_by, region, evaluable, exclusion_reason, target_unreachable, attainable_range_B1,

```
ADDED AT ITERATION 8, and each is required on every row:
```text
keep_fraction_B1_level, keep_fraction_comp_level          the realised global keep fraction in the
keep_fraction_B1_blank1..3, keep_fraction_comp_blank1..3  evaluated sample and in each blank, and
kept_scorable_B1_level, kept_scorable_comp_level          the realised kept-scorable-read counts,
kept_scorable_B1_blank1..3, kept_scorable_comp_blank1..3  which V-e checks (§15.5.4, MAJ-L1-03)
cut_or_k_B1_level, cut_or_k_comp_level, cut_or_k_*_blank1..3

                                  value is an implementation fault (§15.5.7 V-e)
n_boot_undefined, n_boot_floored, boot_admissible         the bootstrap-side counterparts of the
boot_mcse_delta_lo, boot_mcse_delta_hi                    band-side columns (SV-03, minor finding)
argmin_unstable_frac_B1, argmin_unstable_frac_comp        SV-04's admissibility quantity
argmin_split                      1 where argmin_replicate_B1 != argmin_replicate_comp; the three
                                  paired_diff_rep columns must be printed beside any verdict on
                                  such a row (§15.5.7 step 5)
unreliable_frac, all_unreliable   §16.3's admissibility condition
sample_sd_B1, sample_sd_comp      the inputs to §15.5.4's symmetric BLANK_TRIPLE determination
comparator_member                 for leg (iv): WHICH member of P this row compares against,
                                  and its swept direction for C4d / C4e
comparator_rho                    that comparator's Spearman correlation with score_b1 over the
                                  scorable ALT reads, and `comparator_informative` (§16.7 c)
seed_only_p05, seed_only_p95, boot_only_p05, boot_only_p95, seed_dominated    (§10.6)
m_phi, m_phi_target, m_phi_abs_err                        AXIS-B's mean-attainability record
rule_size_model_c                 RESULT 3's MEASURED size of the pooled rule for THIS cell under
                                  the correlated-blank model, from EXEC-002's own re-run of
                                  s6_operating_characteristics.py. Required beside every HOLD
                                  (§15.5.9 item 2)
familywise_size_model_c           RESULT 4's measured size of the family-wise refutation rule for
                                  this cell, required beside every refutation label
refutation_eligible_p01           the same statistic against the 1st percentile of the centred
                                  min; a label eligible at the 5th but not the 1st is emitted
                                  "<label> — SIZE-QUALIFIED" (§15.5.9 RESULT 4)
v1_0_registered_outcome, detection_table_ref              §20.0's machine-readable discharge
```

`criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv` carries, per criterion per individual:
`criterion, individual, outcome, operating_pct, blank_convention, branch_labels_applicable,
posthoc`, with `branch_labels_applicable` holding the **complete** set of §20.7 labels that apply,
not the selected one.

**THE `posthoc` REGISTER IS TWO-VALUED AND PER ROW (resolves PH-05).** The iteration-7 rule
stamped `POST_HOC_2026-09-19` on every row of every v1.1 artifact, which mislabels the
PREREGISTERED S1-S4 rows of `criteria_evaluation_v1_1.tsv` and the copied-forward EXEC-001 row of
`statistical_tests.tsv`.

```text
`posthoc` takes exactly one of two literal values on EVERY criterion-level and test-level row,
never blank and never abbreviated:
    POST_HOC_2026-09-19     for S5, S6, S6-STRICT, S7, F9-F14 and every rule in §1.1
    PREREGISTERED_V1_0      for S1, S2, S3, S4, F1-F8 and for EXEC-001 rows copied forward
```

**COLUMN-LEVEL MANIFEST, for any artifact carrying BOTH registers on ONE row.** `posthoc` is a
per-row register, but `ablation_results.tsv` and `per_sample_metrics.tsv` put preregistered
quantities (`zlo_min`, `retention_ratio`, `levels_detected_replicate_rule`, `retention`) and post
hoc ones (`delta_vs_C3`, `delta_vs_C4*`, `retention_ratio_3blank`, `retention_ratio_lo/hi`,
`alt_enrichment_tf0`, `sig_figs_allowed`) on the SAME row, so whichever single value is written,
half the row is mislabelled. Therefore:

```text
Every artifact that mixes registers within a row MUST carry, as its FIRST line, a header comment
of the form
    # POSTHOC_COLUMNS: <comma-separated list of post hoc column names, verbatim>
listing every post hoc column by name. This is REQUIRED for results_v1_1/ablation_results.tsv and
results_v1_1/per_sample_metrics.tsv and for any other artifact in the same position. A reader or
a downstream tool partitions the row by that manifest; `posthoc` then registers the ROW's
criterion, where the row has one.
```

### 22.3 EXEC-002's versions of the v1.0 artifact schemas (folding in D3 and D4)

**Retitled at iteration 8 (minor finding adopted).** The iteration-7 heading, "Amendments to v1.0
artifacts", read as licensing edits to EXEC-001's files, which §22.4 forbids. Nothing here amends
an EXEC-001 artifact. These are the schemas of the files **EXEC-002 writes under `results_v1_1/`**.

**Both files below carry the `# POSTHOC_COLUMNS:` manifest line required by §22.2.**

`results_v1_1/per_sample_metrics.tsv` — v1.0's set, with these now **named in the protocol**
rather than added as deviations:

```text
... retention, retention_scorable_only, n_blank_kept, n_blank_kept_3, n_level_kept,
    sig_figs_allowed, alt_enrichment_tf0, ...
```

`results_v1_1/ablation_results.tsv` — v1.0's set, with D4's band columns and v1.1's intervals
named. **Every C1 band column is SUFFIXED** `_200` (the preserved vector S1, S4 and F8 read) or
`_2000` (the post hoc vector), and no unsuffixed C1 band column may be emitted (MAJ-L2-01):

```text
benchmark_id, individual, level, arm, variance_construction,
zlo_min, zlo_min_p05, zlo_min_p95,
delta_vs_B0, delta_vs_C1, delta_vs_C2, delta_vs_C3, delta_vs_C4a, delta_vs_C4b,
retention_blank, retention_level, retention_ratio, retention_ratio_3blank,
retention_ratio_lo, retention_ratio_hi, n_boot_undefined, boot_admissible,
retention_ratio_p05_200, retention_ratio_p95_200, retention_ratio_p05_2000,
retention_ratio_p95_2000, band_admissible_2000,
zlo_min_c1_200, zlo_min_c1_2000,
n_blank_kept, n_blank_kept_3, n_level_kept, sig_figs_allowed,
levels_detected_replicate_rule, criterion, criterion_outcome, posthoc,
v1_0_registered_outcome, detection_table_ref
```

`retention_ratio_3blank` and every `_lo` / `_hi` / `_2000` / `boot_*` column is POST HOC and is
named in this file's `# POSTHOC_COLUMNS:` manifest. `zlo_min`, `retention_ratio`, `retention`,
`retention_blank`, `retention_level`, `levels_detected_replicate_rule`, `zlo_min_c1_200` and
`retention_ratio_p05_200` / `p95_200` are PREREGISTERED quantities and are not.

**§16.5's display rule applies to the DISPLAY of `retention_ratio_3blank` and its relatives.**
Where the denominator rests on fewer than 5 kept blank reads the column carries the two raw counts
`n_blank_kept_3` and `n_level_kept` and the ratio cell is written `NOT_DISPLAYED`, not a number.

The verdict-to-prediction mapping (v1.0 §22, resolves B007) is **unchanged**: `prediction = 1` iff
`verdict == "detected"`; `UNRELIABLE`, `not detected`, `no interval` and `no estimate` all give
`prediction = 0`, with the three-way verdict retained in its own column.

### 22.4 Preservation requirement — ONE location, named

```text
EXEC-002 writes to  execution_v1_1/  and  results_v1_1/  and to NO OTHER LOCATION.
```

The iteration-6 draft listed the new artifacts under `results/` in §22.2 and required
`results_v1_1/` "or an equivalently distinct location" in §22.4. That is two contradictory
instructions plus a judgement call, in the section whose whole purpose is preservation. There is
now one location and no alternative.

**EXEC-002 may not overwrite any EXEC-001 artifact.** `results/registered_outcome.txt`,
`results/ablation_results.tsv`, `results/per_sample_metrics.tsv`, `results/c1_draws.tsv`,
`results/statistical_tests.tsv`, `execution/raw/*.json`, `execution/files_opened.txt`,
`execution/random_seeds.yaml`, `execution/inputs_manifest.tsv`, `execution/protocol_deviations.md`,
**`execution/scripts/`** and `protocols/v1.0_locked.md` are **read-only inputs** to this iteration
(§14 declares the aggregation-side reads).

**EXEC-002's OWN SCRIPTS ARE NEW FILES AT NEW PATHS** (minor finding adopted; the iteration-7
read-only list omitted `execution/scripts/`, and §22.1 said "EXEC-002's `run_sample.py` must emit"
without naming a path, which invited editing the EXEC-001 scripts in place and mutating the audit
trail that binding constraint (d) protects):

```text
execution_v1_1/scripts/run_sample.py     the §22.1 extended BAM/CRAM pass
execution_v1_1/scripts/aggregate.py      the §15.5 sweep, nulls and criteria
execution_v1_1/scripts/precheck.py       §26's input-availability and §14's tag precheck
execution/scripts/*                      READ-ONLY. Copied, never edited.
```

### 22.5 `registered_outcome_v1_1.txt` — the layout is NORMATIVE (resolves M6)

The iteration-6 draft required the file to OPEN with the §20.0 block. That block's last line is
`REGISTERED_OUTCOME: H1 SUPPORTED`, so the first machine-readable verdict line, and the first line
any reader or `grep` meets, would have announced an unqualified success in a file whose actual
finding may be that the mechanism half is withdrawn. The mandatory-context rule would have become
a mechanism for propagating the headline it exists to qualify.

```text
# BM-0001-methyl-gate-mrd — v1.1 outcome (POST HOC; see protocol §1.1)
V1_1_OUTCOME:        <the §20.7 headline label WITH its §20.7 RULE 5 register suffix>
MECHANISM_VERDICT:   <WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE | WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE |
                      NOT WITHDRAWN — NOT ESTABLISHED>
WITHDRAWAL_BASIS:    §20.6 clause 0b — EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md. EXEC-002 determined WHICH label
                     applies; it could not change the withdrawal.
POSTHOC:             POST_HOC_2026-09-19
BRANCH_LABELS_APPLICABLE: <the complete set, comma separated, EACH WITH ITS REGISTER SUFFIX,
                      §20.7 RULE 1 and RULE 5>
S6_POWER_SCOPE:      <§15.5.9's figure with its scope label verbatim, and RESULT 5's statement
                      that the power of S6 as installed is unknown and strictly lower>

<the withdrawal sentence of §20.7, in full, where applicable — ABOVE the fence>

--- BEGIN V1.0 PREREGISTERED OUTCOME (EXEC-001, PRESERVED VERBATIM) ---
<the §20.0 block, byte for byte, unedited>
--- END V1.0 PREREGISTERED OUTCOME ---

--- BEGIN V1.0 NAMED OUTCOMES (protocols/v1.0_locked.md lines 713-731, PRESERVED VERBATIM) ---
<the §20.0 named-outcome block, byte for byte, including the introducing line
 "Named outcomes, which may not be relabelled:">
--- END V1.0 NAMED OUTCOMES ---

<the §16.4 detection table, verbatim>
<the §16.3 per-replicate verdict tally, with unreliable_frac>
<the §16.6 S3 near-miss block>
<the §16.7 (a) ALT-enrichment diagnostic>
<the §18.1 LIBRARY-OF-ORIGIN limitation paragraph, verbatim>
```

No key may be ambiguous between the two blocks: the v1.0 block's `REGISTERED_OUTCOME:` key appears
only inside the fence, and the v1.1 verdict uses `V1_1_OUTCOME:`. The same fencing applies wherever
§20.6 requires the v1.0 block beside a verdict, in every artifact and in the evidence summary.

## 23. Execution constraints

v1.0 §23 is carried over: host, environment (Python 3.10, `pysam` 0.24.0, `numpy` 2.2.6,
`scipy` 1.15.3), read-only source BAMs, the `mrdz` import rule and SHA-256 recording, the `@PG`
chain and duplicate-flag capture, and the known-benign stderr that must be counted rather than
suppressed.

**The `mrdz` import rule extends to the sweep.** Every `z`, `lo`, `hi`, `blank_mean`, `blank_sd`
and verdict in `retention_sweep.tsv` and `matched_retention_comparison.tsv`, at every ladder point,
in every bootstrap resample and in every permutation draw, must come from the checksummed
`mrdz.score.blank_spread`, `mrdz.score.z_interval` and `mrdz.score.interval_verdict`.
**Reimplementation is forbidden**, including a vectorised re-derivation. The iteration-6 draft
stated the import rule only for v1.0's code path.

**ONE CARVE-OUT, and only one (resolves X-05).** §15.5.10's mandatory sd-inflation cannot be
obtained from `mrdz`, because `z_interval(sample_total, blank_totals, sample_sd)` derives `sd`
internally through `blank_spread(blank_totals)` and exposes no inflation parameter. The two lines
```text
    lo_f = z - f * (z - lo)
    hi_f = z + f * (hi - z)
```
computed on `z`, `lo` and `hi` **as returned by `mrdz.score.z_interval` at f = 1** are ARITHMETIC
ON mrdz OUTPUTS and are permitted. They are exact, because the half-width is linear in `sd_b`.
Nothing else is carved out. In particular, **rescaling `blank_totals` so that `blank_spread`
returns `f * sd_b` is FORBIDDEN**: it also moves `blank_mean`, and therefore moves `z` itself, so
the recomputed `lo` would be wrong — and a wrong inflation arithmetic could let a leg that should
be SD-DEPENDENT contribute to a positive S6.

### 23.1 Re-run cost, from EXEC-001's measured runtime

```text
Basis (execution/execution_log.md, EXEC-001):
    primary passes      941 s to 1,180 s each, 28 passes, two waves of 14 concurrent
    primary wall-clock  16:21 to 16:52  -> ~31 minutes
    exploratory         14 passes, 16:50 to 16:57
    total wall-clock    ~36 minutes; no timeout, no retry, no killed process

EXEC-002:
    THE INPUT FORMAT HAS CHANGED AND THE INHERITED PER-PASS FIGURE NO LONGER APPLIES (X-01).
        The 941-1,180 s/pass figures above were measured decoding ~82 GB BAMs by BGZF random
        access. The replacements are ~43 GB reference-compressed CRAMs (§6.1), and a pileup over
        3,925-4,000 discrete positions requires container decode against a reference, which is
        materially SLOWER per random access. The figures above are therefore an UNVERIFIED LOWER
        BOUND inherited from a different input format, and this protocol says so rather than
        budgeting against an input that no longer exists.
    NORMATIVE: the executor RUNS ONE PASS FIRST, on HCC1395 TF0 rep1, MEASURES it, and records
        the measured seconds-per-pass in execution_v1_1/execution_log.md BEFORE launching the
        remaining 27. The EXEC-002 budget is then stated from that measurement. If the measured
        figure exceeds 3x the inherited one, the executor records it as a deviation
        (`Methodology changed: NO`) and reports the revised wall-clock rather than reducing any
        frozen constant.
    28 primary CRAM passes with the §22.1 extended schema.   MEASURED, not assumed; the inherited
                                                             lower bound is ~31-35 min wall at
                                                             14-way concurrency
        The extra per-pass work beyond EXEC-001 is: reading the NM tag (or MD where NM is absent),
        reading query_alignment_length and mapping_quality off records already fetched, retaining
        the ML probability byte `ref_calls` already reads before it thresholds at ML, counting
        ambiguous-band positions, and serialising the arrays of §22.1 already held in memory. That
        is serialisation and field reads, not computation. The CRAM decode, not the new arrays, is
        the term that is not yet measured.
    1 determinism re-run pass on one sample (§23.2, §26).                  ~16-20 min wall
        Present in the iteration-6 checklist and absent from its budget. It is now budgeted.
    0 exploratory passes. §19: the exploratory arm is NOT re-run.
    Aggregation, CPU-only, no I/O beyond the declared reads of §14:
        the deterministic arms over the full ladder, vectorised over the stored index arrays;
        2000 draws for EACH of C1 and C3, each draw spanning all 14 samples of an individual,
        each draw matched to every target on its OWN ladder (§15.5.5);
        200 C2 tie-break seeds, which reorder a stored index array and cost nothing;
        2000 bootstrap resamples, joint across targets (§15.5.8 a);
        4 sd-inflation factors, which are a scalar multiplication of an already-computed term.
        Raising B from 200 to 2000 multiplies the permutation cost tenfold against the
        iteration-6 estimate; the bootstrap is a new cost of the same order.
        Budget <= 2 hours and REPORT THE MEASURED FIGURE. If aggregation exceeds 2 hours the
        executor records it as a deviation rather than reducing B; B is frozen by §20.8 clause 3.

Total EXEC-002 budget: under 3 hours wall-clock ON THE INHERITED PER-PASS FIGURE, which is a lower
bound for an input format that no longer exists. **The budget is REPORTED FROM THE MEASURED FIRST
PASS, not assumed.** A re-run is affordable on any plausible CRAM decode penalty; what this
protocol states is the cost and its uncertainty, rather than assuming the sweep is free.
```

The iteration-6 budget of "200 draws x 17 grid points" is superseded: there is no grid and no
adaptive refinement, so the per-draw point count is bounded by the ladder, which is bounded by
`n_scorable_reads`. In practice only the ladder points bracketing each target's attainable count
need evaluating, which the executor may exploit provided `retention_sweep.tsv` still reports the
full curve at a stated stride and the reported stride is recorded.

### 23.2 Concurrency and determinism — the AS-EXECUTED derivations, quoted (resolves X3)

- Concurrency cap **14**, unchanged.
- **The iteration-6 draft stated that "C1's draw seeds `20260831 + j` are unchanged". That is not
  what EXEC-001 did**, and reproducing what the draft described would have made F11 fire on a
  correct run. The as-executed derivations, quoted from `execution/scripts/aggregate.py` lines
  101-106, `execution/scripts/run_sample.py` lines 110-117 and `execution/random_seeds.yaml`:

```text
C2 TIE-BREAK (unchanged; seed index j = 0)
    seed = int( sha256( '20260831|' + basename(bam) ).hexdigest()[:12], 16 )
    ** basename(bam) IS THE ORIGINAL .bam BASENAME, e.g. 'TF0_25x.rep1.bam', NOT the .cram
    basename of the file EXEC-002 actually opens. The 2026-09-12 conversion changed the extension
    (§6.1); hashing the new basename would change the seed, change C2's tie-break, and fire F11 on
    an otherwise correct run. EXEC-002 constructs the j = 0 seed string from the BAM basename
    recorded in execution/inputs_manifest.tsv and records both basenames per sample (§22.1
    `input_path`). This is normative and is checked by §26. **
    rng  = python random.Random(seed)
    order = list(scorable);  rng.shuffle(order);  order.sort(key = lambda nm: -score_c2[nm])
    (stable sort, so ties keep the shuffled order);  keep = set(order[:k])

C2 TIE-BREAK BAND (new, §10.6; seed indices j = 1..199)
    seed_j = int( sha256( '20260919|C2|' + basename(input) + '|' + str(j) ).hexdigest()[:12], 16 )
    everything else identical, including the stable sort.
    REPRODUCED IN AGGREGATION FROM INDICES ALONE, stated explicitly because `c2_tie_rank[]` alone
    cannot do it (minor finding adopted):
        order = list(range(n_scorable));  random.Random(seed_j).shuffle(order)
        order.sort(key = lambda i: -score_c2[i])      # stable
        keep = set(order[:k])
    This is exact, because Python's `shuffle` permutes POSITIONS and the stored index space is
    order-isomorphic to the name-sorted `scorable` list `run_sample.py` builds. Distributionally
    the rank-based shortcut agrees — a uniform shuffle followed by a stable sort induces a uniform
    order within each tie block — so no reported number moves in expectation; the shortcut is
    nonetheless not the normative path and `c2_tie_rank[]` is a diagnostic (§22.1).
    NOTE: `basename(input)` is the CRAM basename under §6.1. For j = 0 the seed string is
    EXEC-001's `'20260831|' + basename(bam)` with the BAM basename, reproduced byte for byte, so
    the F11 slice is unaffected by the format change. The executor records both basenames.

C1 PERMUTATION (unchanged in FORM; B raised from 200 to 2000 by §15.5.8)
    seed = int( sha256( '20260831|C1|' + individual + '|' + str(j) ).hexdigest()[:12], 16 )
    rng  = numpy.random.default_rng(seed)
    ONE generator per (individual, draw index j), ADVANCED OVER THAT INDIVIDUAL'S SAMPLES IN A
    FIXED ORDER — the `keys` list sorted by (LEVELS index, replicate) — drawing
    rng.permutation(n) once per sample. This single-generator-advanced-in-order structure is
    part of the definition and changing it changes every C1 number.
    Draws j = 0..199 MUST reproduce EXEC-001's C1 values exactly (F11). Draws j = 200..1999 are
    new and extend the same stream structure with the same derivation.
    THE TWO AGGREGATIONS ARE EMITTED SEPARATELY AND NEVER MIXED (MAJ-L2-01):
        `_200`  columns  = the median / percentiles over j = 0..199, with EXEC-001's silent-drop
                           behaviour on NA draws intact. S1, S4 clause (ii), F8 and every
                           F11-checked C1 column read THESE and only these.
        `_2000` columns  = the median / percentiles over j = 0..1999, with §15.5.8 (c)'s
                           undefined-draw exclusion and admissibility ceiling applied. S5 leg (c),
                           S6 leg (iii) and the permutation nulls read THESE and only these.
    No artifact may emit an unsuffixed C1 aggregate column. An executor who computes C1 over 2000
    draws and feeds the result to F11 will fire F11 on a correct run; that is the contradiction
    this split removes.

C3 PERMUTATION (new, fully specified)
    seed = int( sha256( '20260919|C3|' + individual + '|' + str(j) ).hexdigest()[:12], 16 )
    rng  = numpy.random.default_rng(seed)
    ONE generator per (individual, draw index j), advanced over that individual's samples in the
    SAME fixed order as C1, drawing one within-stratum permutation per sample: for stratum s in
    ascending stratum index, rng.permutation(len(s)) applied to that stratum's score_b1 values.

BOOTSTRAP (new, §15.5.8 a)
    seed = int( sha256( '20260919|BOOT|' + individual + '|' + str(b) ).hexdigest()[:12], 16 )
    rng  = numpy.random.default_rng(seed); one generator per (individual, resample index b),
    advanced over samples in the same fixed order; the C2 tie-break seed index for that resample
    is rng.integers(0, 200) drawn FIRST, before the candidate resampling.
```

- **ONE PERMUTATION PER (draw, sample), REUSED AT EVERY LADDER POINT.** For each (draw j, sample)
  exactly one permutation is drawn and then evaluated at every ladder point needed: for C1 the
  permuted mask is thresholded at each required `K`; for C3 the permuted score vector is compared
  against each required cut. An executor who draws a fresh permutation per ladder point produces a
  different, equally "seeded" C1 and breaks the PCT = 95 reproduction. This is normative.
- Determinism requirement: two runs of EXEC-002 on the same inputs must produce identical outputs.
  The executor verifies this on one sample by a full re-run pass (budgeted in §23.1) and records
  the check.

## 24. Known limitations

v1.0 §24 items 1-10 are carried over **verbatim and unchanged**. Ten are added.

```text
11. (QC-03) The registered outcome is about zlo_min. The DETECTION outcome under the frozen
    replicate rule changes in exactly ONE of four individual x construction cells: HCC1395 under
    V2. §16.4 makes reprinting the table mandatory.

12. (QC-04) COLO829's B1 statistic runs on single-digit counts: blank totals 2 / 0 / 4 / 3 / 6 and
    0.1% totals 9 / 19 / 18. One blank replicate yields G = 0, hence sample_sd = 0, hence the
    `no estimate` verdict — the case v1.0 §15 defines a priori. §16.2's interval rule and
    §15.5.6's floor are the design response; they do not make the counts larger.

13. (QC-05) S3 holds as written. Two COLO829 blanks return UNRELIABLE under V2 (rep3
    [-1.64, +3.15], rep5 [+0.69, +4.31]). Neither is a false detection. §16.6 requires this beside
    every statement of S3.

14. Everything listed in §1.1 is POST HOC. It can qualify or withdraw v1.0's conclusion; it cannot
    be reported as preregistered, and no result derived from it may be presented without the v1.0
    preregistered outcome beside it in full (§20.0, §22.5). A reader is entitled to discount a post
    hoc criterion. §20.9 shows every added criterion is a further necessary condition against v1.0,
    and §20.10 shows the mechanism criterion already fails on the data in hand.

15. The matched-count comparison degenerates at both ends of the retention axis. §15.5.11 defines
    the region rule numerically and §20.8 clause 3 freezes it, but the NUMBER of DISCRIMINATING
    targets remains data-dependent and is reported. If it is zero the verdict is UNDECIDABLE.

16. C3 excludes CpG density at STRATUM resolution only. It does NOT hold fixed: per-read basecall
    or modification-call error rate, read length, local coverage, allele-specific germline
    methylation, or mapping quality. C4a and C4b address two of those; the rest are UNCONTROLLED
    in this design and no v1.1 result may be read as excluding them. §18.1 is the binding
    statement.

17. v1.0 §24 item 3 says the pseudo-blank interval is optimistic "equally, for every arm". THAT
    CLAUSE DOES NOT HOLD for v1.1's retention-matched cross-arm comparison. The interval
    half-width is t95(2) * sd_b / sqrt(3) / sample_sd, and at matched retention the arms differ
    precisely in their blank counts, which is the input to sd_b and, under V2, to sample_sd. The
    understatement therefore enters each arm's lower bound by a different factor and does not
    cancel. EV-0036 measures the factor at 11.7 (between-library sd 89.08 against the five
    pseudo-blanks' 7.64); EV-0045 adds that the three TF0 replicates share ~90% of their normal
    reads on HCC1395 and ~45% on COLO829, so the effective replicate count is nearer one than
    three. §15.5.10 is the mandatory sensitivity analysis and §21 F14 the consequence.

18. RESOLUTION LIMIT, computed before EXEC-002 (§15.5.9), WITH ITS SCOPE LABEL ATTACHED. The
    figures are for **leg (i), AXIS-L, pooled** — ONE of the 4 legs x 2 axes x 2 constructions
    that §20.4 installs — and they are: power 0.46-0.59 (independent blanks) or 0.61-0.78
    (correlated blanks) at 1.5x and 0.78-0.87 or 0.87-0.95 at 2x, against SIZES of 0.09-0.18 and
    0.27-0.34; and an UPPER BOUND on a positive result on BOTH individuals of <= 0.23 or <= 0.47
    at 1.5x (§15.5.9 RESULT 3 and RESULT 5, f = 1). Iteration 7's "of order 0.06" came from a
    symmetric-normal proxy under three independent blanks and is SUPERSEDED. **THE POWER OF S6 AS INSTALLED IS UNKNOWN AND STRICTLY LOWER** (§15.5.9 RESULT 5),
    and no artifact may quote the leg-(i) figure as the criterion's. THIS DESIGN CANNOT RESOLVE A
    MODEST TRUE EFFECT. A non-holding S6 is therefore routed to UNDECIDABLE unless the refutation
    side clears its own family-wise band (§15.5.8 d, §20.7 L-H), and **no negative mechanism claim
    may be made from a failure to clear a bar this design cannot clear** — which is why §20.7
    separates WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE from WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE.

    WHAT THE POWER FIGURE PERMITS TO BE CONCLUDED, stated here rather than asserted inside a
    criterion (AGENTS.md rule 8; moved from §20.5). A `NOT REPLICATED` split between the two
    individuals is, at these counts, a likely outcome under a real modest effect as well as under
    no effect. The figure therefore permits the conclusion that **a split does not discriminate
    between those two states**; it does not permit the conclusion that the mechanism is present,
    and it does not permit the conclusion that it is absent. The figure must be printed beside
    L-G with its scope label, and this sentence is what it licenses.

    WHY EXEC-002 IS STILL WORTH ITS BUDGET, re-answered under §20.6 clause 0b. The iteration-7
    justification rested partly on "the size is controlled at 0.05 in both directions", which was
    the MODEL-I, f = 1, three-independent-blanks figure and is REMOVED (SV-05); §15.5.9's MODEL-C
    table carries the design's stated size. What remains, and is sufficient:
      (a) EXEC-002 determines WHICH non-success label applies, and in particular whether any
          REFUTATION label becomes eligible under §15.5.8 (d)'s rebuilt centred rule — a direction
          that at iteration 7 could not fire at all, so it has never been tested;
      (b) a detectable alternative exists (the 2x column, and COLO829's non-differential-C2
          column);
      (c) the §16.7 diagnostics — the (d, t) joint distribution of kept ALT reads, the TF0
          ALT-enrichment ratio, and the score correlations against the whole of P — are the
          material the next PREREGISTRATION needs, and none of them exists today;
      (d) the sweep is the only way to address QC-02's question at all.
    What EXEC-002 may NOT do is change the withdrawal, which §20.6 clause 0b settles in advance.

19. score_b1 = d / t is a per-read DISCORDANCE rate and is measurably coupled to a read's
    propensity to carry a spurious ALT base: B1 enriches ALT-carrying reads 8.15-fold over its
    global keep fraction in a TF0 blank with no tumour in it (§16.7 a). At EXEC-001's cut of
    exactly 1/13 on HCC1395, any read with t <= 12 and a SINGLE discordant call is kept. §16.7
    makes both facts standing outputs. C4a and C4b test the confound; they do not remove it, and
    no result may be reported without §16.7 (a) beside it.

20. The 200 C2 tie-break seeds, the 2000 permutation draws and the 2000 bootstrap resamples all
    address SAMPLING and TIE-BREAK variability within this dataset. NONE of them addresses the
    single effective blank (EV-0014, EV-0017, EV-0045) or the two-individual sample size. **Every
    interval this protocol computes is CONDITIONAL ON THIS BLANK LIBRARY**: the candidate
    bootstrap resamples WITHIN samples, and no resampling of a library sequenced once can generate
    between-library variance (SV-05). That scope must be stated wherever an interval is stated. No
    limit of blank, limit of detection, or generalisation beyond these two cell lines is claimed
    or claimable.

21. LIBRARY OF ORIGIN IS UNCONTROLLED, and it is the limitation that bounds every mechanism
    statement this benchmark can make (MAJ-L1-01). v1.0 §6 builds the mixtures by subsampling
    tumour into that individual's OWN MATCHED NORMAL, so tumour reads and normal reads come from
    TWO DIFFERENT LIBRARIES. Any read property that discriminates library of origin — read length,
    basecall quality, modification-call confidence, fragment-end composition, or a property not
    named here — produces BOTH differential retention AND a matched-count z advantage, with no
    tumour biology involved. P's five members test five NAMED pattern-blind properties; the class
    is OPEN and no arm in this design is a library-of-origin control. **NO v1.1 OUTCOME,
    INCLUDING THE MOST POSITIVE ONE, DISTINGUISHES "methylation identifies tumour-derived reads"
    FROM "some read property identifies the spiked-in library."** §18.1 is the binding statement
    and it must be reprinted beside any mechanism statement (§22.5).

22. THE INPUTS ARE NOT THE FILES EXEC-001 READ, and their fidelity is asserted rather than
    verified (X-01, EV-0052). All 28 BAMs were converted to CRAM on 2026-09-12 with
    `store_md=1 store_nm=1`, against a reference whose md5 matches the one BM-0001 used. The
    conversion metadata records `md5_sam_body` per file and lists mm_tags among the verified
    properties, but **per-file `md5_sam_body` was never recomputed**, so whole-file fidelity is not
    established; 30 sampled reads per file carried MM, which establishes decodability and tag
    presence only. The design response is §15.5.7 V-f (an exact reproduction check on every
    sweep-independent quantity) and §21 F11's INPUT-FORMAT branch. Consequences that do not
    disappear: NM and MD on the decoded records may be products of the conversion rather than
    original annotations, which is why §14's tag precheck MEASURES them; and the CRAM headers
    declare @RG entries the source BAMs did not, which EV-0052 records as an incidental finding
    bearing on open question C1 and which is NOT used by anything in this protocol.
```

## 25. Unresolved decisions

v1.0 §25's U1, U2 and U3 are carried over unchanged, none marked
`Must resolve before execution: YES`. Three are added.

```text
U4:
Question: Is the density-stratified permutation C3 informative on these samples, AND is density
          actually held fixed at the realised stratum resolution?
Why it matters: these are two different questions and the iteration-7 draft asked only the first.
          §10.1 now stratifies on the EXACT integer score_C2, merging only to clear
          STRATUM_MIN = 30 reads, but the realised merges are data-dependent: `t` spans more than
          an order of magnitude on ONT reads, so the high-t tail may merge into wide strata, and
          within a wide stratum `d/t > cut` prefers low-t reads by arithmetic alone.
Required evidence: n_alt_exchangeable (POWER), and stratum_width_max, n_strata, n_merges and the
          per-stratum [t_min, t_max] against 1/cut (VALIDITY), per sample, emitted by §22.1 and
          checked by §10.5's two triggers.
Must resolve before execution: NO — both checks are outputs and §10.5 fixes the response
          (leg (ii) UNDECIDABLE, never HELD and never a refutation) in advance.

U5:
Question: Does the common evaluable window contain enough DISCRIMINATING targets on COLO829, whose
          scorable-ALT counts at 0.1% are 51 / 55 / 63 and whose B1 blank totals are 2 / 0 / 4?
Why it matters: it determines whether COLO829 can carry S7 at all. §15.5.6 clause (b) requires all
          three blank replicates to be non-empty, and COLO829 B1's rep2 is empty at the operating
          point, so the answer may well be no.
Required evidence: the count floor of §15.5.6 applied to the EXEC-002 sweep; §15.5.9's simulated
          P(evaluable) column is the advance estimate (0.011 at tau = 0.10 rising to 0.991 at
          tau = 0.70).
Must resolve before execution: NO — the response is fixed in advance: fewer than 3 evaluable or
          zero DISCRIMINATING targets gives MECHANISM UNDECIDABLE on that individual (§20.7 L-H).

U6:
Question: Does the declared pattern-blind set P = {C4a, C4b, C4c, C4d, C4e} cover the confounds
          that matter, and which computable twins remain untested?
Why it matters: P has five named members and the class is OPEN (§10.2, §18.1). A B1 win over all
          five licenses less than "no read-property explanation", and licenses nothing at all
          about library of origin (§24 item 21).
CORRECTION TO THE ITERATION-7 REASONING (minor finding adopted). U6 previously concluded that
          "the structurally exact twin of score_b1 is not computable" from the single premise that
          `modified_bases` supplies CpG calls only. **That premise rules out ONE candidate twin —
          disagreement at confident NON-CpG positions — and does not establish that no computable
          twin exists.** It does not, and the panel produced one: mean per-read modification-call
          UNCERTAINTY, computable from the same ML probability byte `ref_calls` already reads,
          invariant under flipping any call to its confident opposite, and correlated with d/t by
          construction. It is now installed as C4c (§10.2). The iteration-7 reasoning would have
          been read as licence for a comparator set that omitted the generating property, which is
          QC-01's failure mode one level down. WHAT REMAINS OPEN is the question above, not the
          non-existence of a twin.
Required evidence: §16.7 (c)'s Spearman correlations of score_b1 against EVERY member of P, over
          the scorable reads and over the scorable ALT reads separately; and §22.1's stored
          `query_alen[]`, `mapq[]` and `call_conf_hist[]`, which let an untested member of the
          class be diagnosed later without re-reading the inputs.
Must resolve before execution: NO — the response is fixed in advance: a member below RHO_MIN
          returns UNDECIDABLE for its contribution to legs (d) and (iv), never HOLDS (§16.7 c).

U7 (NEW at iteration 8):
Question: Is the 2026-09-12 BAM -> CRAM conversion lossless for this benchmark's quantities?
Why it matters: EV-0052 records that `md5_sam_body` is preserved in the conversion metadata but
          was NEVER RECOMPUTED, and that decodability and MM-tag presence were verified on 30
          sampled reads per file, which is not whole-file fidelity. The inputs are not the files
          EXEC-001 read (§6.1, §24 item 22).
Required evidence: §15.5.7 V-f, an exact reproduction of every sweep-independent per-sample and
          per-candidate quantity against EXEC-001's stored raw, run BEFORE any curve is read.
Must resolve before execution: NO — V-f is the first thing EXEC-002 runs after the passes, and
          §21 F11's INPUT-FORMAT branch fixes the response in advance: a V-f failure HALTS and is
          REPORTED as an input-format fault, and is not returned to the sweep author as an
          implementation fault it is not.
```

---

```text
HANDOFF_STATUS: READY_FOR_REVIEW

Next role:
Adversarial reviewer (grill-me), review iteration 8, against this draft.

This is protocols/v1.1_draft.md. It is a DRAFT and is NOT locked. It does not supersede
protocols/v1.0_locked.md; v1.0 and the whole of EXEC-001 are preserved unmutated and are
read-only inputs to this iteration.

What a reviewer should attack first at ITERATION 8, named by the designer rather than left to be
found:

  1. §15.5.9 is still the load-bearing new content, and it now decides more than before: the
     design's STATED SIZE comes from its MODEL-C (correlated-blank) table, and §15.5.8 (d)'s
     rebuilt refutation rule is admitted only because RESULT 4 gives it operating characteristics.
     The model's approximations are enumerated in the script's docstring — in particular the inner
     candidate bootstrap is approximated IN COUNT SPACE rather than per read. Is that
     approximation adequate for the quantity it is being used to justify, and is MODEL-C's
     "effective blank count 1" the right conservative substitution for EV-0045's 90% / 45% read
     sharing, or does it overshoot?

  2. §15.5.4's GLOBAL KEEP FRACTION threshold transfer is the iteration-8 choice that decides
     every AXIS-L leg, and lens 4 at iteration 7 preferred the cut-VALUE transfer instead. The
     rejection argument is that a cut does not exist for C2 and a mixed rule reintroduces QC-02's
     defect at the blank end. Is that argument right? And does matching the GLOBAL keep fraction
     rather than the kept-ALT count in the blanks introduce a mismatch of its own?

  3. C4c (§10.2) is installed on a stated invariance proof: u(p) = u(255 - p), so score_C4c is
     unchanged when any call is flipped to its confident opposite. Is that proof correct at the
     boundary, is the arm actually computable from what `rd.modified_bases` returns for these
     files, and does adding three arms to P make leg (iv) harder for B1 or merely noisier — five
     comparators at these counts is five chances for one of them to win by accident, and the
     family-wise control of §15.5.8 (d) is per LEG, not across members of P.

  4. §20.6 clause 0b says the positive branch is arithmetically closed and that the withdrawal is
     already established by EXEC-001 plus qc_001.md. Is that the right reading of F11 plus §16.2,
     or does it concede too much in advance — and is the WITHDRAWN-ON-EVIDENCE /
     WITHDRAWN-AS-UNDECIDABLE split a real distinction or a softening?

  5. §16.3's ALL_UNRELIABLE condition and §16.7 (c)'s RHO_MIN condition and §15.5.8's
     ARGMIN_UNSTABLE_MAX and the bootstrap admissibility ceiling are four new routes to
     UNDECIDABLE. Together with §10.5's two triggers, is there now ANY data configuration in which
     S6 can HOLD — and if not, has the design crossed from "strictly harder" into "unsatisfiable",
     which is the reverse failure lens 2 asks about?

  6. RE-ANSWERED AT ITERATION 8, because the iteration-7 answer rested on a verdict EXEC-002
     cannot affect. §20.6 clause 0b establishes that the withdrawal is settled before the run, so
     the 3-hour budget is NOT justified by the verdict. It is justified by four things §24 item 18
     now lists: determining WHICH non-success label applies; testing the refutation direction,
     which at iteration 7 could not fire at all and has therefore never been tested; producing the
     §16.7 diagnostics the next preregistration needs; and answering QC-02's question, which no
     other analysis addresses. Is that sufficient, and is the §23.1 budget — now stated as a
     MEASURED figure from one CRAM pass rather than an inherited BAM figure — honest?

  7. X-01 was found by an availability check, not by this designer. The inputs changed format
     under the benchmark and §24 item 22 records that whole-file fidelity is asserted rather than
     verified. Is §15.5.7 V-f plus §21 F11's INPUT-FORMAT branch an adequate response, or should
     EXEC-002 recompute md5_sam_body on the 28 files before spending any compute at all?
```
